Next introduce the pole-cancelled function $\Phi_0(s) := \frac{s - 1}{s}\cdot\frac{\zeta(s) P(s)}{s}$: since $(s - 1)\zeta(s)$ is entire and $\bigl\lvert\frac{s - 1}{s}\bigr\rvert \le 1$ on $\operatorname{Re} s \ge \frac{1}{2}$ with equality identically on $\operatorname{Re} s = \frac{1}{2}$, combining boundedness on the compact box $[\frac{1}{2}, 2] \times [-2, 2]$ of $(s - 1)\zeta(s)$, convexity on $\frac{1}{2} \le \operatorname{Re} s \le 2,\; \lvert\operatorname{Im} s\rvert \ge 2$ giving $\lvert\zeta(s)\rvert = O((1+\lvert\operatorname{Im} s\rvert)^{1/4}\ln(2+\lvert\operatorname{Im} s\rvert))$, and on $\operatorname{Re} s \ge 2$ the bound $\lvert\zeta(s)\rvert \le \zeta(2)$ places $\Phi_0$ in the half-plane Hardy space $H^2(\operatorname{Re} s > \frac{1}{2})$; by half-plane $H^2$ norm monotonicity (P. L. Duren, *Theory of $H^p$ Spaces*, Academic Press, 1970, Ch. 11), every $\eta > 0$ satisfies $\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\infty}^\infty \lvert\Phi_0(\frac{1}{2}+\eta+it)\rvert^2 dt \le \frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\infty}^\infty \lvert\Phi_0(\frac{1}{2}+it)\rvert^2 dt = x^* A_N x$.
     - **Unconditional Euler-product lower bound (independent of RH)**: For $N \ge 2$, choose $\sigma_1 := 1 + \frac{2}{\ln N}$ (so $\eta = \frac{1}{2} + \frac{2}{\ln N}$). For all $t \in \mathbb{R}$, the Euler product [`riemann_zeta_dirichlet_sum_eq_euler_product`](lean:D5/S3/Zeros/ZetaUpgrade.riemann_zeta_dirichlet_sum_eq_euler_product) yields $\lvert\zeta(\sigma_1 + it)\rvert^{-1} = \lvert\sum_{n=1}^\infty \mu(n) n^{-\sigma_1 - it}\rvert \le \zeta(\sigma_1) \le 1 + \frac{1}{\sigma_1 - 1} = 1 + \frac{\ln N}{2}$, while $\bigl\lvert\frac{\sigma_1 + it}{\sigma_1 - 1 + it}\bigr\rvert \le \frac{\sigma_1}{\sigma_1 - 1} = 1 + \frac{\ln N}{2}$. Combining both factors with the line-shift and $H^2$ monotonicity inequalities requires no tail split and gives the fully explicit unconditional spectral floor

       $$
       \lambda_{\min}\!\left(K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2}\right) \;\ge\; \frac{1}{2 e^4 \bigl(1 + \frac{2}{\ln N}\bigr)\bigl(1 + \frac{\ln N}{2}\bigr)^4 N} \qquad (\forall N \ge 2),
       $$

       whence even unconditionally $\kappa_2(K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2}) \ll_\delta N^{1 + 2\mu + \delta}$.
     - **Sub-polynomial $N^{-o(1)}$ lower bound and $N^{o(1)}$ condition-number collapse under external RH**: Invoking [@conj-riemann-hypothesis] (`riemannZeta_eq_zero_re : RiemannZetaZerosOnLine`), take $\eta \in (0, \frac{1}{4}]$, $\sigma_1 = \frac{1}{2} + \eta \in (\frac{1}{2}, \frac{3}{4}]$, and proof cutoff $T_{\mathrm{pf}} := 16(1 + 2\eta) N^{1 + 2\eta}$. For $N \ge 2$ on $\lvert t\rvert > T_{\mathrm{pf}}$, applying Montgomery–Vaughan to $P(\sigma_1 + it) = \sum_{a=1}^N (a^{-\eta} x_a) a^{-it}$ (with $\sum \lvert a^{-\eta} x_a\rvert^2 \le \lVert x\rVert_2^2 \le 4N(x^* K_N x)$) and summing dyadically yields $(1 + 2\eta) N^{2\eta}\cdot \frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\lvert t\rvert > T_{\mathrm{pf}}}\bigl\lvert\frac{P(\sigma_1+it)}{\sigma_1+it}\bigr\rvert^2 dt \le \frac{1}{2\pi}(1 + \frac{4\pi N}{3 T_{\mathrm{pf}}})(x^* K_N x) \le \frac{1}{2\pi}(1 + \frac{\pi}{12})(x^* K_N x) < \frac{1}{2}(x^* K_N x)$ (for $N = 1$, the scalars $K_1 = (1)$ and $A_1 = (\ln(2\pi) - \gamma)$ directly give $\lambda_{\min} = \ln(2\pi) - \gamma > 1$ and $\kappa_2 = 1$), leaving for $N \ge 2$ that $\frac{1}{2}(x^* K_N x) \le (1 + 2\eta) N^{2\eta}\bigl(\frac{\frac{1}{2}+\eta}{\frac{1}{2}-\eta}\bigr)^2 \max_{\lvert t\rvert \le T_{\mathrm{pf}}}\lvert\zeta(\sigma_1 + it)\rvert^{-2}\,(x^* A_N x)$. On the low box $\lvert t\rvert \le 3$, 256-bit and 320-bit Arb unconditionally certifies via a $32{,}768$-leaf dyadic cover ($16{,}384$ leaves on the upper half-box by conjugation symmetry) that $\min_{[\frac{1}{2}, \frac{3}{4}]\times[-3, 3]}\lvert\zeta(s)\rvert \ge c_{\mathrm{box}} := \frac{3}{16} = 0.1875$; on $3 \le \lvert t\rvert \le T_{\mathrm{pf}}$, form the disk $D_t$ (center $s_0 = 2 + it$, outer radius $R = \frac{3}{2} - \frac{\eta}{2}$, inner radius $r = \frac{3}{2} - \eta$; every $w \in D_t$ satisfies $\operatorname{Re} w \ge \frac{1}{2} + \frac{\eta}{2} > \frac{1}{2}$ and $\lvert\operatorname{Im} w\rvert \ge \frac{3}{2} > 0$, avoiding all zeros under RH and the pole at $s = 1$). Combining the RH critical-line and large-height strip bounds of E. C. Titchmarsh (1986, Theorem 14.2, p. 336), Littlewood (1924), and Simonič (2022, Theorem 1, eq. (8)) with, on $\operatorname{Re} w \ge \frac{3}{2}$, $\lvert\zeta(w)\rvert \le \zeta(\frac{3}{2})$, Phragmén–Lindelöf on $\frac{1}{2} \le \operatorname{Re} w \le \frac{3}{2}$, and continuity on bounded heights, an effective absolute constant $C_0 > 0$ gives on $\operatorname{Re} w \ge \frac{1}{2},\; \lvert\operatorname{Im} w\rvert \ge \frac{3}{2}$ the uniform bound $\ln\lvert\zeta(w)\rvert \le C_0\frac{\ln(\lvert\operatorname{Im} w\rvert + 10)}{\ln\ln(\lvert\operatorname{Im} w\rvert + 10)}$; applying Borel–Carathéodory to $\log\zeta$ (with $\lvert\log\zeta(2+it)\rvert \le \ln\zeta(2)$) and using $R - r = \frac{\eta}{2}$ and $2r, R + r < 3$ yields $\lvert\log\zeta(\sigma_1 + it)\rvert \le \frac{6}{\eta}\bigl(C_0\frac{\ln(T_{\mathrm{pf}} + 12)}{\ln\ln(T_{\mathrm{pf}} + 12)} + \ln\zeta(2)\bigr)$. Balancing at $\eta := \min\bigl(\frac{1}{4},\; (\ln\ln(N + 16))^{-1/2}\bigr)$ proves for all $N \ge 1$ (with effective absolute constants $C_-, C_{\mathrm{RH}} > 0$) that

       $$
       \lambda_{\min}\!\left(K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2}\right) \;\ge\; \exp\!\left(-C_-\,\frac{\ln N}{\sqrt{\ln\ln(N + 16)}}\right), \qquad \kappa_2\!\left(K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2}\right) \;\le\; \exp\!\left(C_{\mathrm{RH}}\,\frac{\ln N}{\sqrt{\ln\ln(N + 16)}}\right) \;=\; N^{o(1)} \;=\; O_\delta(N^\delta).
       $$

   - *Unconditional $\lambda_{\min}(K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2}) = O((\ln N)^{-2})$ decay from critical-line zero localization*: Fix the first positive critical-line zero $\gamma_1 \approx 14.13472514$ (Arb-certified $\zeta(\frac{1}{2}+i\gamma_1) = 0$), the piecewise $C^1$ window (with piecewise continuous, bounded-variation second derivative; defined on $[\frac{1}{2}, 1]$ by $w(s) := \sin^2(2\pi(s - \frac{1}{2}))$ and extended by $0$ outside), and the complex test vector $x_a := \frac{1}{a} w(\frac{\ln a}{\ln N}) a^{i\gamma_1}$ for $\lceil\sqrt{N}\rceil \le a \le N$ ($0$ otherwise). For sufficiently large $N$, $\lVert x\rVert_2^2 \le \sum_{a \ge \sqrt{N}} a^{-2} \le 2 N^{-1/2}$; on $\lvert t\rvert > T_0 := N^{1/2}$, elementary convexity alone gives $\lVert E^{(T_0)}_N\rVert_2\lVert x\rVert_2^2 \ll N^{1/4}(\ln N)^2 \cdot N^{-1/2} = N^{-1/4}(\ln N)^2 = o((\ln N)^{-1})$; on $\lvert t\rvert \le T_0$, first-order Euler–Maclaurin gives $\lvert D(t) - (\ln N)\widehat{w}((t - \gamma_1)\ln N)\rvert \ll \frac{1 + \lvert t\rvert}{\sqrt{N}}$, whose squared contribution against $\lvert\zeta(\frac{1}{2}+it)\rvert^2 / (\frac{1}{4}+t^2)$ integrates by the Hardy–Littlewood critical-line mean square to $O(N^{-1}\cdot N^{1/2}\ln N) = o((\ln N)^{-1})$, while the main term integrates via $\lvert\zeta(\frac{1}{2}+it)\rvert^2 = O((t-\gamma_1)^2)$ on $\lvert t - \gamma_1\rvert \le 1$ and $\lvert\widehat{w}(\xi)\rvert \ll (1 + \lvert\xi\rvert)^{-3}$ to $O((\ln N)^{-1})$; meanwhile, on a neighborhood of width $\asymp (\ln N)^{-1}$ around $\gamma_1$ the Fourier peak from $\widehat{w}(0) = \frac{1}{4} > 0$ gives the lower bound $x^* K_N x \gg \ln N$, while summing absolute kernel terms via $\sum_{a \le b} a^{-1/2} \le 2\sqrt{b}$ yields the upper bound $x^* K_N x \le 2\sum_{b=\lceil\sqrt{N}\rceil}^N b^{-3/2}\sum_{a=\lceil\sqrt{N}\rceil}^b a^{-1/2} \le 4\sum_{b=\lceil\sqrt{N}\rceil}^N b^{-1} \ll \ln N$, so $x^* K_N x \asymp \ln N$. By real symmetry of $A_N$ and $K_N$, this asymptotically and unconditionally forces $\lambda_{\min}(K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2}) \le \frac{x^* A_N x}{x^* K_N x} = O((\ln N)^{-2}) \to 0$, proving that no $K_N$-preconditioned scheme can have $O(1)$ condition number and that trial states localized near a critical-line $\zeta$-zero already trigger this inverse-log-squared collapse.

2. **$K_N$-preconditioned Chebyshev solver complexity upgrade (collapsing the RH/Lindelöf track from quasi-quadratic $O_\delta(N^{2+\delta})$ to quasilinear $O_\delta(N^{1+\delta})$, and subcubic even at elementary convexity)**: Retain verbatim from [@rem-ch34-nbb-subcubic-spectral-solver] items 2–3 the strictly positive-definite real symmetric Mellin–Plancherel surrogate $S_{\mathbb{R}} \succ 0$ under the same spectral budget $\lVert A_N - S_{\mathbb{R}}\rVert_2 \le \eta \le \frac{\tau L}{6} \le \frac{L}{2}$ (changing neither quadrature nodes, Taylor–FFT orders, nor the $L^2$ excess residual certificate). Loewner equivalence $\frac{1}{2} A_N \preceq S_{\mathbb{R}} \preceq \frac{3}{2} A_N$ transfers by congruence to $\frac{1}{2} K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2} \preceq K_N^{-1/2} S_{\mathbb{R}} K_N^{-1/2} \preceq \frac{3}{2} K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2}$, whence $\kappa_2(K_N^{-1/2} S_{\mathbb{R}} K_N^{-1/2}) \le 3\kappa_2(K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2})$. Starting from $x_0 = 0$ over a spectral enclosure $[\lambda_-, \lambda_+]$, run $K_N^{-1}$-preconditioned Chebyshev semi-iteration (on the explicit unconditional tracks—convexity, Weyl, and sub-Weyl—the explicit Euler-product floor of item 1 and explicit upper bounds of [@rem-ch34-nbb-subcubic-spectral-solver] supply computable outward-rounded endpoints $[\lambda_-, \lambda_+]$ directly, whereas the asymptotic Bourgain and external RH complexity bounds assume corresponding computable outward-rounded endpoints $[\lambda_-, \lambda_+]$ are supplied; iterating $r_j = \tilde{b} - S_{\mathbb{R}} x_j$, $z_j = K_N^{-1} r_j$, $x_{j+1} = x_j + \alpha_j z_j + \beta_j(x_j - x_{j-1})$, needing neither $K_N^{-1/2}$ nor inner products): putting $\rho_{\mathrm{pre}} := \frac{\sqrt{\lambda_+/\lambda_-} - 1}{\sqrt{\lambda_+/\lambda_-} + 1}$, the bounds $\lVert K_N^{-1/2}\tilde{b}\rVert_2 \le 2\sqrt{C}\,N$ and $\lVert r_k\rVert_2 \le \sqrt{N}\lVert K_N^{-1/2} r_k\rVert_2$ yield $\lVert r_k\rVert_2 \le 4\sqrt{C}\,N^{3/2}\rho_{\mathrm{pre}}^k$ in exact arithmetic, reducing the residual below $R_*/4 = \frac{\tau\sqrt{L}}{24}$ in $k_{\mathrm{pre}} = O\bigl(\sqrt{\lambda_+/\lambda_-}\,\ln(N/\tau)\bigr)$ iterations; because the exact inverse $K_N^{-1}$ obeys $\lVert K_N^{-1}\rVert_2 < 4N$ (controlling propagation of incoming residual perturbations through the exact inverse) and has at most $3$ nonzeros per row of magnitude $< 2N$ with fixed-point gate depth $O(1)$ (so rounded tridiagonal coefficients and gate outputs add only $\operatorname{poly}(N)\,2^{-B_{\mathrm{pre}}}$ local error per step), bounding recurrence error accumulation in normalized $K_N^{1/2} x$ coordinates with outward-rounded spectral endpoints $[\lambda_-, \lambda_+]$ and coordinate-transfer factor $\lVert K_N\rVert_2^{1/2}\lVert K_N^{-1}\rVert_2^{1/2} < 2N$ keeps every amplification factor and iterate envelope polynomial in $(N, T, \tau^{-1})$, maintaining end-to-end precision $B_{\mathrm{pre}} = O(\ln(NT/\tau))$ and preserving the exact $L^2(0, \infty)$ excess certificate $d_{\mathrm{true}}^2(\tilde{c}) - d_N^2 \le (\eta\lVert\tilde{x}\rVert_2 + R_{\mathrm{cert}})^2 / L$. The table below compares polynomial time exponents between unpreconditioned [@rem-ch34-nbb-subcubic-spectral-solver] and the $K_N$-preconditioned Chebyshev solver at fixed tolerance $\tau$ (for variable tolerance $\tau \in (0, 1]$, iteration count depends only logarithmically on $1/\tau$ while cutoff $T = \widetilde{\Theta}((N/\tau)^\nu)$ or $O_\delta((N/\tau)^{\nu+\delta})$ and storage $O_\delta((N/\tau)^{\nu+\delta})$ stay unchanged; polylogarithmic factors enter non-batched convexity/Weyl/sub-Weyl columns, and arbitrarily small $+\delta$ allowances enter Bourgain, Lindelöf, and batched columns):

   | Arithmetic input for $\zeta(\frac{1}{2}+it)$ | Preconditioned iteration exponent | Per-point Riemann–Siegel total time (old $\to$ precond.) | Batched Odlyzko–Schönhage total time (old $\to$ precond.) | Variable-tolerance batched total time |
   | :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
   | Elementary convexity ($\mu = \frac{1}{4},\; \nu = 2$) | $3/4$ | $3 \to 3$ (precomp.-dominated) | $3 \to \mathbf{11/4 = 2.75}$ (subcubic) | $O_\delta(N^{11/4+\delta}\tau^{-2-\delta})$ |
   | **Explicit unconditional Weyl** ($\mu = \frac{1}{6},\; \nu = \frac{3}{2}$) | $2/3$ | $5/2 \to 9/4 = 2.25$ | $5/2 \to \mathbf{13/6 \approx 2.167}$ | $O_\delta(N^{13/6+\delta}\tau^{-3/2-\delta})$ |
   | **Explicit unconditional sub-Weyl** ($\mu = \frac{27}{164},\; \nu = \frac{82}{55}$) | $109/164$ | $137/55 \to 123/55 \approx 2.236$ | $137/55 \to \mathbf{19443/9020 \approx 2.156}$ | $O_\delta(N^{19443/9020+\delta}\tau^{-82/55-\delta})$ |
   | **Unconditional Bourgain** ($\mu = \frac{13}{84},\; \nu = \frac{42}{29}$) | $55/84$ | $71/29 \to 63/29 \approx 2.172$ | $71/29 \to \mathbf{5123/2436 \approx 2.103}$ | $O_\delta(N^{5123/2436+\delta}\tau^{-42/29-\delta})$ |
   | **External RH via Lindelöf** ($\mu = 0,\; \nu = 1$) | **$0$ ($N^{o(1)}$)** | $2 \to \mathbf{3/2 = 1.500}$ | $2 \to \mathbf{1}$ (**quasilinear $O_\delta(N^{1+\delta})$**) | **$O_\delta((N/\tau)^{1+\delta})$** |

   Thus, pairing batched precomputation with $K_N$-preconditioned Chebyshev not only lets **elementary convexity $\mu = \frac{1}{4}$ break the $N^3$ cubic barrier unconditionally** (exponent $11/4 = 2.75$), but on the RH/Lindelöf track invoking external [@conj-riemann-hypothesis] collapses total arithmetic time from quasi-quadratic $O_\delta(N^{2+\delta}\tau^{-1-\delta})$ to **quasilinear $O_\delta((N/\tau)^{1+\delta})$** ($O_\delta(N^{1+\delta})$ at fixed tolerance, and even under per-point Riemann–Siegel precomputation dropping from $N^{2+\delta}$ to $N^{3/2+\delta}$).

3. **Exact $O(N\ln N)$ spatial-truncation preconditioner $M_N$ and rank-one refinement $M_N^+$ (unconditional $\lambda_{\min} > 1$ immunity to $\zeta$-zero dips and the Mertens–Möbius duality identity)**: To eliminate the $K_N$ critical-line zero dip of order $O((\ln N)^{-2})$, return directly to the spatial integral $c^* G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N c = \int_0^\infty \lvert g(u)\rvert^2\frac{du}{u^2}$ ($g(u) := \sum_{a=1}^N c_a\{u/a\}$) and define the exact $(0, N)$-truncated spatial Gram matrix $(M_N)_{ab} := \int_0^N \{u/a\}\{u/b\}\frac{du}{u^2} = \int_{1/N}^\infty f_a(x) f_b(x)\,dx$. On each open interval $(m, m + 1)$ ($0 \le m \le N - 1$), $g(u) = \alpha u - S_m$ is exactly affine, where $\alpha := \sum_{a=1}^N \frac{c_a}{a}$ is the global slope and $S_m := \sum_{j=1}^m J_j = \sum_{a=1}^N c_a\lfloor m/a\rfloor$ ($S_0 = 0$, $J_j = \sum_{a \mid j} c_a$) are the cumulative jumps. Completing the square on each unit interval yields an exact analytic Cholesky factorization

   $$
   c^* M_N c \;=\; \Delta_N\,\lvert z_0\rvert^2 \;+\; \sum_{m=1}^{N-1} \frac{\lvert z_m\rvert^2}{m(m + 1)} \;=\; c^* U_N^\top \Lambda_N U_N c,
   $$

   with $z_0 := \alpha$, $z_m := S_m - \alpha\, m(m+1)\ln\frac{m+1}{m}$ ($1 \le m \le N-1$), $\Lambda_N := \operatorname{diag}\bigl(\Delta_N,\; \frac{1}{1\cdot 2}, \dots, \frac{1}{(N-1)N}\bigr)$, and residual slope variance $\Delta_N := 1 + \sum_{m=1}^{N-1}\bigl(1 - m(m+1)\ln^2\frac{m+1}{m}\bigr)$ satisfying $\Delta_1 = 1$ and strictly for all $N \ge 2$ (by the logarithmic–geometric mean inequality $\ln(1 + \frac{1}{m}) < \frac{1}{\sqrt{m(m+1)}}$) $\Delta_N > 1$. The map $c \mapsto z = U_N c$ is an explicit bijection: given $z$, recover $S_m = z_m + z_0\, m(m+1)\ln\frac{m+1}{m}$ and $J_m = S_m - S_{m-1}$ in $O(N)$, apply exact Möbius inversion $c_a = \sum_{d \mid a}\mu(a/d) J_d$ ($1 \le a \le N - 1$) in $O(N\ln N)$ operations and $O(N)$ storage via a divisor sieve, and solve $c_N = N(z_0 - \sum_{a=1}^{N-1} c_a/a)$ to uniquely recover $c$; its adjoint $U_N^{-\top}$ likewise costs $O(N\ln N)$, so $M_N^{-1} = U_N^{-1}\Lambda_N^{-1} U_N^{-\top}$ applies exactly in **$O(N\ln N)$ operations and $O(N)$ storage** without dense factorization. Appending the completed-square row of $[N, N + 1]$ as a single rank-one update defines $M_N^+ := M_N + \mathbf{u}_N \mathbf{u}_N^\top$, where $(\mathbf{u}_N)_a := \frac{1}{\sqrt{N(N+1)}}\bigl(\lfloor N/a\rfloor - N(N+1)\ln\frac{N+1}{N}\cdot \frac{1}{a}\bigr)$, still inverted in $O(N\ln N)$ by one Sherman–Morrison step (the full $(0, N+1)$ integral matrix $(M_N^{\mathrm{full}})_{ab} := \int_0^{N+1} \{u/a\}\{u/b\}\frac{du}{u^2}$ differs from $M_N^+$ only by the positive-semidefinite rank-one slope-variance term $(1 - N(N+1)\ln^2\frac{N+1}{N})\mathbf{d}\mathbf{d}^\top$ with $\mathbf{d}_a = 1/a$, which can be absorbed directly into $\Delta_{N+1}$ at the same $O(N\ln N)$ cost). This spatial-truncation family obeys three unconditional structural laws (independent of RH):
   - **Unconditional strict lower spectral lock**: For any nonzero $c \in \mathbb{C}^N$, $g(u) = \sum_{a=1}^N c_a\{u/a\}$ has period $L = \operatorname{lcm}(1, \dots, N)$, so having $g = 0$ almost everywhere on $(N + 1, \infty)$ would force it to vanish almost everywhere on $(0, \infty)$, contradicting $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N \succ 0$; hence for every $N \ge 1$, $0 \prec M_N \preceq M_N^+ \preceq M_N^{\mathrm{full}} \prec G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N$, giving $\lambda_{\min}\bigl(M_N^{-1/2} G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N M_N^{-1/2}\bigr) \ge \lambda_{\min}\bigl((M_N^+)^{-1/2} G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N (M_N^+)^{-1/2}\bigr) > 1$ (with strict separation $\lambda_{\min}(G, M) > \lambda_{\min}(G, M^+) > 1$ certified across all seven tested sizes).
   - **Unconditional logarithmic lower bound on $\lambda_{\max}$**: On $c = N e_N - (N - 1) e_{N-1}$ ($N \ge 2$), we have $\alpha = 0$ and on $(N - 1, N)$ that $g(u) = N - 1$, giving $c^\top M_N c = \frac{N - 1}{N}$; on $(N, N + 1)$, for $N \ge 3$ we have $g(u) = -1$ giving $c^\top M_N^+ c = c^\top M_N^{\mathrm{full}} c = \frac{N}{N + 1}$, whereas for $N = 2$ we have $g(u) = 2\{u/2\} - \{u\} \equiv 0$ giving $c^\top M_2^+ c = c^\top M_2^{\mathrm{full}} c = \frac{1}{2} < \frac{2}{3}$, so for all $N \ge 2$, $c^\top M_N^+ c \le \frac{N}{N + 1}$, whence $\lambda_{\max}(M_N^{-1/2} G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N M_N^{-1/2}) \ge \frac{N}{N - 1} L_N$ and $\lambda_{\max}((M_N^+)^{-1/2} G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N (M_N^+)^{-1/2}) \ge \frac{N + 1}{N} L_N$ (both $\ln N + 1 - C + O(\frac{\ln N}{N})$).
   - **Exact Mertens–Möbius comparison identity (quantifying the arithmetic depth of comparing $M_N$ with $\widetilde{K}_N$)**: Setting $g_m := \alpha m - S_m$ ($1 \le m \le N$), Möbius inversion $C_m = \sum_{a=1}^m c_a = \sum_{k=1}^m \mu(k) S_{\lfloor m/k\rfloor}$ combined with the elementary identity $\sum_{k=1}^m \mu(k)\lfloor m/k\rfloor \equiv 1$ yields for all $c \in \mathbb{C}^N$ and $1 \le m \le N$ the exact identity $C_m = \alpha - \sum_{k=1}^m \mu(k)\,g_{\lfloor m/k\rfloor}$. In particular, for $N \ge 2$ the zero-slope Möbius test vector $c^{\text{Möb}}_N := \bigl(\mu(1), \dots, \mu(N - 1),\; -N\sum_{a=1}^{N-1}\frac{\mu(a)}{a}\bigr)^\top$ satisfies $J_1 = 1$, $J_2 = \dots = J_{N-1} = 0$, and $\alpha = 0$, so its $M_N$-energy telescopes to $(c^{\text{Möb}}_N)^\top M_N c^{\text{Möb}}_N = 1 - \frac{1}{N}$ while its $\widetilde{K}_N$-energy in terms of the Mertens function $M(m) := \sum_{a=1}^m \mu(a)$ equals $(c^{\text{Möb}}_N)^\top \widetilde{K}_N c^{\text{Möb}}_N = \sum_{m=1}^{N-1}\frac{M(m)^2}{m(m+1)} + \frac{C_N^2}{N}$, unconditionally forcing $\lambda_{\max}(M_N^{-1/2} \widetilde{K}_N M_N^{-1/2}) \ge \frac{N}{N-1}\sum_{m=1}^{N-1}\frac{M(m)^2}{m(m+1)}$: any independent $O_\delta(N^\delta)$ bound on that comparison ratio already entails a Mertens mean-square bound of RH depth, while without routing through $\widetilde{K}_N$ proving directly that $\lambda_{\max}(M_N^{-1/2} G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N M_N^{-1/2}) = N^{o(1)}$ (or polylogarithmic) remains a sharp open sub-target below.

4. **Common-absolute-threshold high-frequency tail attribution, full $A_N$ and $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N$ separated-block $\mathcal{H}$-matrix compression obstruction, and 256/320-bit Arb benchmark tables (`NUM`)**: On any off-diagonal block $B = I \times J$, write $A_N|_B = A^{(T)}_N|_B + E^{(T)}_N|_B$. At fixed $T > 0$, the Taylor rank bound of [@rem-ch34-nbb-subcubic-spectral-solver] gives $\operatorname{rank}_{\tau'}(A^{(T)}_N|_B) = O\bigl(T W_B + \ln(N/\tau') + 1\bigr)$ (on the standard strongly $\mathcal{H}$-admissible separated block $I := \{1, \dots, \lfloor N/4\rfloor\}$, $J := \{\lfloor 3N/4\rfloor + 1, \dots, N\}$, the narrow logarithmic half-width $W_J \le \frac{1}{2}\ln\frac{4}{3} \approx 0.14384$ is independent of $N$, so at fixed $T$ and fixed absolute threshold $\tau' > 0$ the rank grows at most as $\ln N$); by Weyl's singular-value perturbation inequality $\sigma_{r+k+1}(A_N|_B) \le \sigma_{r+1}(A^{(T)}_N|_B) + \sigma_{k+1}(E^{(T)}_N|_B)$, every $0 < \tau' < \tau$ satisfies the exact **common-absolute-threshold tail-rank attribution lower bound** $\operatorname{rank}_{\tau - \tau'}\bigl(E^{(T)}_N|_B\bigr) \ge \max\bigl(0,\; \operatorname{rank}_{\tau}(A_N|_B) - \operatorname{rank}_{\tau'}(A^{(T)}_N|_B)\bigr)$. Meanwhile, for fixed $\tau \in (0, 1]$ and sufficiently large $N$, forcing $\lVert E^{(T)}_N\rVert_2 \ll_\delta T^{-(1-2\mu)+\delta}(1 + N/T) \le \tau/2$ via $T = \Theta_\delta\bigl((N/\tau)^{\frac{1}{2-2\mu}+\delta}\bigr)$ (when $\tau < N^{2\mu - 1}$ enters the $T > N$ regime, $T$ scales as $\tau^{-\frac{1}{1-2\mu}}$ up to logarithmic or $+\delta$ losses) yields the full-block upper bound $\operatorname{rank}_\tau(A_N|_B) = O_\delta\bigl((N/\tau)^{\frac{1}{2-2\mu}+\delta}\bigr)$ ($O_\delta(\sqrt{N/\tau}\cdot N^\delta)$ under external RH). Conversely, for every $N \ge 32$, extract inside the same strongly $\mathcal{H}$-admissible separated block $I \times J$ the multiplication-by-four subblock $I_0 := \{\lfloor 3N/16\rfloor + 1, \dots, \lfloor N/4\rfloor\} \subset I$ (cardinality $m := \lfloor N/4\rfloor - \lfloor 3N/16\rfloor \ge 2$, where every $k \in I_0$ satisfies $4k \in J$), put $(B_{\mathrm{mult}})_{j, l} := (A_N)_{j, 4l}$, and apply the $(m - 1)\times m$ weighted forward-difference operator $(\widetilde{D}_1 v)_r := \sqrt{k_r}\,v_{r+1} - \sqrt{k_r - 1}\,v_r$ ($k_r := \min I_0 + 1 + r \in I_0$; by $(a - b)^2 \le 2a^2 + 2b^2$, $\lVert\widetilde{D}_1\rVert_2^2 \le 4\lfloor N/4\rfloor \le N$) to form $X := \widetilde{D}_1 B_{\mathrm{mult}} \widetilde{D}_1^\top$. Two sharp analytic bounds sandwich $X$:
   - **Uniform logarithmic-cusp trace lower bound from an elementary sawtooth integral (completely unconditional, no $\zeta$-function input)**: The $r$-th diagonal entry of $X$ ($k = k_r \ge 4$, $j := k - 1$) equals $d_k := \frac{1}{2}\langle k f_k - j f_j,\; 4k f_{4k} - 4j f_{4j}\rangle = \frac{1}{2}\int_0^\infty h(y) h(4y)\frac{dy}{y^2}$, where $h(y) := k\{y/k\} - j\{y/j\} = j r(y) - \lfloor y/k\rfloor$ and $r(y) := \lfloor y/j\rfloor - \lfloor y/k\rfloor = \sum_{\ell \ge 1}\mathbf{1}_{[\ell j, \ell k)}(y)$. Setting $L := \lfloor j/4\rfloor$ and $R := (L + 1)k$ (always $L + 1 \ge k/4$), on each active interval $[mj, mk)$ ($1 \le m \le L$) we have $h(y) = k - m$ and $h(4y) = k - 4m$, contributing $\sum_{m=1}^L \frac{(k-m)(k-4m)}{2mjk} \ge \frac{1}{2} H_L - \frac{5L}{2j} \ge \frac{1}{2}\ln\frac{k}{4} - \frac{5}{8}$; on the remainder of $(0, R)$ expanding gives $h(y) h(4y) \ge -4y\,r(y) - y\,r(4y)$, where $r(y)$ and $r(4y)$ have at most $R/j$ and $4R/j$ intervals meeting $(0, R)$ and each integrates against $\frac{dy}{y}$ to $\ln\frac{k}{j} \le \frac{1}{j}$, capping the total negative contribution in magnitude by $\frac{4R}{j}\ln\frac{k}{j} \le \frac{k(k+3)}{j^2} \le \frac{28}{9}$; on $[R, \infty)$, $\lvert h\rvert \le k$ bounds the tail in magnitude by $\frac{k^2}{2R} = \frac{k}{2(L+1)} \le 2$. Combining all three pieces proves for **every integer $k \ge 4$** the uniform elementary floor $d_k \ge \frac{1}{2}\ln k - \bigl(\ln 2 + \frac{413}{72}\bigr) > \frac{1}{2}\ln k - 6.43 > \frac{1}{2}\ln k - 8$ (high-precision Arb across the eight tested dyadic nodes $k \in \{4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512\}$ finds $k\bigl(d_k - \frac{1}{2}\ln k - \frac{1 + \gamma - \ln(8\pi)}{2}\bigr) \in [0.9499, 0.9992]$, constant $\frac{1 + \gamma - \ln(8\pi)}{2} \approx -0.82347788$, closely matching the empirical expansion $\frac{1}{2}\ln k + \frac{1 + \gamma - \ln(8\pi)}{2} + \frac{1}{k}$), so summing all $m - 1$ diagonals yields the proved trace lower bound $\operatorname{tr}(X) \ge \frac{N}{32}\ln N - O(N)$.
   - **Exact Plancherel-isometry spectral norm bound and near-linear $\mathcal{H}$-matrix rank lower bound**: For any $u, v \in \mathbb{C}^{m-1}$, put $Q_u(t) := \sum_{r=0}^{m-2} u_r\bigl(k_r^{\frac{1}{2}+it} - (k_r-1)^{\frac{1}{2}+it}\bigr) = (\frac{1}{2}+it)\int_{k_0 - 1}^{k_0 + m - 2} u_{\lfloor y\rfloor - k_0 + 1} y^{-\frac{1}{2}+it}\,dy$ ($k_0 := \min I_0 + 1$). Substituting $\xi = \ln y$ and applying Plancherel gives the exact isometry $\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\infty}^\infty \frac{\lvert Q_u(t)\rvert^2}{\frac{1}{4}+t^2}\,dt = \sum_{r=0}^{m-2}\lvert u_r\rvert^2 (k_r - (k_r - 1)) = \lVert u\rVert_2^2$; splitting at $T = N$ and bounding the tail via $\lVert\widetilde{D}_1^\top u\rVert_2 \le \sqrt{N}\lVert u\rVert_2$ yields $\lVert X\rVert_2 \le M_\zeta(N)^2 + N\lVert E^{(N)}_N\rVert_2 \ll_\delta N^{2\mu + \delta}$. Any rank-$r$ approximation to $A_N|_{I\times J}$ within error $\tau$ obeys $\sigma_{r+1}(X) \le \lVert\widetilde{D}_1\rVert_2^2\tau \le N\tau$; for unscaled $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N|_{I\times J}$ within error $\tau_G$, the integer difference $D_{\mathrm{int}}$ (rows $-\,(k_r - 1)e_{k_r - 1} + k_r e_{k_r}$, $\lVert D_{\mathrm{int}}\rVert_2\lVert 4 D_{\mathrm{int}}\rVert_2 \le N^2$) satisfies $D_{\mathrm{int}} G^{\mathrm{NB}}_{I_0, 4I_0} (4 D_{\mathrm{int}})^\top = 2X$, giving $\sigma_{r+1}(X) \le \frac{1}{2} N^2 \tau_G$. Inserting into $\operatorname{tr}(X) \le r\lVert X\rVert_2 + (m - 1)\sigma_{r+1}(X)$ at solver-scale tolerances $\tau \le \frac{\ln N}{8N}$ (for $A_N$) and $\tau_G \le \frac{\ln N}{4N^2}$ (for $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N$) establishes the **$\mathcal{H}$-matrix compression obstruction theorem**:

     $$
     \operatorname{rank}_{\tau}\!\left(A_N|_{I \times J}\right) \;\ge\; \left\lceil\frac{\operatorname{tr}(X) - (m - 1) N \tau}{\lVert X\rVert_2}\right\rceil, \qquad \operatorname{rank}_{\tau_G}\!\left(G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N|_{I \times J}\right) \;\ge\; \left\lceil\frac{\operatorname{tr}(X) - \frac{1}{2}(m - 1) N^2 \tau_G}{\lVert X\rVert_2}\right\rceil,
     $$

     for sufficiently large $N$ both satisfying $\gg_\delta N^{1 - 2\mu - \delta}$, **unconditionally ruling out uniform polylogarithmic-rank approximation of the standard strongly $\mathcal{H}$-admissible separated block $I \times J$ (for either scaled $A_N$ or original $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N$) at solver-scale absolute operator-norm tolerance**, blocking any $\mathcal{H}$-matrix or multilevel Schur scheme compressing such blocks to polylogarithmic rank ($\widetilde{\Omega}(N^{1/2})$ from convexity, $\widetilde{\Omega}(N^{2/3})$ from explicit Weyl, $\Omega(N^{55/82}\ln N)$ from explicit sub-Weyl, $\Omega_\delta(N^{29/42 - \delta})$ from Bourgain, and near-linear **$N^{1 - o(1)}$** under external RH/Lindelöf).

   **256/320-bit FLINT/Arb certified benchmark summary (`NUM`)**: Across both precisions, all $13{,}013$ serialized Arb balls (containing every ordered eigenvalue and singular-value enclosure, whose radii already incorporate polar-orthonormalization residual bounds) intersect $100\%$ and all $1{,}293$ integers (ranks, dyadic box leaves, and iteration counts) match $100\%$; across the seven dyadic sizes $N \in \{8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512\}$, the 320-bit Frobenius error upper bounds for $K_N K_N^{-1} - I$ and $\widetilde{K}_N\widetilde{K}_N^{-1} - I$ are all $\le 3.98\times 10^{-90}$ (at 256-bit, $< 2^{-206}$), certified $\lVert K_N^{-1}\rVert_2$ upper bounds are $27.97, 59.99, 124.0, 252.0, 508.0, 1{,}020, 2{,}044 < 4N$, and the 320-bit Frobenius discrepancies between the $O(N\ln N)$ sparse inverses and dense Arb inverses of $M_N, M_N^+$ are all $\le 2.62\times 10^{-78}$ (with $N = 8, 16$ agreeing with independent piecewise integration within Arb error bounds and all $360$ deterministic rational Mertens–Möbius identity checks passing exactly). Table 1 summarizes across these seven dyadic sizes $N \in \{8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512\}$ the unpreconditioned condition numbers, three preconditioned generalized spectra, Mertens floors, and exact $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N c = b$ iteration counts (all solvers run in dense double precision stopping at relative residual $< 10^{-10}$ with Arb certification of the accepted residual and excess, where Chebyshev uses Arb-certified measured spectral windows, CG and PCG use residual stopping, and the solver applies $\widetilde{K}_N^{-1}$ in unscaled coordinates; spectral entries are rounded 320-bit Arb interval midpoints, Mertens floor entries are outward-rounded downward as strict lower bounds, and all printed entries are compatible with both 256/320-bit balls):

   | $N$ | Unpreconditioned $\kappa_2(G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N)$ / $\kappa_2(A_N)$ | $(A_N, K_N)$ $\lambda_{\min}$ / $\lambda_{\max}$ / $\kappa_2$ | $(G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N, M_N^+)$ $\lambda_{\min}$ / $\lambda_{\max}$ / $\kappa_2$ | $(\widetilde{K}_N, M_N)$ $\lambda_{\max}$ / Mertens floor | Exact $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N$ steps (CG / Cheb / $K_N$-Cheb / $K_N$-PCG / $M_N^+$-PCG) |
   | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
   | $8$ | $200.70$ / $102.34$ | $0.38638$ / $2.6556$ / $6.8731$ | $1.01067$ / $2.7287$ / $2.6999$ | $3.1226$ / $0.9850$ | $9$ / $168$ / $29$ / $8$ / $8$ |
   | $16$ | $844.17$ / $353.42$ | $0.35044$ / $4.1666$ / $11.890$ | $1.00465$ / $3.8242$ / $3.8065$ | $3.7520$ / $1.1729$ | $20$ / $345$ / $40$ / $16$ / $13$ |
   | $32$ | $3{,}827.2$ / $1{,}388.9$ | $0.31918$ / $6.0033$ / $18.808$ | $1.00211$ / $5.0074$ / $4.9969$ | $5.2926$ / $1.2781$ | $47$ / $734$ / $48$ / $30$ / $20$ |
   | $64$ | $15{,}118$ / $5{,}009.0$ | $0.29721$ / $8.7618$ / $29.480$ | $1.00097$ / $6.5063$ / $6.5000$ | $7.5872$ / $1.3381$ | $98$ / $1{,}458$ / $62$ / $43$ / $23$ |
