   Together, the common-threshold attribution and obstruction lower bounds close the spatial-side structural picture: at $N = 256$ and $\tau_{\mathrm{abs}} = 10^{-5}$, the full separated block already reaches rank $63/64$ (adjacent $127/128$) whereas fixed cutoffs $T = 10, 100$ reach only $5, 16$ (adjacent $8, 30$; with $T = 100$ rising gently across $N = 64 \to 128 \to 256$ as $13 \to 15 \to 16$ and $26 \to 29 \to 30$), and even at $T = 400$ the cutoff at $10^{-5}$ reaches rank $42$ and at the half-threshold $5\times 10^{-6}$ reaches $43$, with Weyl immediately certifying for the high-frequency tail $E^{(400)}_{256}$ at least $63 - 43 = 20$ singular values above $5\times 10^{-6}$ (actual $61$); meanwhile, the separately sampled eight dyadic diagonal cusp nodes $k \in \{4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512\}$ all satisfy $d_k - \frac{1}{2}\ln k \in [-0.82153, -0.58598]$ (far above the uniform theoretical floor $-6.43 > -8$), while Table 4's analytic ceiling rank lower bounds computed directly from each $X$'s Arb-certified trace $\operatorname{tr}(X)$ and spectral norm $\lVert X\rVert_2$ at solver tolerances $\frac{\ln N}{8N}$ and $\frac{\ln N}{4N^2}$ are strictly positive and increasing across the four tested sizes $N \in \{64, 128, 256, 512\}$.
:::

With raw spatial inner products intrinsically dense, standard separated off-diagonal blocks blocked at solver tolerance by a polynomial—and under RH near-linear—rank wall, and unpreconditioned condition numbers growing as $N^{2+o(1)}$, a complementary alternative moves to the Fourier–Weil spectral side: imagine placing on the finite real-axis window $[-R, R]$ a smooth even wave packet $g$ whose transform must read out at conjugate mirror nodes $t \pm i\eta$ opposite signs $+1$ and $-1$; as the nodes approach the critical-line axis ($\eta \to 0$) or the imaginary symmetry axis ($t \to 0$), evenness and finite support sharply compress the kernel's differential leverage, forcing the packet to pay a quantifiable amplitude and curvature cost.

::: {.remark #rem-ch34-signed-orbit-support-budget title="Necessary Support–Amplitude Budget and Exact L2 Minimum Norm for Resolving Signed Orbits with Compactly Supported Even Functions (Unformalized Application)"}
**Provenance and verification status.** This remark gives an elementary necessary criterion and an exact closed-form $L^2$ minimum norm for resolving a single signed spectral orbit with a compactly supported even test function; both follow independently from the elementary derivations below. This application is not invoked here via green `lean=` anchors: construction names below such as `UnitSupportBurnolPacket` and `OrbitBurnolPacket` refer to `trureturing` constructions for finite spectral-orbit packets with unit-support existence and synthesis support bounds given by the frozen declarations [`exists_unit_support_orbitBurnolPacket`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/UnitSupportBurnolPacket.exists_unit_support_orbitBurnolPacket) and [`unit_support_burnol_radius`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/UnitSupportBurnolPacket.unit_support_burnol_radius), while the necessary budget bounds and exact $L^2$ minimum are established independently by the written derivation in this remark. Classical inequalities for general functions of exponential type appear in R. P. Boas, Jr. (*Inequalities for functions of exponential type*, *Math. Scand.* **4**, 29–32, 1956, [10.7146/math.scand.a-10453](https://doi.org/10.7146/math.scand.a-10453)); the conjugate-point difference bound, the low-height reflection factor $m = \min(1, R|t|)$ forced by evenness, the second-order jet bound, the exact $L^2$ Schur-complement minimum norm, and the connection to signed packet readouts follow directly from the elementary derivation below.

**Dimensionless normalization, transform convention, and necessary bounds.** Under the Riemann $\zeta$ spectral parametrization $\rho = \beta + it$, both the off-line displacement $\eta = \frac{1}{2} - \beta$ and the height $t$ are dimensionless pure numbers, distinct from a dimensional physical imaginary momentum $i\hbar\kappa$ in quantum mechanics. Throughout, we fix dimensionless spectral coordinates $t, \eta \in \mathbb{R}$ satisfying $t\eta \neq 0$ together with a dimensionless dual coordinate $x \in \mathbb{R}$, so the support half-width cap $R > 0$ is dimensionless as well. Let $g : \mathbb{R} \to \mathbb{C}$ be a smooth complex-valued even function satisfying $g(-x) = g(x)$ and $\operatorname{supp} g \subseteq [-R, R]$, and adopt the angular-frequency Fourier–Laplace transform over $\mathbb{R}$ without $2\pi$ in the exponent,

$$
F(z) \;:=\; \int_{-\infty}^{\infty} e^{-izx} g(x)\,dx, \qquad z_+ \;:=\; t + i\eta, \quad z_- \;:=\; t - i\eta.
$$

Define the zero- and second-order $L^1$ seminorms and the separation factor by

$$
\begin{aligned}
L_0 &\;:=\; \int_{-\infty}^{\infty} |g(x)|\,dx, \qquad & L_2 &\;:=\; \int_{-\infty}^{\infty} |g''(x)|\,dx, \\
m &\;:=\; \min(1, R|t|), \qquad & S &\;:=\; m\sinh(R|\eta|) \;>\; 0,
\end{aligned}
$$

where $t\eta \neq 0$ and $R > 0$ imply $m > 0$ and $\sinh(R|\eta|) > 0$, hence $S > 0$. If a unit signed readout holds within tolerance $0 \le \varepsilon < 1$, meaning $|F(z_+) - 1| \le \varepsilon$ and $|F(z_-) + 1| \le \varepsilon$, then

$$
L_0 S \;\ge\; 1 - \varepsilon, \qquad L_2 S \;\ge\; \max\!\bigl(0,\; |t^2 - \eta^2| - (t^2 + \eta^2)\varepsilon\bigr).
$$

At exact unit readout $\varepsilon = 0$, one has $L_0 \ge 1/S$ and $L_2 \ge |t^2 - \eta^2|/S$. Under the fixed dimensionless normalization of $(t, \eta)$ and $x$ above, $L_0$ and $L_2$ share the same dimensionless scale rather than mixing incompatible dimensions, and summing them gives the second-order jet lower bound

$$
L_0 + L_2 \;\ge\; \frac{1 + |t^2 - \eta^2|}{S}.
$$

Because $1/S$ and $(1 - \varepsilon)/S$ come from bounding the kernel pointwise in modulus, they provide only a necessary lower bound on $L_0$, not the exact minimum norm of an interpolation problem; when one instead minimizes the $L^2([-R, R])$ energy subject to both exact moment constraints simultaneously, a $2\times 2$ Gram Schur complement below yields the exact unique minimum norm in closed form.

**Even cosine difference and integration-by-parts derivation.** Compact support $\operatorname{supp} g \subseteq [-R, R]$ ensures absolute convergence of $F(z)$. Substituting $x \mapsto -x$ and using $g(-x) = g(x)$ gives $F(z) = \int_{-R}^{R} \cos(zx)g(x)\,dx$. Expanding $\cos\bigl((t \pm i\eta)x\bigr) = \cos(tx)\cosh(\eta x) \mp i\sin(tx)\sinh(\eta x)$ yields the exact conjugate cosine difference

$$
\cos\bigl((t + i\eta)x\bigr) - \cos\bigl((t - i\eta)x\bigr) \;=\; -2i\sin(tx)\sinh(\eta x).
$$

For $|x| \le R$, $|\sin(tx)| \le \min(1, |tx|) \le \min(1, R|t|) = m$ and $|\sinh(\eta x)| \le \sinh(R|\eta|)$, so the integral triangle inequality gives $|F(z_+) - F(z_-)| \le 2 S L_0$. Conversely, writing $F(z_\pm) = \pm 1 + e_\pm$ with $|e_\pm| \le \varepsilon$, the reverse triangle inequality gives

$$
|F(z_+) - F(z_-)| \;=\; |2 + e_+ - e_-| \;\ge\; 2 - |e_+| - |e_-| \;\ge\; 2(1 - \varepsilon).
$$

Dividing by $2$ proves $L_0 S \ge 1 - \varepsilon$, valid for every integrable even function supported in $[-R, R]$. Next, since $g$ is smooth, even, and supported in $[-R, R]$, its second derivative $g''$ shares all three properties. Integrating $F_{g''}(z) = \int_{-\infty}^{\infty} e^{-izx}g''(x)\,dx$ by parts twice eliminates both boundary stages because $g$ and $g'$ vanish at $\pm R$, leaving $F_{g''}(z) = -z^2 F(z)$. Substituting $F(z_\pm) = \pm 1 + e_\pm$ together with $z_+^2 + z_-^2 = 2(t^2 - \eta^2)$ and $|z_\pm|^2 = t^2 + \eta^2$ yields

$$
\begin{aligned}
|F_{g''}(z_+) - F_{g''}(z_-)| &\;=\; \bigl|-z_+^2 - z_-^2 - z_+^2 e_+ + z_-^2 e_-\bigr| \\
&\;\ge\; 2|t^2 - \eta^2| - 2(t^2 + \eta^2)\varepsilon.
\end{aligned}
$$

Applying the cosine-difference bound to the even function $g''$ gives $|F_{g''}(z_+) - F_{g''}(z_-)| \le 2 S L_2$; combining this with $L_2 \ge 0$ proves the finite-error bound on $L_2 S$, and setting $\varepsilon = 0$ and adding $L_0 \ge 1/S$ recovers the exact-readout bound on $L_0 + L_2$. Since $|z_+^2 - z_-^2| = |z_+ - z_-|\cdot |z_+ + z_-| = 4|t\eta|$ and $S \sim R^2|t\eta|$ when $R|t|, R|\eta|$ are small, both direct collision ($\eta \to 0$) and reflection collision ($t \to 0$) are intrinsic to the evaluation functionals and persist under any change of basis coefficients. In the degenerate cases $\eta = 0$, where $z_+ = z_-$, or $t = 0$, where $z_- = -z_+$ and evenness forces $F(-z) = F(z)$, any nonzero opposite-sign target with relative error below $1$ forces a single complex value into two disjoint error disks.

**Exact $L^2([-R, R])$ minimum norm in closed form ($2\times 2$ Gram Schur complement, manual derivation).** For exact readout $F(z_+) = 1,\; F(z_-) = -1$, put $P(x) := \cos(tx)\cosh(\eta x)$ and $Q(x) := \sin(tx)\sinh(\eta x)$ (real-valued even functions); the two readout constraints are equivalent to $\int_{-R}^R P(x)g(x)\,dx = 0$ and $\int_{-R}^R Q(x)g(x)\,dx = i$. Setting $z := \eta + it$ and $W := \frac{\sinh(2Rz)}{4z}$, integrating $P^2 + Q^2 = \frac{1}{2}\bigl(\cosh(2\eta x) + \cos(2tx)\bigr)$ and $(P + iQ)^2 = \frac{1}{2}\bigl(1 + \cosh(2zx)\bigr)$ over $[-R, R]$ yields the $2\times 2$ Gram entries:

$$
A_P := \int_{-R}^R P^2 = \frac{R}{2} + \frac{\sinh(2R\eta)}{4\eta} + \frac{\sin(2Rt)}{4t} + \operatorname{Re} W, \qquad
A_Q := \int_{-R}^R Q^2 = -\frac{R}{2} + \frac{\sinh(2R\eta)}{4\eta} + \frac{\sin(2Rt)}{4t} - \operatorname{Re} W, \qquad
J := \int_{-R}^R PQ = \operatorname{Im} W.
$$

For $R > 0$ and $t\eta \neq 0$, $P(0) = 1$, $Q(0) = 0$, and $Q$ is not identically zero on $[-R, R]$, so $P, Q$ are linearly independent and the Schur complement $A_Q - J^2/A_P > 0$ is strictly positive. Taking the orthogonal projection $Q - (J/A_P)P$ of $Q$ onto $P^\perp$ gives the **unique** least-$L^2([-R, R])$-norm even complex solution and its exact norm:

$$
g_*(x) \;=\; i\,\frac{Q(x) - (J/A_P)\,P(x)}{A_Q - J^2/A_P}, \qquad \min_{\substack{g \in L^2_{\mathrm{even}}([-R,R]) \\ F(z_+) = 1,\; F(z_-) = -1}} \lVert g\rVert_{L^2} \;=\; \frac{1}{\sqrt{A_Q - J^2/A_P}}.
$$

Extending $g_*$ by zero outside $[-R, R]$ gives $\operatorname{supp} g_* \subseteq [-R, R]$; over smooth compactly supported even functions $C_c^\infty((-R, R))_{\mathrm{even}}$, $g_*$ is analytic on $[-R, R]$ and cannot vanish smoothly at both endpoints, yet density together with a two-dimensional moment adjustment proves that the same value $\frac{1}{\sqrt{A_Q - J^2/A_P}}$ is the **exact infimum**. Comparing with the Cauchy–Schwarz lower bound $\lVert g\rVert_{L^2} \ge \frac{1}{\sqrt{2R}\,S}$ implied by $L_0 \ge 1/S$: in the small-window limit $R|t|, R|\eta| \to 0$, the orthogonalized kernel obeys the uniform Taylor expansion $Q(x) - (J/A_P)P(x) = t\eta\bigl(x^2 - \frac{R^2}{3}\bigr) + O\!\left(|t\eta|R^2\bigl((Rt)^2 + (R\eta)^2\bigr)\right)$ on $[-R, R]$, whence $A_Q - J^2/A_P = \frac{8}{45} t^2\eta^2 R^5\bigl(1 + O((Rt)^2 + (R\eta)^2)\bigr)$, so the exact minimum $L^2$ norm strictly exceeds the Cauchy–Schwarz bound by an asymptotic factor **$\frac{3\sqrt{5}}{2} \approx 3.354102$** (80-digit numerical check rounded as shown, moment residuals $< 10^{-75}$, `NUM`: at $(R, t, \eta) = (1, 1, 0.1)$, exact minimum $\approx 23.32135$ vs. $1/(\sqrt{2R}S) \approx 7.05930$, ratio $3.3036$; at $(1, 4, 0.3)$, $\approx 7.10488$ vs. $2.32204$, ratio $3.0598$; at $(2, 1, 0.3)$, $\approx 1.50665$ vs. $0.78536$, ratio $1.9184$; at $(1, 0.001, 0.002)$, $\approx 1.185854\times 10^6$ vs. $3.53553\times 10^5$, ratio $3.354104 \to \frac{3\sqrt{5}}{2}$).

**Actual Burnol packets, support caps, and repeated squaring.** The Burnol packet construction maps a spectral point $\rho = \beta + it$ to

$$
\gamma \;:=\; \frac{\rho - 1/2}{i} \;=\; t + i\!\left(\frac{1}{2} - \beta\right), \qquad \eta \;=\; \frac{1}{2} - \beta,
$$

so $(z_+, z_-) = (\gamma, \overline{\gamma})$. Within a finite packet `OrbitBurnolPacket` and the unit-support construction in the module `UnitSupportBurnolPacket`, let $q = |\iota|$ be the channel count given by the cardinality of the finite index set $\iota$, and let $N \in \mathbb{N}$ be the shared localization (convolution) depth ($N$ is neither the channel count $q$ nor identified by default with the divisibility-graph vertex cap or graph squaring depth $d$): for each channel $i \in \iota$, `burnolBasis` convolves the $(N + 1)$-fold peak power `convolutionSuccPower` of $P.\mathrm{peak}$ at depth $N$ with the seed $P.\mathrm{killer}(i)$. For each $j \in \iota$, both the $j$-th seed $P.\mathrm{killer}(j)$ prescribed by `killer_values` and the localized synthesis `burnolSynthesis` evaluated on the $j$-th one-hot coefficient vector give exact signed readouts $F(\gamma) = 1, F(\overline{\gamma}) = -1$ ($\varepsilon = 0$) of the same orbit in that packet. Because $S$ is nondecreasing in $R$, any support cap $R$ satisfying $\operatorname{supp} g \subseteq [-R, R]$ applies validly: at the seed stage one only has $\operatorname{supp} P.\mathrm{killer}(j) \subseteq [-1, 1]$, so $R = 1$ is a **seed support cap** rather than an exact support equality; certified second-order jet upper budgets $J_0 \ge L_0, J_2 \ge L_2$ inherit the $R = 1$ bounds, with $1/S$ scaling as $\frac{1}{\min(1, |t|)\,|\eta|}$ as $\eta \to 0$ at fixed $t \neq 0$. At the one-hot `burnolSynthesis` stage, unit-support convolution gives $\operatorname{supp} \subseteq [-(N + 2), N + 2]$, so $R_N = N + 2$ is likewise only a **support cap** rather than an exact support radius. Under $L_0 \le L$, substituting $R_N = N + 2$ shows that exact readout requires $(N + 2)|\eta| \ge \operatorname{arsinh}(1/L)$; for the same exact-readout packet, when its norm upper bound $L$ and support-radius cap $R$ are fixed, $R|\eta| \ge \operatorname{arsinh}(1/L)$ prevents $|\eta|$ from being arbitrarily small, and increasing nominal multiplication layers alone does not cancel this necessary condition. When $N + 1 = 2^k$ is formed by $k$ repeated-squaring steps, $R_N = 2^k + 1$ is again only an available support cap—neither a minimum required support radius nor a refutation of logarithmic multiplication depth or an arithmetic runtime lower bound—showing only that $k$ squaring stages can conceal a $2^k + 1$ support cap.

**Necessary check, signal normalization, and scope boundaries.** Under an amplitude budget $L_0 \le L$ with $L > 0$, such as $L_0 \le 2RA$ from $|g| \le A$ on $[-R, R]$, unit readout forces $R|\eta| \ge \operatorname{arsinh}\!\bigl(\frac{1-\varepsilon}{mL}\bigr) \ge \operatorname{arsinh}\!\bigl(\frac{1-\varepsilon}{L}\bigr)$. Over a certified enclosure $|\eta| \le \eta_{\max}$, the strict inequality $L\sinh(R\eta_{\max}) < 1 - \varepsilon$ excludes readout under that budget: for any $R, L > 0$ and $0 \le \varepsilon < 1$, the generic complex spectral node $t = 1/R$, $\eta = \operatorname{arsinh}((1-\varepsilon)/L)/(2R)$—a generic test node rather than an asserted $\zeta$-zero—gives $m = 1$ and $L\sinh(R\eta) < 1 - \varepsilon$. Satisfying $L\sinh(R\eta_{\max}) \ge 1 - \varepsilon$ passes only a single-orbit necessary check and is not sufficient for single- or multi-node interpolation to exist. For opposite targets $\pm a$ with absolute error at most $e \ge 0$, one has $L_0 S \ge \max(0, |a| - e)$; scaling $g$ by $a$ scales the quadratic Weil functional by $|a|^2$, so reducing $|a|$ shrinks the raw $L^1$ budget only by shrinking signal and tolerated error proportionally. When $a \neq 0$ and $0 \le e < |a|$, setting $\varepsilon := e / |a| \in [0, 1)$ and $B := L / |a|$ gives

$$
L_0 S \;\ge\; \max(0,\; |a| - e), \qquad B S \;\ge\; 1 - \frac{e}{|a|} \;=\; 1 - \varepsilon,
$$

producing the signal-normalized **necessary budget lower bound**

$$
B_{\min} \;:=\; \frac{1 - \varepsilon}{\min(1, R|t|)\sinh(R|\eta|)}.
$$

This quantity is a necessary $L^1$ lower bound on $B$, not an achievable minimum (at exact unit readout $\varepsilon = 0$, the exact $L^2$ minimum norm is given by the Schur-complement formula $\frac{1}{\sqrt{A_Q - J^2/A_P}}$ above, whereas for $0 < \varepsilon < 1$ the scaled packet $(1 - \varepsilon)g_*$ already satisfies the tolerance with smaller norm $\frac{1 - \varepsilon}{\sqrt{A_Q - J^2/A_P}}$). Four boundaries apply throughout: this derivation asserts no actual off-line $\zeta$-zeros (none exist under the externally verified [@conj-riemann-hypothesis]), does not itself prove the Riemann Hypothesis, cannot be inverted to deduce mixed spectral majorant lower bounds from lower bounds on $(L_0, L_2)$, and establishes no unconditional arithmetic or bit-complexity lower bound.
:::

::: {.viz src="arithmetic-schur-lab" preset="budget" height="500"}
**Necessary budget lower-bound surface and excluded region for resolving signed orbits with compactly supported even functions (`budget`).** The 3D $(R, |\eta|, B)$ stage displays the purple necessary budget lower-bound surface $B_{\min} = (1 - \varepsilon) / \bigl(\min(1, R|t|)\sinh(R|\eta|)\bigr)$, the magenta excluded region beneath it, and the amber dot marking the current parameter point. Controls adjust $t$ and relative error $\varepsilon$ while reading out the necessary budget lower bound $B_{\min}$; points in the magenta region violate the necessary condition, whereas lying outside it is necessary rather than sufficient for an interpolating function to exist.
:::

::: {.remark #rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget title="Explicit Conditional Joint Certification, Unconditional Jensen Super-Exponential Killer Bottleneck, and Critical Bandwidth Transition for Unit-Support Burnol Packets (Manual Derivation, Classical Literature, and 256/320-Bit Arb Verification)"}
**Provenance and verification status.** This remark continues [@rem-ch34-signed-orbit-support-budget] directly atop the frozen upstream `dev` interface tracked by this repository's branch lock (all $22$ inline `lean:` declarations cited below resolve via `frozen-state` as frozen `theorem`s with unchanged statement IDs at both the historical replay commit `637b05b5f1fda314ed62c68b3ce7963d2f815576` and 2026-11-22 `dev` snapshots including `351ecef3…`, `b8d8997f…`, and `4f9fb196…`, including [`exists_unit_support_orbitBurnolPacket`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/UnitSupportBurnolPacket.exists_unit_support_orbitBurnolPacket), [`unit_support_burnol_radius`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/UnitSupportBurnolPacket.unit_support_burnol_radius), [`peak_tail_of_two_jet_budget`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/UnitSupportBurnolPacket.peak_tail_of_two_jet_budget), [`finiteMixedMajorantTotal_le_head_tail`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/WeilMixedHeadTailBudget.finiteMixedMajorantTotal_le_head_tail), and [`rationalBurnol_support_and_margin`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/BurnolRationalDepthBudget.rationalBurnol_support_and_margin)), establishing an explicit computable conditional joint certification, an unconditional Jensen super-exponential lower bound, and a coarse-peak-route bit-cost audit (`Manual derivation` / `NUM`, not formalized in this book; core derivations, frozen GID audit, reproducible 256/320-bit `python-flint 0.9.0` scripts, full 256-bit Arb interval log, 320-bit exact-agreement addendum, and the `analytic=analytic` analyticity replay with like-for-like augmented diagnostics are archived in [Issue #918 main derivation](https://github.com/the-omega-institute/trureturing-books/issues/918#issuecomment-7074662770), [replay scripts and GID audit](https://github.com/the-omega-institute/trureturing-books/issues/918#issuecomment-7074665037), [256-bit Arb log](https://github.com/the-omega-institute/trureturing-books/issues/918#issuecomment-7074665300), [exact peak second-derivative infimum and 320-bit agreement addendum](https://github.com/the-omega-institute/trureturing-books/issues/918#issuecomment-7074680509), and [`analytic=analytic` replay and augmented diagnostics](https://github.com/the-omega-institute/trureturing-books/issues/918#issuecomment-7076146110), while the sharpened geometric-panel Clenshaw–Curtis quadrature analysis in item 3 is given directly in this remark). Under the externally verified classical Riemann Hypothesis ([@conj-riemann-hypothesis], `riemannZeta_eq_zero_re : RiemannZetaZerosOnLine`, not frozen in this book's pinned `trureturing` repository), no genuine off-line $\zeta$-zero exists and every genuine off-line orbit frame is empty ($q = 0$); in Lean, `ZeroData` requires every indexed point to be a genuine nontrivial $\zeta$-zero and the orbit frame selects its targets from that enumeration, so the hypothetical-target benchmarks below do **not** instantiate Lean's `OrbitBurnolPacket` on the actual $\zeta$-zero spectrum, but instead operate strictly **without invoking `riemannZeta_eq_zero_re : RiemannZetaZerosOnLine`** by executing the same finite-packet inequalities and bit-cost audit on an **augmented test spectrum** consisting of **all genuine nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros** together with adjoined hypothetical off-line four-point target orbits (assigned unit multiplicity $m_i = 1$), while separately relying on the Platt–Trudgian finite-height verification cited in item 2 to certify that all genuine nontrivial zeros inside all nine benchmark balls ($R_E \le 4.38\times 10^{10}$) lie on the critical line and are simple; meanwhile the peak lower bounds in item 1, the pointwise-killing Jensen super-exponential bottleneck in item 2 (with the leading coefficient $\frac{1}{2}$ for critical bandwidth applying in the full-multiplicity or simple-zero model carrying the full analytic mass of all retained genuine zeros in the two-sided ball with no genuine zero deletions, or with deletion loss controlled by the two-jet height cap as in item 2), and the zero-count and fourth-moment tail bounds in item 3 hold completely unconditionally.

1. **Frozen packet interface, necessary peak jet bounds, exact $L^2$ second-derivative infimum, and $\eta \to 0$ equal-target cancellation**: Retaining the spectral parametrization $\gamma_n := (\rho_n - \frac{1}{2})/i$ (with analytic multiplicity $m_n$) from [@rem-ch34-signed-orbit-support-budget], write the target off-line nodes as $z_i = t_i + i\eta_i$ and $\bar{z}_i = t_i - i\eta_i$ ($i \in \iota$, $q = \lvert\iota\rvert$, $t_i\eta_i \neq 0$; here $z_i$ follows the spectral coordinate $z_+ = t + i\eta$ of the preceding remark, distinct from its temporary parameter $\eta + it$ inside $W$), and for $q \ge 1$ put $R_0 := \max_{i\in\iota}\lvert z_i\rvert$ and $T_{\max} := \max_{i\in\iota}\lvert t_i\rvert$. In the abstract record `OrbitBurnolPacket`, `exceptional` may be any finite index set satisfying its fields, while the unit-support construction in `UnitSupportBurnolPacket` on genuine `ZeroData` specifically chooses the two-sided ball $E := Z.\mathrm{symmetricIndices}(R_E) = \{n : \lvert\gamma_n\rvert \le R_E\}$ (on the augmented test spectrum, write $E_{\mathrm{aug}}$ for the analogous finite index set consisting of all genuine nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros with $\lvert\gamma_n\rvert \le R_E$ together with the adjoined four-point target orbit index set $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$, and write $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}} \subseteq E$ for the complete four-point target orbit index set on a genuine frame); denoting this packet by $\mathrm{Pkt}$, its peak $b = \mathrm{Pkt}.\mathrm{peak}$ and channel killers $k_i = \mathrm{Pkt}.\mathrm{killer}(i)$ form `burnolBasis` $= b^{*(N+1)} * k_i$ and `burnolSynthesis` $= \sum_{i\in\iota} a_i(b^{*(N+1)} * k_i)$ at shared depth $N$. Write $\lVert b^{(\ell)}\rVert_{L^1}$ for the true $L^1$ norms and $J_\ell^{\mathrm{peak}} \ge \lVert b^{(\ell)}\rVert_{L^1}$ ($\ell = 0, 2$) for their certified upper budgets. For any smooth even peak with $\operatorname{supp} b \subseteq [-1, 1]$ and $\widehat{b}(z_i) = \widehat{b}(\bar{z}_i) = 1$, direct integration and two integrations by parts give $\lvert\widehat{b}(z)\rvert \le e^{\lvert\eta\rvert}\lVert b\rVert_{L^1}$ and (for $z \neq 0$) $\lvert\widehat{b}(z)\rvert \le e^{\lvert\eta\rvert}\lVert b''\rVert_{L^1} / \lvert z\rvert^2$, whence $(1 + \lvert z\rvert^2)\lvert\widehat{b}(z)\rvert \le e^{\lvert\eta\rvert}(\lVert b\rVert_{L^1} + \lVert b''\rVert_{L^1})$, forcing $J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} \ge \lVert b\rVert_{L^1} \ge e^{-\lvert\eta_i\rvert}$ and $J_2^{\mathrm{peak}} \ge \lVert b''\rVert_{L^1} \ge \lvert z_i\rvert^2 e^{-\lvert\eta_i\rvert}$. Meanwhile, the general frozen closed-strip lemmas [`closedStripJetBudget_spec`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/TestFunctions/FourierLaplaceClosedStripDecay.closedStripJetBudget_spec) and [`closedStripJetBudget_le_three_jets`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/WeilMixedHeadTailBudget.closedStripJetBudget_le_three_jets) directly yield $3(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}}) \ge 1 + t_i^2$ for any nonempty target in the half-strip $\lvert\eta_i\rvert \le \frac{1}{2}$ (covering all hypothetical nodes with $\lvert\eta_i\rvert \le 0.25$ below; for the specialization to genuine `ZeroData` zeros, see [`zero_transform_pair_le_three_jets`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/WeilMixedHeadTailBudget.zero_transform_pair_le_three_jets)), while `peak_tail_of_two_jet_budget` takes $R_E \ge 6(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}}) + 1$ as a sufficient hypothesis for the two-jet cutoff—hence **whenever that two-jet cutoff formula is used with a nonempty target set ($q \ge 1$, $\lvert\eta_i\rvert \le \frac{1}{2}$)**, one unconditionally gets

   $$
   R_E \;\ge\; 2 T_{\max}^2 + 3
   $$

   (for $q = 0$, both maxima are empty and no target-based radius floor arises).

   For a single channel $z = t + i\eta$ ($t\eta \neq 0$), take the real even basis $P(x) = \cos(tx)\cosh(\eta x)$, $Q(x) = \sin(tx)\sinh(\eta x)$ at $R = 1$ from [@rem-ch34-signed-orbit-support-budget] with $2\times 2$ Gram matrix $G_1 := \begin{pmatrix} A_P & J \\ J & A_Q \end{pmatrix}$ (evaluated via $W := \frac{\sinh(2(\eta + it))}{4(\eta + it)}$): the equal-target peak moments $(1, 0)$ give the exact minimum in $L^2_{\mathrm{even}}([-1, 1])$ (and exact infimum over $C_c^\infty((-1, 1))_{\mathrm{even}}$) as $(A_P - J^2/A_Q)^{-1/2}$ (e.g., $\approx 1.0818$ at $(1, 0.25)$ and $\approx 1.00014$ at $(14.134725, 0.1)$). For the second derivative $f = b''$, in addition to the integration-by-parts moments $v_2 := (\eta^2 - t^2,\; 2t\eta)^\top$, zero total mass $\int_{-1}^1 f = 0$ is mandatory (evenness automatically gives $\int_{-1}^1 x f = 0$; conversely, for any $f \in C_c^\infty((-1, 1))_{\mathrm{even}}$ satisfying $\int_{-1}^1 f = 0$, setting $b(x) := \int_{-1}^x (x - y)f(y)\,dy$ recovers a smooth even function $b$ with $\operatorname{supp} b \subseteq (-1, 1)$, and combining this with the two prescribed moments $v_2$ on $f = b''$ together with $\widehat{f}(z) = -z^2\widehat{b}(z)$ ($z \neq 0$) recovers the equal-target peak readout $\widehat{b}(z) = \widehat{b}(\bar{z}) = 1$). Because $t\eta \neq 0$, comparing the $x^2, x^4$ Taylor coefficients shows that $1, P, Q$ are linearly independent in $L^2_{\mathrm{even}}([-1, 1])$, so projecting $P, Q$ orthogonally onto the orthogonal complement $1^\perp$ of constants via $v_0 := \bigl(\operatorname{Re}\frac{2\sin z}{z},\; -\operatorname{Im}\frac{2\sin z}{z}\bigr)^\top$ produces a strictly positive-definite invertible projected Gram matrix $G_1 - \frac{1}{2}\,v_0 v_0^\top$; density of $C_c^\infty((-1, 1))_{\mathrm{even}}$ together with a three-dimensional moment correction preserving zero mass and both $v_2$ moments then yields the **exact $L^2$ second-derivative infimum**

   $$
   \inf_{\substack{b \in C_c^\infty((-1, 1))_{\mathrm{even}} \\ \widehat{b}(z) = \widehat{b}(\bar{z}) = 1}} \lVert b''\rVert_{L^2} \;=\; \sqrt{v_2^\top\!\left(G_1 - \frac{1}{2}\,v_0 v_0^\top\right)^{-1} v_2}
   $$

   (Arb verification: $\approx 17.1088$, $17.1468$, $32.2698$, and $209.5128$ at $(1, 0.25)$, $(1, 0.01)$, $(5, 0.1)$, and $(14.134725, 0.1)$, strictly exceeding the two-moment lower bound $(v_2^\top G_1^{-1} v_2)^{1/2} \approx 5.3717, 5.4274, 32.0213, 208.5780$ that omits $\int_{-1}^1 f = 0$; the corresponding exact $L^1$ infima are given by the duals $[\inf_{\lambda\in\mathbb{R}}\lVert P - \lambda Q\rVert_{L^\infty([-1, 1])}]^{-1}$ and $\sup_{\lambda_0, \lambda_P, \lambda_Q \in \mathbb{R}}\{v_{2,P}\lambda_P + v_{2,Q}\lambda_Q : \lVert\lambda_0 + \lambda_P P + \lambda_Q Q\rVert_{L^\infty([-1, 1])} \le 1\}$).

   To turn the existence theorems into an explicit computable witness, note that the frozen seed `normalizedEvenSeed` (see [`normalizedEvenSeed_tsupport`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/InterpolationJets/QuantitativeEvenSeed.normalizedEvenSeed_tsupport) and [`quantitativeEvenSeed_transform_lower`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/InterpolationJets/QuantitativeEvenSeed.quantitativeEvenSeed_transform_lower), both specific to that construction) uses Mathlib's normalized `ContDiffBump` (inner radius $h/2$, outer radius $h$), whereas our explicit numerical witness uses the piecewise even bump that is smooth and compactly supported on $\mathbb{R}$ and real-analytic on its interior $(-1, 1)$: $\psi_1(x) := Z_1^{-1} e^{-1/(1-x^2)}$ for $\lvert x\rvert < 1$ and $\psi_1(x) := 0$ for $\lvert x\rvert \ge 1$ (Arb-certified normalization $Z_1 \approx 0.4439938162$, full ball of radius $< 4.61\times 10^{-57}$ in Issue #918), together with its rescaling $\psi_h(x) := h^{-1}\psi_1(x/h)$ (unit mass and unit $L^1$ norm, support in $[-h, h]$, derivative scaling $\lVert\psi_h^{(m)}\rVert_{L^1} = h^{-m} B_m$). The explicit bump $\psi_h$ meets the hypotheses of the general perturbation theorem [`fourierLaplace_sub_one_norm_le`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/InterpolationJets/QuantitativeEvenSeed.fourierLaplace_sub_one_norm_le) (with $h = \frac{1}{4(R_{\mathrm{ball}} + 1)}$, $\lvert\widehat{\psi_h}(w) - 1\rvert \le 2h\lvert w\rvert < \frac{1}{2}$ on $\lvert w\rvert \le R_{\mathrm{ball}}$, hence $\lvert\widehat{\psi_h}(w)\rvert > \frac{1}{2}$) and serves as an explicit seed witness for the even polynomial differential interpolation identity [`fourierLaplace_evenPolynomialDifferential`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/TestFunctions/EvenTestFunctionFiniteInterpolation.fourierLaplace_evenPolynomialDifferential) (for the corresponding existential finite-interpolation theorems, see [`even_weilTestFunction_finite_interpolation_with_radius`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/TestFunctions/EvenTestFunctionFiniteInterpolation.even_weilTestFunction_finite_interpolation_with_radius) and [`even_weilTestFunction_finite_interpolation_unit_support`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/TestFunctions/EvenTestFunctionFiniteInterpolation.even_weilTestFunction_finite_interpolation_unit_support)). Setting $\delta_x := 1 - x^2$ and $g_0(x) := e^{-1/\delta_x}$: for every interior point $x \in (-1, 1)$, on the disk $\lvert v\rvert \le \frac{1}{2}\delta_x$ we have $\lvert(2xv + v^2)/\delta_x\rvert \le r_0(x) := \lvert x\rvert + \frac{1}{4}\delta_x < 1$; combining the conformal-disk bound $\operatorname{Re}(1 - \xi)^{-1} \ge (1 + r_0(x))^{-1} > \frac{1}{2}$ ($\lvert\xi\rvert \le r_0(x) < 1$) with Cauchy's integral formula yields endpoint flatness and the elementary Gevrey-2 all-order bound for every $m \ge 1$,

   $$
   B_m \;:=\; \lVert\psi_1^{(m)}\rVert_{L^1} \;\le\; \frac{2}{Z_1}\,m!\left(\frac{4m}{e}\right)^m
   $$

   ($B_0 = 1$; Arb-certified rounded values: $B_2 \approx 7.193161$, $B_4 \approx 2423.748$, $B_6 \approx 1.197446\times 10^7$, $B_8 \approx 2.846292\times 10^{11}$). Assuming pairwise distinct squared nodes $u_\mu := w_\mu^2$ ($w_\mu \in \{z_i, \bar{z}_i\}$; ensured within each channel by $t_i\eta_i \neq 0$, and across channels jointly by indexed target distinctness `nodeEquiv` and opposite-sign separation `signSeparated`, giving $w_\mu \neq \pm w_\nu$), taking $h_0 := \frac{1}{4(R_0 + 1)}$ and the Lagrange peak polynomial $P_b(u) := \sum_{\mu=1}^{2q} \widehat{\psi_{h_0}}(w_\mu)^{-1}\prod_{\nu\neq \mu}\frac{u - u_\nu}{u_\mu - u_\nu} = \sum_{m=0}^{2q-1} p_m u^m$ of degree at most $2q - 1$, we set $b := P_b(-\partial_x^2)\psi_{h_0}$. **Key $\eta \to 0$ removable-singularity cancellation**: for $q = 1$, writing $\alpha_+ := \widehat{\psi_{h_0}}(z_1)$, the real coefficients of $P_b(u) = p_0 + p_1 u$ obey $p_1 = \frac{\alpha_+^{-1} - \overline{\alpha_+}^{-1}}{4it_1\eta_1} = \frac{-\operatorname{Im}\alpha_+}{2t_1\eta_1\lvert\alpha_+\rvert^2}$; since $\operatorname{Im}\alpha_+ = -\int_{-h_0}^{h_0} \sin(t_1 x)\sinh(\eta_1 x)\psi_{h_0}(x)\,dx$ satisfies $\lvert\operatorname{Im}\alpha_+\rvert \le \lvert t_1\eta_1\rvert h_0^2\cosh(\eta_1 h_0)$ and $\lvert\alpha_+\rvert > \frac{1}{2}$, the denominator $t_1\eta_1$ cancels identically, giving $\lvert p_1\rvert \le 2h_0^2\cosh(\eta_1 h_0)$ and $\lvert p_0\rvert \le 2 + \lvert p_1\rvert R_0^2$, hence $\lVert b\rVert_{L^1} = O(1)$ and $\lVert b''\rVert_{L^1} = O((1 + R_0)^2)$ uniformly as $\eta_1 \to 0$. For any fixed $q \ge 1$ (and in particular, for $q \ge 2$, as the inter-orbit collision spacing $\delta := \min_{i \neq j}\lvert z_i - z_j\rvert \to 0$, as in the two-channel benchmark $(1, 0.1)$ and $(1 + \delta, 0.1)$ in item 4), evenness makes $H(u) := 1/\widehat{\psi_{h_0}}(\sqrt{u})$ single-valued and analytic on the disk $\lvert u\rvert \le R_{\mathrm{ct}} := 4(R_0 + 1)^2$ with $\lvert\widehat{\psi_{h_0}}(\sqrt{u}) - 1\rvert \le \cosh(\frac{1}{2}) - 1 < \frac{1}{2}$ (so $\lvert H(\zeta)\rvert \le 2$), while on the contour $\lvert\zeta\rvert = R_{\mathrm{ct}}$ we have $\lvert u_\nu\rvert \le R_0^2 \le \frac{1}{4} R_{\mathrm{ct}}$, giving $\lvert\zeta - u_\nu\rvert \ge 3(R_0 + 1)^2$, whence Newton divided differences satisfy $\lvert H[u_1, \dots, u_{m+1}]\rvert \le \frac{8}{3}\cdot 3^{-m}(R_0 + 1)^{-2m}$ and expanding the Newton basis gives monomial coefficient scaling $\lvert p_m\rvert = O_q((R_0 + 1)^{-2m})$ ($0 \le m \le 2q - 1$); multiplying by the derivative scaling $h_0^{-1} = 4(R_0 + 1)$ yields $\lvert p_m\rvert h_0^{-2m} B_{2m} = O_q(1)$ and $\lvert p_m\rvert h_0^{-2m-2} B_{2m+2} = O_q((R_0 + 1)^2)$ term by term, establishing $\lVert b\rVert_{L^1} = O_q(1)$ and $\lVert b''\rVert_{L^1} = O_q((R_0 + 1)^2)$ uniformly over target configurations for each fixed $q \ge 1$ while canceling inter-orbit $\delta \to 0$ collision blow-up for $q \ge 2$. By contrast, the opposite-sign slope $\operatorname{Re}(\alpha_+^{-1})/(2it_1\eta_1)$ has $\operatorname{Re}(\alpha_+^{-1}) > \frac{2}{3}$ from $\lvert\alpha_+ - 1\rvert < \frac{1}{2}$, so no cancellation occurs and the intrinsic $1/\lvert t_1\eta_1\rvert$ blow-up of [@rem-ch34-signed-orbit-support-budget] persists.

2. **Unconditional Jensen / Paley–Wiener super-exponential killer bottleneck and critical bandwidth transition**: Feeding the two-jet cutoff $R_E \ge 2 T_{\max}^2 + 3$ and genuine $\zeta$-zeros into the killer $k_i$ immediately triggers super-exponential growth under unit support. For any integrable even killer $k$ with $\operatorname{supp} k \subseteq [-A, A]$ ($A > 0$) and $K(z_i) := \widehat{k}(z_i) = 1$, fix a finite set $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}} \subset \mathbb{C}$ of **distinct** complex locations equipped with certified positive integer vanishing weights $1 \le \nu_w \le \operatorname{ord}_w(K)$ ($\nu_w = 1$ per pointwise zero under `kills_exception`, or $\nu_w = m_w$ under Hermite vanishing or certified simplicity; summing over distinct locations avoids double-counting multiplicities), and define the centered counting function $n_{z_i}(r) := \sum_{w \in \mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}},\; \lvert w - z_i\rvert < r} \nu_w$ and Jensen sum $S(\rho) := \sum_{w \in \mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}},\; \lvert w - z_i\rvert < \rho} \nu_w\,\ln\frac{\rho}{\lvert w - z_i\rvert} = \int_0^\rho \frac{n_{z_i}(r)}{r}\,dr$ depending solely on the prescribed zero data. Paley–Wiener growth $\lvert K(w)\rvert \le \lVert k\rVert_{L^1} e^{A\lvert\operatorname{Im} w\rvert}$ and Jensen's formula on $\lvert w - z_i\rvert \le \rho$ (taking a limit from neighboring zero-free radii $\rho' \to \rho$ and using continuity of $S(\rho)$ and $I(\rho, \eta_i)$ whenever a zero of $K$ lies on the boundary circle $\lvert w - z_i\rvert = \rho$) then give for every $\rho > 0$

   $$
   \begin{aligned}
   \ln\lVert k\rVert_{L^1} &\;\ge\; S(\rho) \;-\; A\,I(\rho, \eta_i), \\
   I(\rho, \eta) &\;:=\; \frac{1}{2\pi}\int_0^{2\pi} \lvert\eta + \rho\sin\theta\rvert\,d\theta \;=\; \begin{cases} \lvert\eta\rvert, & 0 < \rho \le \lvert\eta\rvert, \\ \frac{2}{\pi}\!\left(\sqrt{\rho^2 - \eta^2} + \lvert\eta\rvert\arcsin\frac{\lvert\eta\rvert}{\rho}\right), & \rho \ge \lvert\eta\rvert. \end{cases}
   \end{aligned}
   $$

   Setting $\vartheta := \lvert\eta\rvert/\rho \in [0, 1]$, the dimensionless quantity inside the parentheses minus $1$ equals $\int_0^\vartheta \arcsin\tau\,d\tau \in \bigl[\frac{1}{2}\vartheta^2,\; (\frac{\pi}{2} - 1)\vartheta^2\bigr]$, so $\frac{\eta^2}{\pi\rho}$ is a **lower** correction to $\frac{2\rho}{\pi}$, whereas the sharp uniform quadratic **upper** bound is $I(\rho, \eta) \le \frac{2\rho}{\pi} + (1 - \frac{2}{\pi})\frac{\eta^2}{\rho}$ (alongside the triangle bound $I(\rho, \eta) \le \frac{2\rho}{\pi} + \lvert\eta\rvert$; Arb quadrature at $(\rho, \lvert\eta\rvert) = (1, 0.25)$ certifies $I \approx 0.656620 > \frac{2}{\pi} + \frac{0.0625}{\pi} \approx 0.656514$).

   One must carefully distinguish **pointwise killing from full analytic multiplicity**: let $N_+(T)$ denote the right-continuous count of nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros with imaginary part in $(0, T]$, counted with analytic multiplicity. The frozen field `kills_exception` prescribes only pointwise vanishing $K_i(\gamma_n) = 0$ ($\operatorname{ord}_{\gamma_n}(K_i) \ge 1$) at each non-target exceptional index $n \in E \setminus \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$, not Hermite vanishing; full analytic multiplicities $m_n$ enter Jensen's sum only when Hermite vanishing is additionally imposed or simplicity of the relevant nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros is independently certified. For $R_E \ge \frac{1}{2}$, the two-sided **analytic-multiplicity** sum over nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros in the genuine two-sided ball $E = Z.\mathrm{symmetricIndices}(R_E)$ unconditionally satisfies $2N_+(\sqrt{R_E^2 - 1/4}) \le \sum_{n\in E} m_n \le 2N_+(R_E)$ (by itself, this does not give the distinct-zero count for pointwise killing); let $M_E := \sum_{w \in \mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}} \nu_w$ denote the certified two-sided non-target total weight under the active regime (when genuine targets lie inside $E$, subtract $4\sum_{i\in\iota} m_i$ under full multiplicity or $4q$ for distinct target nodes; in the augmented test-spectrum benchmarks below, hypothetical targets are adjoined separately outside the genuine zero count $2N_+$, so no subtraction applies to $2N_+$). When all prescribed locations lie inside the closed ball ($\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}} \subset \{w : \lvert w\rvert \le R_E\}$, so every location obeys $\lvert w - z_i\rvert \le D := R_E + \lvert z_i\rvert$), taking $A = 1$ and $\rho = \frac{\pi M_E}{2} > D$ gives the coarse bound $\ln\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1} \ge M_E \ln\frac{\pi M_E}{2eD} - \lvert\eta_i\rvert$; when $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}$ carries the **full analytic-multiplicity mass of all retained genuine zeros** in the two-sided ball (i.e., full multiplicity or certified simplicity, with any deleted genuine target ordinates confined to $O(\sqrt{R_E})$ or a fixed finite set), pairing $\gamma$ with $-\gamma$ ($u = \operatorname{Re}\gamma > 0$, $\lvert\operatorname{Im}\gamma\rvert \le \frac{1}{2}$) and using $\lvert z_i - \gamma\rvert\lvert z_i + \gamma\rvert = \lvert z_i^2 - \gamma^2\rvert \le \lvert\gamma\rvert^2 + \lvert z_i\rvert^2 \le u^2 + a_0$ ($a_0 = \frac{1}{4} + \lvert z_i\rvert^2$) bounds the retained Jensen sum below for every $\rho \ge D$ by the Stieltjes comparison $\int_0^T \ln\frac{\rho^2}{u^2 + a_0}\,dN_+(u) = N_+(T)\ln\frac{\rho^2}{T^2 + a_0} + 2\int_0^T \frac{u N_+(u)}{u^2 + a_0}\,du$ ($T = \sqrt{R_E^2 - 1/4}$) minus $m_\gamma\ln\frac{\rho^2}{u^2 + a_0}$ for every deleted positive-height genuine target atom in $(0, T]$ (empty in the nine augmented benchmarks below, which delete no genuine zeros); in finite computations with $T \ge H_{\mathrm{cut}} = 1500$ and $\rho > D$ we sum exact Jensen terms $\nu_w\ln\frac{\rho}{\lvert w - z_i\rvert}$ only over retained isolated locations below $H_{\mathrm{cut}}$, take the shifted Stieltjes integral $\int_{(H_{\mathrm{cut}}, T]} \ln\frac{\rho^2}{u^2 + a_0}\,dN_+(u) = \bigl(N_+(T) - N_+(H_{\mathrm{cut}})\bigr)\ln\frac{\rho^2}{T^2 + a_0} + 2\int_{H_{\mathrm{cut}}}^T \frac{u(N_+(u) - N_+(H_{\mathrm{cut}}))}{u^2 + a_0}\,du$ on the high segment $(H_{\mathrm{cut}}, T]$, and subtract $m_\gamma\ln\frac{\rho^2}{u^2 + a_0}$ ($u = \operatorname{Re}\gamma$) for each deleted positive-height genuine target atom in $(H_{\mathrm{cut}}, T]$ (also empty across all nine augmented test-spectrum benchmarks below), which both preserves near-target contributions and, asymptotically at fixed center as $R_E \to \infty$ with $\rho \simeq \frac{\pi M_E}{2}$, cancels the leading $-M_E$ loss via the $(\frac{1}{\pi} + o(1))R_E\ln R_E$ main term of the integration-by-parts integral $2\int_0^T \frac{u N_+(u)}{u^2 + a_0}\,du$ (the unconditional $0.4058$ simple-zero proportion below does not by itself supply that $dN_+$ integral for pointwise killing). **Completely unconditional super-exponential lower bound (independent of RH)**: by H. M. Bui, J. B. Conrey & M. P. Young (*More than 41% of the zeros of the zeta function are on the critical line*, *Acta Arith.* **150**(1), 35–64, 2011, Theorem 1.1, [10.4064/aa150-1-3](https://doi.org/10.4064/aa150-1-3)), the proportion of simple nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros on the critical line has $\liminf \ge 0.4058$, and none of these critical-line zeros can belong to any hypothetical off-line target orbit; thus for fixed unit support $A = 1$, a two-sided-ball exceptional set (so $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}} \subset \{w : \lvert w\rvert \le R_E\}$ includes all eligible simple critical-line zeros in the ball), $\lvert z_i\rvert = O(R_E)$, and discarded target weight $o(R_E\ln R_E)$, **pointwise `kills_exception` alone unconditionally forces as $R_E \to \infty$**

   $$
   \liminf_{R_E \to \infty} \frac{\ln\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1}}{R_E \ln R_E \ln\ln R_E} \;\ge\; \frac{0.4058}{\pi},
   $$

   which combined with the two-jet cutoff $R_E \ge 2 T_{\max}^2 + 3$ from item 1 (as $T_{\max} \to \infty$ with discarded target weight $o(T_{\max}^2\ln T_{\max})$) yields $\ln\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1} \ge \bigl(\frac{1.6232}{\pi} - o(1)\bigr) T_{\max}^2 \ln T_{\max} \ln\ln T_{\max}$. When $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}$ carries the full analytic-multiplicity mass of all retained genuine zeros in the ball along an unbounded family $R_E \to \infty$ (e.g., Hermite killing on all non-target genuine zeros in the ball, or simplicity along $R_E \to \infty$; note that Hermite killing restricted to the critical line alone still requires a full-density input on that line) with discarded target weight $o(R_E\ln R_E)$, $M_E = (\frac{1}{\pi} + o(1))R_E \ln R_E$ sharpens these **asymptotic** leading constants to **$\frac{1}{\pi}$** (and $\frac{4}{\pi}$ in $T_{\max}$); on finite heights, D. Platt & T. Trudgian (*The Riemann hypothesis is true up to $3\cdot 10^{12}$*, *Bull. London Math. Soc.* **53**(3), 792–797, 2021, Theorem 1 and §2, [10.1112/blms.12460](https://doi.org/10.1112/blms.12460)) verify via sign changes plus Turing completeness that every **nontrivial** $\zeta$-zero up to height $3{,}000{,}175{,}332{,}800$ lies on the critical line and is simple, directly supplying full pointwise simple-zero weight across all nine benchmarks ($R_E \le 4.38\times 10^{10}$) below. Moreover, for the explicit differential killer of item 3 whose actual support radius is $h_E = \frac{1}{4(R_E + 1)}$, substituting $A = h_E$ and $\rho = \frac{\pi M_E}{2 h_E}$ strengthens its Jensen lower bound to **$\Omega(R_E \ln^2 R_E)$**.

   **Critical bandwidth and bandwidth–density phase transition (genuine two-sided ball full-multiplicity model)**: For a given prescribed finite zero set $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}$ (with weights $1 \le \nu_w \le \operatorname{ord}_w(K)$) and center $z_i$, define the critical bandwidth for a positive logarithmic Jensen lower bound by $A_{\mathrm{crit}} := \sup_{\rho > 0}\frac{S(\rho)}{I(\rho, \eta_i)}$ (when $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}} = \emptyset$, $S(\rho) \equiv 0$ so $A_{\mathrm{crit}} = 0$; whenever $A < A_{\mathrm{crit}}$, some $\rho > 0$ yields $\ln\lVert k\rVert_{L^1} > 0$, whereas whenever $A \ge A_{\mathrm{crit}}$, $S(\rho) - A\,I(\rho, \eta_i) \le 0$ for every $\rho > 0$). Matching the Paley–Wiener Nyquist capacity $\frac{2A}{\pi}$ against the Riemann zero density $\frac{1}{\pi}\ln R_E$ at a fixed center when $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}$ consists of genuine nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros in the two-sided ball $\lvert\gamma\rvert \le R_E$ with weights bounded by their genuine $\zeta$-multiplicities ($1 \le \nu_w \le m_w$): on the lower side, when $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}$ carries **full analytic-multiplicity weight** $\nu_w = m_w$ on all **retained** genuine zeros in the ball (including the augmented test spectrum where no genuine zeros are deleted, or a genuine frame where the two-jet height cap $\lvert t_j\rvert \le \sqrt{R_E}$ controls **deletion loss**—at fixed center and fixed positive separation, deleted target ordinates are $O(\sqrt{R_E})$ and remove only $O_{z_i}(\sqrt{R_E}\ln^2 R_E)$ at $\rho = R_E + \lvert z_i\rvert$, noting that the two-jet cap controls only deletion loss while the lower bound itself still requires full analytic multiplicity on all retained genuine zeros, and that discarded mass $o(R_E\ln R_E)$ alone does not guarantee an $O_{z_i}(R_E)$ Jensen loss), taking $A = \kappa\ln R_E$ ($0 < \kappa < \frac{1}{2}$) with $\rho \sim \frac{R_E}{2\kappa}$ forces $\ln\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1} \ge \bigl(\frac{1}{\pi}\ln\frac{1}{2\kappa} - o(1)\bigr) R_E \ln R_E$, while at $\rho = R_E + \lvert z_i\rvert$ the full-multiplicity Stieltjes estimate gives $S(\rho) \ge \frac{R_E}{\pi}\ln R_E - O_{z_i}(R_E)$ and $I(\rho, \eta_i) = \frac{2 R_E}{\pi} + O_{z_i}(1)$, dividing to prove $A_{\mathrm{crit}} \ge \frac{1}{2}\ln R_E - O_{z_i}(1)$. On the upper side, for $R_E \ge e$ and $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}} \neq \emptyset$ (for $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}} = \emptyset$, $A_{\mathrm{crit}} = 0$ trivially), put $a_z := e + \lvert t_i\rvert$; because the genuine nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros form a discrete set in $\mathbb{C}$ and $z_i \notin \mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}$ (from $K(z_i) = 1$), we fix a positive separation floor $d_z > 0$ **uniformly in $R_E$** at the fixed center $z_i$ satisfying $d_z \le \min_{w \in \mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}}\lvert w - z_i\rvert$. Item 3's Trudgian count implies $2N_+(u) \le \frac{u}{\pi}\ln u + 8u$ for $u \ge e$ (while for $u < e$, $N_+(u) = 0$ because the certified first positive nontrivial zero ordinate exceeds $14 > e$), and because $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}$ contains only genuine zeros in the ball with $\nu_w \le m_w$, the centered count $n_{z_i}(r)$ vanishes for $r < d_z$ and obeys $n_{z_i}(r) \le 2N_+(\min(R_E, r + \lvert t_i\rvert))$ for $r \ge d_z$, whence strict decrease of $r \mapsto \frac{\ln(r + a_z)}{r}$ gives $\frac{n_{z_i}(r)}{r} \le \frac{1}{\pi}\ln R_E + \frac{a_z}{\pi d_z}\ln(a_z + d_z) + 8(1 + \frac{a_z}{d_z})$; substituting into $S(\rho) = \int_0^\rho \frac{n_{z_i}(r)}{r}\,dr$ and pairing with $I(\rho, \eta_i) \ge \frac{2\rho}{\pi}$, the explicit $R_E$-independent constant $C_{z_i} := \frac{a_z}{2 d_z}\ln(a_z + d_z) + 4\pi(1 + \frac{a_z}{d_z})$ guarantees $A_{\mathrm{crit}} \le A$ for every $A \ge \frac{1}{2}\ln R_E + C_{z_i}$, eliminating the positive logarithmic Jensen obstruction arising from that prescribed genuine finite zero set (removing this necessary obstruction does not by itself construct a small-norm interpolating killer).

3. **Explicit computable conditional joint upper certification, fourth-moment tail, executable integer depth, and bit-cost audit**: Zero-count and fourth-moment tails rest on two unconditional primary references: (i) T. S. Trudgian (*An improved upper bound for the argument of the Riemann zeta-function on the critical line II*, *J. Number Theory* **134**, 280–292, 2014, Theorem 1, Corollary 1, and eq. (2.5), [10.1016/j.jnt.2013.07.017](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jnt.2013.07.017)) proves $\lvert N_+(T) - L_{\mathrm{RvM}}(T)\rvert \le E_{\mathrm{Tr}}(T) := 0.111\ln T + 0.275\ln\ln T + 2.450 + \frac{0.2}{T}$ for all $T \ge e$ (with $L_{\mathrm{RvM}}(T) := \frac{T}{2\pi}\ln\frac{T}{2\pi e} + \frac{7}{8}$), yielding via Stieltjes integration by parts and log-concavity the fourth-moment tail bound $\Theta_{\mathrm{Tr}}(R_E) := 2\bigl[\frac{\ln(T/(2\pi)) + 1/3}{6\pi T^3} + \frac{2E_{\mathrm{Tr}}(T) + (0.111 + 0.275/\ln T)/4}{T^4}\bigr]$ ($T := \sqrt{R_E^2 - 1/4} \ge e$); (ii) R. P. Brent, D. J. Platt & T. S. Trudgian (*Accurate estimation of sums over zeros of the Riemann zeta-function*, *Math. Comp.* **90**(332), 2923–2935, 2021, Theorem 1, eqs. (1)–(3), §2 eq. (10), Lemma 2, and Lemma 3, [10.1090/mcom/3652](https://doi.org/10.1090/mcom/3652)) bound the tail remainder beyond the smooth integral plus $-\phi(T)(N_+(T) - L_{\mathrm{RvM}}(T))$ for any $C^2$ weight $\phi$ with $\phi \ge 0$, $\phi' \le 0$, $\phi'' > 0$, and $\int_T^\infty \phi(u)\ln u\,du < \infty$ ($T \ge 2\pi$; with the half-weight convention at a zero ordinate, or right-hand limits with right-continuous $N_+(T)$ for a strict tail, while all benchmark cutoffs below avoid zero ordinates) by $2(2.067 + 0.059\ln T)\lvert\phi'(T)\rvert + (0.059 + \frac{1}{150})\frac{\phi(T)}{T}$. Setting $\phi(u) = u^{-4}$ (which satisfies $\phi''(u) = 20 u^{-6} > 0$), multiplying by the two-sided factor $2$, and inserting the exact FLINT count $N_+(T)$ gives the explicit sharper tail bound used in the tables below:

   $$
   \Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E) \;:=\; 2\!\left[\frac{\ln(T/(2\pi)) + 1/3}{6\pi T^3} \;+\; \frac{L_{\mathrm{RvM}}(T) - N_+(T)}{T^4} \;+\; \frac{8(2.067 + 0.059\ln T) + 0.059 + \frac{1}{150}}{T^5}\right]
   $$

   ($T := \sqrt{R_E^2 - 1/4} \ge 2\pi$); combining the $1{,}069$ FLINT-isolated simple positive zeros below $1500$ (which saturate the count; the $1{,}100$-th zero lies at height $\approx 1535.0423$) with the $\phi(u) = (1 + u^2)^{-2}$ two-sided tail (satisfying $\phi''(u) = \frac{4(5u^2 - 1)}{(1 + u^2)^4} > 0$ on $u \ge 2\pi$) certifies the two-sided fourth moment over **genuine nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros only**, $S_4^{\mathrm{gen}} := \sum_n \frac{m_n}{(1 + (\operatorname{Re}\gamma_n)^2)^2} \in [7.377245548\times 10^{-5} \pm 8.41\times 10^{-15}]$ (on the augmented test spectrum, adjoining a unit-multiplicity target orbit at height $t_i$ adds $4/(1 + t_i^2)^2$ to the full-spectrum fourth moment, e.g., $+1$ at $t_i = 1$).

   Starting from the certified peak budget $(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}}, J_2^{\mathrm{peak}})$ of item 1, choose a certified cutoff on the $1/1024$-dyadic rational grid satisfying the BPT domain $R_E \ge \max\bigl(R_0,\; 6(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}}) + 1,\; \sqrt{4\pi^2 + 1/4}\bigr)$ and $h_E := \frac{1}{4(R_E + 1)}$ (each of the nine benchmarks below additionally asserts the optional count-equality shortcut $N_+(R_E) = N_+(T)$, where $T = \sqrt{R_E^2 - 1/4}$, via FLINT to sandwich the exact two-sided ball count unambiguously; if for some input $(T, R_E]$ contains a zero ordinate so that $N_+(R_E) > N_+(T)$, one either reads ball membership directly from the certified node list while retaining exact $N_+(T)$ for BPT, or advances along the fixed $1/1024$-dyadic grid before fixing final ball metadata and precision, charging any extra count queries to $T_{\mathrm{nodes}}(P_{\mathrm{in}})$). Under pairwise distinct squared nodes (targets separated via `nodeEquiv` and `signSeparated`, and targets occupying distinct reflection classes from all **non-target** indices $E \setminus \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$ or $E_{\mathrm{aug}} \setminus \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$), merging reflection pairs $\pm w$ across targets and non-target zeros yields $s$ distinct squared nodes $u_\mu$ (with target squared-node labels denoted $\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{targ}}$), a degree cap $d_{\mathrm{poly}} := s - 1$ ($\deg P_i \le d_{\mathrm{poly}}$; the script certifies this cap without testing whether the leading monomial survives), and Lagrange cardinal polynomials $L_{i,\pm}(u) := \prod_{\nu \neq i\pm}\frac{u - u_\nu}{u_{i\pm} - u_\nu}$; among the listed squared interpolation nodes, the $i$-th killer polynomial $P_i(u) := \frac{L_{i,+}(u)}{\widehat{\psi_{h_E}}(z_i)} - \frac{L_{i,-}(u)}{\widehat{\psi_{h_E}}(\bar{z}_i)}$ is nonzero only at the two target nodes $u_{i+}, u_{i-}$, and we set $k_i := P_i(-\partial_x^2)\psi_{h_E}$. Without expanding monomials across orders of magnitude from $10^5$ to $10^{11}$ (specifically $100{,}170$ to $150{,}934{,}911{,}628$), Cauchy's derivative bound yields the exact-product upper budget $J_\ell(i)$ for the true killer $L^1$ norms $\lVert k_i^{(\ell)}\rVert_{L^1}$ ($\ell \in \{0, 2\}$, with $0^0 = 1$):

   $$
   \lVert k_i^{(\ell)}\rVert_{L^1} \;\le\; J_\ell(i) \;:=\; \left[\sum_{\mu \in \{i+, i-\}} 2\prod_{\nu \neq \mu} \frac{1 + \lvert u_\nu\rvert}{\lvert u_\mu - u_\nu\rvert}\right] \frac{2}{Z_1}\,(2d_{\mathrm{poly}} + \ell)!\left[\frac{4(2d_{\mathrm{poly}} + \ell)}{e h_E}\right]^{2d_{\mathrm{poly}} + \ell}.
   $$

   In the replay benchmark script, for every high node above $H_{\mathrm{cut}} = 1500$ satisfying $H_{\mathrm{cut}}^2 > \lvert u_\mu\rvert$ (satisfied for all target squared nodes across all nine benchmarks since $\max_{\mu \in \mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{targ}}}\lvert u_\mu\rvert \le 199.8005 < H_{\mathrm{cut}}^2 = 1500^2$), each factor $\frac{1 + \lvert u_\nu\rvert}{\lvert u_\mu - u_\nu\rvert}$ is replaced by the uniform upper factor $1 + \frac{1 + \lvert u_\mu\rvert}{H_{\mathrm{cut}}^2 - \lvert u_\mu\rvert}$ together with exact counts, producing certified replay budgets $J_\ell^{\mathrm{rep}}(i) \ge J_\ell(i)$ and replay majorant $C_{\mathrm{ub}}^{\mathrm{rep}} \ge C_{\mathrm{ub}}$ bounding the exact-product quantities from above.

   Writing $(G_{\mathrm{Weil}})_{ij} := \sum_n m_n\,\widehat{\mathrm{basis}_j}(\gamma_n)\,\overline{\widehat{\mathrm{basis}_i}(\bar{\gamma}_n)}$, whose target quadratic form `frameOddTargetQuadratic` is $-4\sum_{i\in\iota} m_i \lvert a_i\rvert^2$, over any hypothetical nonempty off-line Lean `ZeroData` frame the frozen theorems [`mixedWeilSummand_factorization`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/FiniteMixedWeilMajorant.mixedWeilSummand_factorization), [`finiteMixedMajorant_head_le`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/WeilMixedHeadTailBudget.finiteMixedMajorant_head_le), `finiteMixedMajorantTotal_le_head_tail`, [`fourthMomentSummand_le_inverse_fourth`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/WeilMixedHeadTailBudget.fourthMomentSummand_le_inverse_fourth), and [`burnol_actual_gram_uniform_remainder`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/WeilFullGramUniformRemainder.burnol_actual_gram_uniform_remainder) (and the same algebraic decomposition on the augmented test spectrum)—using exact `kills_exception` cancellation on $E \setminus \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$ (or $E_{\mathrm{aug}} \setminus \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$)—yield the **head–tail mixed majorant**

   $$
   C_{\mathrm{ub}} \;=\; 4\sum_{i \in \iota} m_i \;+\; \left(\sum_{i \in \iota} 3\bigl(J_0(i) + J_2(i)\bigr)\right)^2 \Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E),
   $$

   ensuring $\lvert\operatorname{Re}(a^* G_{\mathrm{Weil}} a) - (-4\sum_i m_i\lvert a_i\rvert^2)\rvert \le 4^{-(N+1)} C_{\mathrm{ub}}\sum_i \lvert a_i\rvert^2$ for all coefficient vectors $a$ (substituting $J_\ell^{\mathrm{rep}}(i)$ gives the replay upper bound $C_{\mathrm{ub}}^{\mathrm{rep}} \ge C_{\mathrm{ub}}$; setting $J_{\mathrm{tot}} := \sum_{i\in\iota} 3(J_0(i) + J_2(i))$, whenever targets obey $\lvert\eta_j\rvert \le \frac{1}{2}$ and the full-spectrum fourth-moment bound shares the same tail enclosure $S_{4,\mathrm{ub}} = S_4^{\mathrm{head}} + \Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E)$ (where the head fourth moment $S_4^{\mathrm{head}} := \sum_{n \in E} \frac{m_n}{(1 + (\operatorname{Re}\gamma_n)^2)^2}$ uses $E_{\mathrm{aug}}$ on the augmented test spectrum, including the target orbits $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$ in both cases), both bounds vanish for the empty frame $q = 0$, whereas for every nonempty frame $q \ge 1$ item 1's integration-by-parts bound together with $\widehat{k_j}(z_j) = 1$ forces $J_{\mathrm{tot}} \ge 3(J_0(j) + J_2(j)) \ge 3 e^{-1/2}(1 + \lvert z_j\rvert^2) > 1 + t_j^2$, hence $4\sum_{j\in\iota} m_j < J_{\mathrm{tot}}^2\sum_{j\in\iota}\frac{4 m_j}{(1 + t_j^2)^2} \le J_{\mathrm{tot}}^2 S_4^{\mathrm{head}}$ and algebraically $C_{\mathrm{ub}} < J_{\mathrm{tot}}^2 S_{4,\mathrm{ub}}$, strictly tighter than the full-spectrum bound of [`finiteMixedMajorantTotal_le_unit_support_jets`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/WeilMixedHeadTailBudget.finiteMixedMajorantTotal_le_unit_support_jets); note that the auxiliary diagnostic `log_plain` in the archived Issue #918 script plugged in genuine-only $S_4^{\mathrm{gen}}$ without adding $4\sum_i m_i/(1 + t_i^2)^2$ and was not used for any published head–tail majorant or depth; it is relabeled `log_plain_gen_only` alongside the like-for-like augmented diagnostic `log_plain_aug` in the addendum cited above). For the empty frame $q = 0$, $C_{\mathrm{ub}} = 0$ and depth $N = 0$ is taken directly. For $q \ge 1$, the Arb replay route takes the ceiling $K_{\mathrm{dyad}} := \lceil\operatorname{ub}(\log_2 C_{\mathrm{ub}}^{\mathrm{rep}})\rceil$ of the **upper endpoint** of the Arb enclosure of $\log_2 C_{\mathrm{ub}}^{\mathrm{rep}}$ ($C_{\mathrm{ub}}^{\mathrm{rep}} \ge C_{\mathrm{ub}} \ge 4$ ensures $K_{\mathrm{dyad}} \ge 2$; the arithmetic-only route below instead reads $K_{\mathrm{dyad}} := \max(0, \operatorname{bitlength}(M_{\mathrm{int}} - 1) - P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ directly from an outward fixed-point upper endpoint $U = M_{\mathrm{int}}/2^{P_{\mathrm{bit}}} \ge C_{\mathrm{ub}}$, and both routes yield valid dyadic majorants $c_{\mathrm{maj}}/d_{\mathrm{maj}} := 2^{K_{\mathrm{dyad}}} \ge C_{\mathrm{ub}}$); supplying $c_{\mathrm{maj}}/d_{\mathrm{maj}}$ and rational error tolerance $p_{\mathrm{err}}/q_{\mathrm{err}} \in (0, 4)$ (note that $q_{\mathrm{err}}$ is the tolerance denominator, distinct from the channel count $q = \lvert\iota\rvert$) to the frozen depth theorems [`rationalQuarterDepth_integer_sound`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/BurnolRationalDepthBudget.rationalQuarterDepth_integer_sound), [`rationalQuarterDepth_real_sound`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/BurnolRationalDepthBudget.rationalQuarterDepth_real_sound), [`rationalQuarterDepth_full_gram_margin`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/BurnolRationalDepthBudget.rationalQuarterDepth_full_gram_margin), and `rationalBurnol_support_and_margin` produces the executable integer depth $N_{\mathrm{UB}} := \mathrm{Nat.log}\ 4\,\lfloor c_{\mathrm{maj}} q_{\mathrm{err}} / (d_{\mathrm{maj}} p_{\mathrm{err}})\rfloor$ satisfying the strict tolerance test $4^{-(N+1)}(c_{\mathrm{maj}}/d_{\mathrm{maj}}) < p_{\mathrm{err}}/q_{\mathrm{err}}$ (with Lean's convention $\mathrm{Nat.log}\ 4\ 0 = 0$; at $p_{\mathrm{err}}/q_{\mathrm{err}} = 2^{-10}$ and $K_{\mathrm{dyad}} \ge 0$, this simplifies to $\lfloor(K_{\mathrm{dyad}} + 10)/2\rfloor$), certifying uniform negative Gram margin $\operatorname{Re}(a^* G_{\mathrm{Weil}} a) \le -(4 - p_{\mathrm{err}}/q_{\mathrm{err}})\lVert a\rVert_2^2$ and unit-support cap $R_N = N_{\mathrm{UB}} + 2$ (for our explicit smooth seeds, real-radius convolution support further tightens the cap to $(N_{\mathrm{UB}} + 1)h_0 + h_E$, including when $h_0$ is irrational). Crucially, letting $L_i \le \ln\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1} \le \ln J_0(i)$ denote the single-channel Jensen logarithmic lower bound of item 2 and defining the tail jet proxy by $C_{\mathrm{proxy}} := \bigl(\sum_{j\in\iota} 3(J_0(j) + J_2(j))\bigr)^2 \Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E) \ge 9 e^{2 L_i}\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E)$, the replay takes a certified integer floor of its logarithmic depth threshold $N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}} := \max\bigl(0,\; \lfloor\operatorname{lb}(\log_4(9 e^{2 L_i}\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E) q_{\mathrm{err}} / p_{\mathrm{err}}))\rfloor\bigr)$—a **lower bound on the minimum depth** required when certifying through this jet-majorant formula (rather than the exact minimum of that formula); because the actual spectral remainder can be smaller than the $L^1$-jet proxy, Jensen's bound does not invert into an unconditional lower bound on the true minimal localization depth.

   **Explicit conditional bit-cost audit (coarse-peak certificate route)**: The conservative bit-cost bound below applies specifically to the **coarse-peak certificate route**, proceeding in five strictly acyclic steps (with $N = 0$ taken directly for $q = 0$ as above; assume $q \ge 1$ below): step 1 acquires target-only guards—an integer target height guard $H_{\mathrm{targ}} \ge 2 + R_0$, target-only squared separation $2^{-b_{\mathrm{targ}}} \le \min(1, \lvert u_\mu - u_\nu\rvert)$ ($\mu \neq \nu \in \mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{targ}}$), and certified target multiplicities $m_i$ (assigned $m_i = 1$ directly on the unit-multiplicity augmented spectrum, or supplied/certified before step 4 on genuine frames); step 2 uses the rational seed radius $h_0^{\mathrm{rat}} := \frac{1}{4(H_{\mathrm{targ}} - 1)} \le \frac{1}{4(R_0 + 1)}$ and elementary rational bounds $Z_1 > \frac{1}{4}$, $e > 1$, and $\lvert\widehat{\psi_{h_0^{\mathrm{rat}}}}(w_\mu)\rvert^{-1} < 2$ to compute purely rational coarse peak jet upper bounds $J_{\ell,\mathrm{rat}}^{\mathrm{peak}} := 32 q \bigl((1 + H_{\mathrm{targ}}^2) 2^{b_{\mathrm{targ}}}\bigr)^{2q - 1} (4q - 2 + \ell)!\bigl(4(4q - 2 + \ell)/h_0^{\mathrm{rat}}\bigr)^{4q - 2 + \ell}$ ($\ell = 0, 2$), producing an initial candidate dyadic integer cutoff satisfying the BPT domain $R_E^{(0)} := \lceil\max\bigl(H_{\mathrm{targ}},\; 6(J_{0,\mathrm{rat}}^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_{2,\mathrm{rat}}^{\mathrm{peak}}) + 1,\; 7\bigr)\rceil$ before querying any ball zero, and performs any optional fixed-grid advance (such as advancing on the $1/1024$ grid with count queries for $N_+(R_E) = N_+(T)$) to pass the resulting final cutoff $R_E \ge R_E^{(0)}$ to step 3 (excluding the extra exact-rational polynomial root isolation and adaptive absolute-value quadrature used in the numerical benchmark script below to shrink $R_E$, whose cost is added separately when used); step 3 acquires ball membership at the final radius $R_E$, certified reflection equivalence classes or exact index pairings for constructing the distinct squared-node list, the node count $s$, the uniform direct/reflection/inter-orbit and target-to-killer squared separation $2^{-b_{\mathrm{sep}}} \le \min(1, \lvert u_\mu - u_\nu\rvert)$, and the exact count $N_+(T)$ required by BPT; step 4 computes the capacity guard $\Lambda$ and precisions $P_{\mathrm{bit}}, P_{\mathrm{in}}$ from the fixed $(R_E, s, b_{\mathrm{sep}})$ and certified target multiplicities $m_i$; step 5 refines all coordinate balls from the refinable interval oracle at guarded input precision $P_{\mathrm{in}}$ (the combined cost of step-1 guard and multiplicity certification, any step-2 optional grid advance and count queries, step-3 metadata acquisition, and step-5 coordinate refinement is charged together as $T_{\mathrm{nodes}}(P_{\mathrm{in}})$; height, off-line shift, and channel count alone cannot bound unenumerated spectral spacings or node acquisition). Let $H_0 \ge 2 + \max(R_E, R_0)$ be an integer height guardrail and set $M_{\mathrm{deg}} := 2(s + q + 1)$, splitting the core certification into a **fixed-capacity algebraic core** and **self-contained precision-controlled preprocessing subroutines**: the algebraic core counts strictly the elementary arithmetic operations and rational comparisons for the killer jets, majorant, and depth formula, with scalar operation count bound $L_{\mathrm{alg}}$ depending solely on combinatorial size and not on $B_{\mathrm{out}}$ or $P_{\mathrm{bit}}$ ($L_{\mathrm{alg}} = O(qs + s)$ in factored form, or $L_{\mathrm{alg}} = O(s^2 + qs + q^2)$ if full monomial coefficient arrays are expanded via $Q_{\mathrm{nod}}(u) := \prod_\mu (u - u_\mu)$ and synthetic division). To cover every intermediate magnitude bound $\lvert x\rvert \le 2^\Lambda$ and every actual nonzero divisor floor $\lvert d\rvert \ge 2^{-\Lambda}$ inside the algebraic core (including general target multiplicities $\sum_{i\in\iota} m_i$ on genuine frames, which specializes to $\sum_{i\in\iota} m_i = q$ on the unit-multiplicity augmented test spectrum), choose the explicit concrete integer dynamic capacity guard

   $$
   \Lambda \;:=\; 4(s + q + 1)\bigl(b_{\mathrm{sep}} + 2\lceil\log_2(H_0 + 1)\rceil + 2\lceil\log_2(M_{\mathrm{deg}} + 2)\rceil + 8\bigr) \;+\; \left\lceil\log_2\!\Bigl(1 + \sum_{i \in \iota} m_i\Bigr)\right\rceil \;+\; \lceil\log_2(p_{\mathrm{err}} + q_{\mathrm{err}} + 2)\rceil \;+\; 16 \;\in\; \mathbb{N}
   $$

   (adding $(N_{\mathrm{UB}} + 1)(\Lambda + 2)$ if $4^{N+1}$ or $(N+1)$-th synthesis powers are expanded). Putting $a := \lceil\log_2(H_0 + 1)\rceil$ and $b := \lceil\log_2(M_{\mathrm{deg}} + 2)\rceil$, evaluating products sequentially in a fixed order keeps every algebraic intermediate within $\lvert x\rvert \le 2^\Lambda$ (permitting signed differences or expanded coefficients that may be negative or zero, such as $L_{\mathrm{RvM}}(T) - N_+(T) \approx -0.464 < 0$ in benchmark 3) and every actual divisor within $\lvert d\rvert \ge 2^{-\Lambda}$: each Lagrange ratio obeys $\frac{1 + \lvert u_\nu\rvert}{\lvert u_\mu - u_\nu\rvert} \in [2^{-(2a + 1)}, 2^{2a + b_{\mathrm{sep}} + 1}]$, the factorial-times-derivative power at order $m = 2d_{\mathrm{poly}} + \ell \le 2s \le M_{\mathrm{deg}}$ stays within $m!(4m/(e h_E))^m \le 2^{m(a + 2b + 4)}$, summing $J_{\mathrm{tot}} = \sum_i 3(J_0(i) + J_2(i))$ and squaring leaves the exponent below $2(s - 1)(2a + b_{\mathrm{sep}} + 1) + 4s(a + 2b + 4) + 2b + 16 < 4(s + q + 1)(b_{\mathrm{sep}} + 2a + 2b + 8) + 16$; on the integer-cutoff route ($T > 6$), Trudgian's estimate gives the signed numerator bound $\lvert L_{\mathrm{RvM}}(T) - N_+(T)\rvert \le E_{\mathrm{Tr}}(T) < T^2 < 2^{2a}$, BPT's domination of the strictly positive reciprocal-fourth zero tail gives $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E) > 0$, and substituting $N_+(T) \ge 0$, $\ln T \le T$, and $T > 6$ into the displayed BPT formula directly gives $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E) < 0.02 < 1$, whence $J_{\mathrm{tot}}^2 \Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E) < J_{\mathrm{tot}}^2 < 2^\Lambda$, while the actual BPT divisors $6\pi, T, T^3, T^4, T^5, 150$, head term $4\sum_{i\in\iota} m_i$, and tolerance integers $p_{\mathrm{err}}, q_{\mathrm{err}}$ all respect the corresponding magnitude and divisor guards. Each elementary arithmetic step in the algebraic core amplifies absolute error by at most $2^{4\Lambda + 8}$, so algebraic-core fractional precision $P_{\mathrm{bit}} := B_{\mathrm{out}} + (4\Lambda + 8)(L_{\mathrm{alg}} + 1) + \lceil\log_2(L_{\mathrm{alg}} + 1)\rceil + 16$ suffices for output absolute precision $B_{\mathrm{out}} \ge \lceil\log_2(q_{\mathrm{err}}/p_{\mathrm{err}})\rceil$ (together with $\Lambda$ integer bits and sign, each algebraic fixed-point scalar has total width $W_{\mathrm{bit}} := \Lambda + P_{\mathrm{bit}} + 2 = O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$), guaranteeing final output absolute error $\le 2^{-B_{\mathrm{out}}}$ provided all external inputs enter the core with absolute error $\le 2^{-P_{\mathrm{bit}}}$. All three classes of non-rational inputs are delivered as certified balls by independent precision-controlled subroutines: (a) **square roots and complex moduli** ($R_0$, $T = \sqrt{R_E^2 - 1/4}$, and the $O(qs + s)$ moduli $\lvert u_\nu\rvert, \lvert u_\mu - u_\nu\rvert$): because forming $u_\mu = w_\mu^2$ and radicands $v = \lvert z\rvert^2 \in [2^{-2\Lambda}, 2^{2\Lambda}]$ amplifies coordinate errors by at most $O(H_0^3)$, coordinates are queried from the node oracle at guarded input precision $P_{\mathrm{in}} := P_{\mathrm{bit}} + 4\Lambda + O(\log P_{\mathrm{bit}}) = O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ (charged in $T_{\mathrm{nodes}}(P_{\mathrm{in}})$) and checked for relative width $v_+ - v_- \le \frac{1}{4} v_-$; for each positive radicand interval $[v_-, v_+]$, the power-of-$4$ scale $k := \lfloor\frac{1}{2}\lfloor\log_2 v_-\rfloor\rfloor$ is chosen directly from the binary exponent of the certified lower endpoint $v_-$ (so the relative-width check guarantees $4^{-k}[v_-, v_+] = [w_-, w_+] \subseteq [1, 5]$ without stalling at powers of $4$), and at internal working width $P_{\mathrm{sqrt}} = P_{\mathrm{bit}} + 2\Lambda + O(\log P_{\mathrm{bit}}) = O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ we initialize $u_0 := 3$, run $O(\log P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ outward Heron–Newton upper steps $u_{n+1} = \operatorname{up}\bigl(\frac{1}{2}(u_n + w_+/u_n)\bigr) \ge \sqrt{w_+}$, bracket below from the same upper sequence via $l_n = \operatorname{down}(w_-/u_n) \le \sqrt{w_-}$, and multiply by $2^k$, using $O(L_{\mathrm{alg}}\log P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ ball ops and outputting positive real certified balls inside $[2^{-\Lambda}, 2^\Lambda]$; (b) **transcendental constants and logarithms** ($\pi$, $e$, $\ln 2$, $\ln T$, and $\ln(2\pi)$, supplying both $\ln T$ and $\ln(T/(2\pi)) = \ln T - \ln(2\pi)$ inside BPT): evaluated with independent integer width $O(\log P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ and fractional width $P_{\mathrm{trans}} = P_{\mathrm{bit}} + O(\log P_{\mathrm{bit}}) = O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ by binary range reduction via midpoint exponent into $[\frac{7}{8}, \frac{9}{4}]$ (avoiding power-of-$2$ boundary stalls, with $y = \frac{x_0 - 1}{x_0 + 1}$ obeying $\lvert y\rvert \le \frac{5}{13} < \frac{1}{2}$) and $O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$-term convergent series for $\ln T$ and $\ln(2\pi)$ followed by guarded subtraction for $\ln(T/(2\pi))$ (unconstrained by the fixed algebraic guard $\Lambda$), using $O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ ball ops and outputting positive real certified balls inside $[2^{-\Lambda}, 2^\Lambda]$; (c) **smooth-bump quadrature** (unnormalized mass $Z_1 = 2\int_0^1 g_0(x)\,dx$, numerators $N_h(w) := 2\int_0^1 g_0(x)\cos(hwx)\,dx$, and $O(q)$ quotients $\widehat{\psi_h}(w) = N_h(w)/Z_1$, where $g_0(x) = e^{-1/(1-x^2)}$ and $h\lvert w\rvert \le \frac{1}{4}$): trims the exponentially flat endpoint tail $[1 - \varepsilon_{\mathrm{trim}}, 1]$ ($\varepsilon_{\mathrm{trim}} := \frac{1}{4(P_{\mathrm{bit}} + 16)}$, unnormalized tail integral $\le 4\varepsilon_{\mathrm{trim}} e^{-1/(2\varepsilon_{\mathrm{trim}})} < 2^{-(P_{\mathrm{bit}} + 16)}$) and splits $[0, 1 - \varepsilon_{\mathrm{trim}}]$ into $J = \lceil\log_{4/3}(1/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{trim}})\rceil = O(\log P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ clamped geometric subintervals $[a_j, a_{j+1}]$ ($a_0 := 0$, $a_{j+1} := \min\bigl(1 - \varepsilon_{\mathrm{trim}},\; 1 - \frac{3}{4}(1 - a_j)\bigr)$, midpoint $x_j$, half-width always obeying $r_j \le \frac{1}{7}(1 - x_j) \le \frac{1}{7}\delta_{x_j}$); defining the unnormalized scaled integrand $F_j(u) := g_0(x_j + r_j u)\cos(hw(x_j + r_j u))$ ($w = 0$ for $Z_1$) in the local variable $u = (x - x_j)/r_j$, the Bernstein ellipse $E_2$ ($\lvert u\rvert \le \frac{5}{4}$) obeys $\lvert r_j u\rvert < \frac{1}{2}\delta_{x_j}$ and $\lvert x_j + r_j u\rvert < 1$, so item 1's conformal-disk bound gives analyticity of $F_j(u)$ and $\lvert F_j(u)\rvert \le 2$ on $E_2$; taking an odd count of $M_Q = P_{\mathrm{bit}} + O(1)$ Clenshaw–Curtis nodes ($\theta_n = \frac{\pi n}{M_Q - 1}$, $0 \le n \le M_Q - 1$) integrates polynomials through degree $M_Q - 1$ exactly while odd Chebyshev modes vanish in both integral and symmetric quadrature, yielding alias error $\le \sum_{2k \ge M_Q + 1} 16\cdot 2^{-2k} = \frac{32}{3}\cdot 2^{-M_Q} < 16\cdot 2^{-M_Q}$ with positive weights $w_n^{\mathrm{CC}} > 0$ ($\sum_n w_n^{\mathrm{CC}} = 2$, precomputed once via complex-disk powering $e^{i 2m\theta_n}$ in $O(P_{\mathrm{bit}}^2)$ ball ops, where step radius $\varepsilon_0 \le 2^{-P_{\mathrm{quad}}}$ with $M_Q\varepsilon_0 \le \frac{1}{4}$ bounds modulus drift by $(1 + \varepsilon_0)^m < 2$ and caps radius amplification at $O(M_Q)$), while pointwise evaluation splits $1/(1 - x^2) = m_y + f_y$ via $m_y := \lfloor\operatorname{lb}(1/(1 - x^2))\rfloor = O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ into integer part (binary powering of $(e^{-1})^{m_y}$) and nonnegative fractional part $f_y \in [0, 2)$ (positive-term series for $1/e^{f_y}$, without integer-boundary stalls) plus an $O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$-term Taylor series for $\cos(hwx)$ ($\lvert hwx\rvert \le \frac{1}{4}$, term-modulus sum $\le \cosh(\frac{1}{4}) < 2$, giving an $O(P_{\mathrm{bit}}\cdot 2^{-P_{\mathrm{quad}}})$ absolute roundoff bound) in $O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ ball ops per node; thus internal width $P_{\mathrm{quad}} = P_{\mathrm{bit}} + O(\log P_{\mathrm{bit}}) = O(P_{\mathrm{bit}})$ executes in $O\bigl((1 + q)P_{\mathrm{bit}}^2\log P_{\mathrm{bit}}\bigr)$ ball ops total to compute $Z_1$ and $N_h(w)$ within error $\le 2^{-(P_{\mathrm{bit}} + 8)}$, and quotient propagation via $Z_1 \in (\frac{1}{4}, 1)$ and $\lvert N_h(w)\rvert < \frac{3}{2}$ returns $< 2^{-P_{\mathrm{bit}}}$-accurate certified balls—a positive real enclosure $Z_1 \in [2^{-\Lambda}, 2^\Lambda]$ and certified **complex** balls for $\widehat{\psi_h}(w)$ obeying $2^{-\Lambda} \le \lvert v\rvert \le 2^\Lambda$ (since $\frac{1}{2} < \lvert v\rvert < \frac{3}{2}$). Finally, for any outward enclosure $[l, u]$, the safe integer ceiling is taken directly as $\lceil u\rceil$, and both $K_{\mathrm{dyad}}$ and $N_{\mathrm{UB}} = \mathrm{Nat.log}\ 4\,\lfloor 2^{K_{\mathrm{dyad}}} q_{\mathrm{err}} / p_{\mathrm{err}}\rfloor$ are computed purely from integer bit-lengths and integer division in $O(P_{\mathrm{bit}}^2)$ bit ops without taking a transcendental logarithm of $C_{\mathrm{ub}}$. Under schoolbook $O(P_{\mathrm{bit}}^2)$ bit multiplication, the conservative conditional bit cost for the coarse-peak certificate route is **$T_{\mathrm{nodes}}(P_{\mathrm{in}}) + O\bigl((L_{\mathrm{alg}} + (1 + q) P_{\mathrm{bit}}^2)P_{\mathrm{bit}}^2\log P_{\mathrm{bit}}\bigr)$** (excluding any separately requested dense Gram or spatial-grid evaluations). Regarding output bit length, item 2's Jensen bound forces any *explicitly written positive rational norm upper bound* $p_{\mathrm{norm}}/q_{\mathrm{norm}} \ge \lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1}$ (integer $q_{\mathrm{norm}} \ge 1$) to have numerator bit-length $\Omega(R_E \ln R_E \ln\ln R_E)$, whereas exponent-only dyadic encodings (storing $K_{\mathrm{dyad}}$), factored lists, or evaluation circuits do not incur that explicit numerator bit-length.

4. **256 / 320-bit Arb benchmark verification and small-window critical bandwidth crossover (`NUM`)**: Independent 256-bit and 320-bit Arb runs (`python-flint 0.9.0`, including the `analytic=analytic` addendum replay cited above verifying complex-logarithm analyticity in `density_jensen`, where all $96$ Jensen balls overlap the original archive) agree identically across all nine hypothetical target sets on every certified dyadic radius $R_E$, exact two-sided zero count $2N_+$, degree cap $d_{\mathrm{poly}}$, integer depths $N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$ and $N_{\mathrm{UB}}$, and support cap $R_N = N_{\mathrm{UB}} + 2$, with all nine radii ($R_E \le 4.38\times 10^{10}$) falling inside the Platt–Trudgian (2021, Theorem 1 and §2) certified simple-zero range $3\times 10^{12}$. Table 1 summarizes all nine augmented test-spectrum benchmarks ($p_{\mathrm{err}}/q_{\mathrm{err}} = 2^{-10}$, unit hypothetical multiplicities $m_i = 1$, exact dyadic $R_E$ rounded up to $1/1024$; the printed killer-jet, majorant, and depth upper-bound $N_{\mathrm{UB}}$ columns are certified via the high-node-majorized replay upper bounds $J_\ell^{\mathrm{rep}}(i) \ge J_\ell(i)$ and $C_{\mathrm{ub}}^{\mathrm{rep}} \ge C_{\mathrm{ub}}$ of item 3, whereas the depth lower bound $N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$ is computed from item 2's Jensen logarithmic lower bound $L_i$ and $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_E)$; **note that the exact dyadic cutoffs $R_E$ and exact integer depths $N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}, N_{\mathrm{UB}}$ are computed directly from the tighter 256-bit Arb enclosures archived in Issue #918, rather than by re-substituting the coarsely outward-rounded 5–8 significant-digit peak-jet and majorant display summaries printed in the table**; every one-sided upper-bound column is outward-rounded upward and the Jensen lower-bound column as well as the $q = 2$ entries of $N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$ are outward-rounded downward, so every printed one-sided bound decimal is a strict certified bound, while hypothetical target nodes, $R_E$, $2N_+$, and single-channel depths are exact; the three $q = 2$ rows use channels $(1, 0.1)$ and $(1 + \delta, 0.1)$, with killer columns showing bounds valid simultaneously for both channels; Arb balls formatted to up to 56 digits and per-channel logs appear in Issue #918):

Two striking phenomena predicted by items 1–2 stand out immediately: first, as $(1, \eta)$ shrinks from $\eta = 0.25$ to $0.05$ and $0.01$, the signed lower bound $B_{\min}$ climbs from $\approx 3.959$ to $\approx 19.99$ and $\approx 100.0$ (rounded from $\approx 99.9983$) and the exact signed $L^2$ minimum climbs from $\approx 9.315$ to $\approx 46.65$ and $\approx 233.3$, yet the equal-target peak stays uniformly bounded ($J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} \approx 1.525$, $J_2^{\mathrm{peak}} \approx (1.25\text{–}1.29)\times 10^4$, $R_E \approx (7.50\text{–}7.73)\times 10^4$) via the $\eta \to 0$ cancellation—while the $> 200{,}000$ Platt–Trudgian-certified genuine critical-line zeros inside $E_{\mathrm{aug}}$ still drive the killer norm $\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1}$ past $10^{114{,}356}$; second, for $q = 2$ as $\delta$ drops from $1$ to $0.1$ and $0.01$, the joint four-moment signed $L^2$ minima jump from $\approx (13{,}223.5,\ 7{,}420.6)$ to $\approx (693{,}080.8,\ 450{,}291.0)$ and $\approx (2{,}267{,}509.4,\ 644{,}926.7)$, yet the equal-target radius $R_E$ drops from $\approx 4.377\times 10^{10}$ to $\approx 2.150\times 10^{10}$ (a $50.9\%$ drop) and $\approx 1.971\times 10^{10}$ (a $55.0\%$ overall drop from $\delta = 1$) because Newton divided differences supply a uniform bound through the $\delta \to 0$ collision (preventing coefficient blow-up) while smaller $R_0 = \sqrt{(1+\delta)^2 + 0.1^2}$ enlarges $h_0 = \frac{1}{4(R_0 + 1)}$ and reduces the derivative-scaling factors $h_0^{-2m-2} B_{2m+2}$ enough to outweigh the mild growth of the nonconstant peak polynomial coefficients.

   Table 2 checks the fourth-moment tail sharpness and the transition of the critical bandwidth $A_{\mathrm{crit}}$ across unit support $A = 1$ at center $z_1 = 1 + 0.25i$ over small windows $R_{\mathrm{win}} \in \{50, 100, 200, 403, 500, 1000\}$ ($\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}$ outward-rounded upward; the actual fourth-moment tail column lists midpoints rounded to six significant digits—the source Arb balls each have radius $< 10^{-14}$, while six-digit rounding introduces deviations of at most $3.22\times 10^{-12}$, $1.59\times 10^{-13}$, and $3.64\times 10^{-14}$ in the first three rows, with unrounded balls in Issue #918; optimized Jensen $\log_{10}\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1}$ outward-rounded downward; and $A_{\mathrm{crit}}$ rounded to four decimals from active-interval Arb bisection certified to $10^{-32}$):
