   | $403$ | $408$ | $7.2611\times 10^{-9}$ | $7.25766\times 10^{-9}$ | $30.3235$ | $1.1866$ |
   | $500$ | $538$ | $4.0181\times 10^{-9}$ | $4.01676\times 10^{-9}$ | $58.3416$ | $1.2836$ |
   | $1000$ | $1{,}298$ | $5.7258\times 10^{-10}$ | $5.72530\times 10^{-10}$ | $267.8721$ | $1.6083$ |

   For $R_{\mathrm{win}} \in \{50, 100, 200\}$, $A_{\mathrm{crit}}(R_{\mathrm{win}}) < 1$ and active-interval Arb enumeration independently confirms that the optimal Jensen bound $-0.25/\ln 10 \approx -0.1086$ is attained across the entire zero-free disk plateau $0 < \rho \le 0.25$ (where $S(\rho) = 0$ and $I(\rho, 0.25) \equiv 0.25$); because enlarging the prescribed set $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{pres}}$ makes $S(\rho)$ and hence $A_{\mathrm{crit}}$ monotonically nondecreasing, $A_{\mathrm{crit}}(R_{\mathrm{win}})$ uniquely first crosses unit support $A = 1$ in $R_{\mathrm{win}} \in (200, 403)$ (an independent Arb check calling the same `critical_bandwidth` routine from Issue #918 on the $116$- and $117$-zero prefixes locates the crossing at the $117$-th positive zero, $R_{\mathrm{win}} \approx 265.56$, where $A_{\mathrm{crit}}$ jumps from $\approx 0.9976$ to $\approx 1.0003$), after which the positive-radius zero-density term dominates and drives the $\log_{10}\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1}$ lower bound to $30.32$, $58.34$, and $267.87$ (the Issue #918 log separately reports that the coarse stationary threshold $\frac{\pi M_E}{2} > R_{\mathrm{win}} + \lvert z_1\rvert$ first crosses at $R_{\mathrm{win}} \approx 121.3701$ and holds permanently from $R_{\mathrm{win}} \approx 122.9468$; by contrast, for a center right next to the first critical-line zero such as $z = 14.134725 + 0.01i$, that single nearby zero already forces $A_{\mathrm{crit}} \approx 54.4763$ and $\log_{10}\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1} \ge 1.7618$ even at $R_{\mathrm{win}} = 50$).
:::

::: {.remark #rem-ch34-spectral-tail-bypass-and-pw-transition title="Square-Root Tightening of the Two-Jet Cutoff, Zero-Killing-Free Exponential-Leverage Spectral-Tail Packets, Cosine-Product Growing-Bandwidth Killers, and Tight Actual-Remainder Depth (Manual Derivation and 256/320-Bit Arb Verification)"}
**Provenance and verification status.** This remark directly advances the first and second open targets of [@prob-ch34-signed-orbit-joint-budget], retaining all frozen interfaces cited in [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] (in particular [`peak_tail_of_two_jet_budget`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/UnitSupportBurnolPacket.peak_tail_of_two_jet_budget), [`zero_transform_pair_le_three_jets`](lean:D5/S3/Weil/BurnolGram/WeilMixedHeadTailBudget.zero_transform_pair_le_three_jets), and `burnolBasis`) together with the same augmented test-spectrum audit regime—strictly **without invoking** the externally verified classical Riemann Hypothesis ([@conj-riemann-hypothesis], `riemannZeta_eq_zero_re : RiemannZetaZerosOnLine`, under which any genuine off-line orbit frame is empty, $q = 0$); every critical-line-background certificate relying on real-axis tail estimates or full surviving-spectrum positivity (item 2's $\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} = 0$ columns $A_{\mathrm{2\text{-}jet}}$ and $A_{\mathrm{spec}}$, item 3's cosine-product full-spectrum certificate, item 4's Table 4 real-$D$ column, and Table 5's critical-line-background $N_{\mathrm{actual}}$ sandwich) explicitly adopts "all genuine non-target background zeros at all heights lie on the critical line" as a model assumption (finite-height head zeros are unconditionally certified via Platt–Trudgian (2021) and FLINT isolation, while item 1, item 2's strip-dominance majorant, and item 4's full-strip $N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}}$ do not rely on this assumption). All independent written derivations and dual-precision ($256$-bit / $320$-bit, `python-flint 0.9.0`, with adaptive escalation up to $4{,}352\text{–}4{,}864$ bits for ill-conditioned Paley–Wiener Gram matrices) FLINT/Arb interval replays below are not formalized in `trureturing` (`Manual derivation` / `NUM`; verification summary, 7-file pinned public Gist revision—containing the four Python replay and verification scripts, `SHA256SUMS`, `crosscheck.log`, and `summary_tables.md`, whose Cycle 8 baseline inputs also agree with the [Issue #918 256-bit Arb log](https://github.com/the-omega-institute/trureturing-books/issues/918#issuecomment-7074665300)—and the SHA-256 manifest including the run-log digests are archived in [Issue #997 verification summary](https://github.com/the-omega-institute/trureturing-books/issues/997#issuecomment-7081682848), [scripts and SHA-256 archive](https://github.com/the-omega-institute/trureturing-books/issues/997#issuecomment-7081752699), and [pinned Gist revision](https://gist.github.com/loning/ca6343e3d31337185e885e36d5fb9a59/543feda0efcd07addb0879087d3aa7141304ffd1); the two precision replays achieve $100\%$ Arb ball overlap across all $3{,}142 + 152$ balls and $100\%$ identity across all $607 + 93$ integers; core SHA-256 digests are `verify_cycle10.py` `a8132255aafb5d9170930257dad5d500598e7db8b21d34d6b414caec1a2c6360`, `run_256.log` `cc2e8384bcec0d066a181deaed9dd1d135a53345e644faac467c224b2437c377`, and `run_320.log` `6f449dd8f1eb2152b62741d8cc39f1042b541ec8ebf02833f6ebcc3aaa9e791d`). Four core results follow:

1. **Written square-root tightening of the two-jet half-value cutoff hypothesis (for fixed-$q$ Newton-budget peaks, unconditionally reducing $R_E$ from $\Omega(T_{\max}^2)$ to $\Theta_q(1 + T_{\max})$)**: Inspecting the frozen theorem `peak_tail_of_two_jet_budget` shows that for an even peak $b$ meeting the smoothness and compact-support premises of `WeilTestFunction` with $\operatorname{supp} b \subseteq [-1, 1]$, $\lVert b\rVert_{L^1} \le J_0^{\mathrm{peak}}$, and $\lVert b''\rVert_{L^1} \le J_2^{\mathrm{peak}}$ (setting $D := 3(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}})$), Lean assumes the linear hypothesis $R \ge 2D + 1$, uses $\lvert\gamma_n\rvert > R$ to obtain $(2D + 1)^2 < \lvert\gamma_n\rvert^2$, proves the denominator inequality $2D \le 1 + (\operatorname{Re}\gamma_n)^2$, and then applies `zero_transform_pair_le_three_jets` over genuine `ZeroData` nontrivial zeros in the half-strip $\lvert\operatorname{Im}\gamma_n\rvert \le \frac{1}{2}$ to the transform envelope $\lvert\widehat{b}(\gamma_n)\rvert,\; \lvert\widehat{b}(\bar{\gamma}_n)\rvert \le \frac{D}{1 + (\operatorname{Re}\gamma_n)^2}$ to conclude $\le \frac{1}{2}$ (and the frozen Lean declaration itself still requires $R \ge 2D + 1$). Tightening the sufficient cutoff hypothesis in writing along the very same envelope, for every exterior node in the half-strip $\lvert\operatorname{Im}\gamma_n\rvert \le \frac{1}{2}$ with $\lvert\gamma_n\rvert > R$ (both genuine `ZeroData` zeros and any adjoined half-strip test nodes) we have $1 + (\operatorname{Re}\gamma_n)^2 = 1 + \lvert\gamma_n\rvert^2 - (\operatorname{Im}\gamma_n)^2 > R^2 + \frac{3}{4}$, yielding **two unconditional square-root sufficient half-value cutoff radii requiring no extra hypotheses on arbitrary `ZeroData` or half-strip adjoined nodes**:

   $$
   R_E^{\mathrm{sqrt}} \;:=\; \sqrt{\max\!\left(0,\; 6\bigl(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}}\bigr) - \frac{3}{4}\right)}, \qquad R_E^{\mathrm{cosh}} \;:=\; \sqrt{\max\!\left(0,\; 2\cosh\!\left(\tfrac{1}{2}\right)\!\bigl(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}}\bigr) - \frac{3}{4}\right)},
   $$

   where the second radius replaces the frozen constant $3$ by the exact even cosine envelope on $[-1, 1]$ across $\lvert\operatorname{Im} z\rvert \le \frac{1}{2}$, $\lvert\cos(zx)\rvert = \sqrt{\cos^2(tx) + \sinh^2(\eta x)} \le \cosh(\frac{1}{2}) < 1.1277$ (paired with two integrations by parts, this even yields $(1 + \lvert z\rvert^2)\lvert\widehat{b}(z)\rvert \le \cosh(\frac{1}{2})(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}})$, so the $\cosh$ cutoff could already replace $-\frac{3}{4}$ by $-1$ inside the square root; Table 1 tests both formulas under the common real-part denominator $R^2 + \frac{3}{4}$, with large-budget limiting ratio $\sqrt{3/\cosh(\frac{1}{2})} \approx 1.6311$). For nonempty targets ($q \ge 1$, $\lvert\eta_i\rvert \le \frac{1}{2}$), target normalization unconditionally forces $3(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}}) \ge 1 + T_{\max}^2$, yielding the lower bound $R_E^{\mathrm{sqrt}} \ge \sqrt{2 T_{\max}^2 + \frac{5}{4}}$ for every valid budget; combining the half-strip geometry $T_{\max} \le R_0 \le \sqrt{T_{\max}^2 + \frac{1}{4}}$ with the fixed-$q$ Newton divided-difference peak construction of item 1 in [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] using budgets chosen from its derivative estimates ($J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}} = O_q((1 + R_0)^2)$; arbitrarily inflated valid upper budgets can produce arbitrarily larger cutoff radii) gives uniformly $R_E^{\mathrm{sqrt}} = \Theta_q(1 + T_{\max})$ (hence as $T_{\max} \to \infty$, $\Theta_q(T_{\max})$, while growing $q$ retains the $q$-dependence of the upper bound)—proving that the quadratic inflation $R_E \ge 2 T_{\max}^2 + 3$ in [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] came purely from the stronger linear sufficient hypothesis inside the frozen lemma. Nevertheless, as long as unit-support pointwise killing (`kills_exception`) is retained with exact unit signed readout, $\lvert z_i\rvert = O(R_E)$, and all eligible simple critical-line zeros in the ball preserved (off-line target deletions cannot remove critical-line simple zeros even if $q$ grows), Bui–Conrey–Young (2011, Theorem 1.1) yields effective killing weight $M_E \ge (\frac{0.4058}{\pi} - o(1))R_E\ln R_E$, so substituting $R_E^{\mathrm{sqrt}} \ge \sqrt{2}\,T_{\max}$ still leaves an unconditional Jensen super-exponential wall along $T_{\max} \to \infty$ lowered by one power: $\ln\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1} \ge \bigl(\frac{0.4058\sqrt{2}}{\pi} - o(1)\bigr) T_{\max}\ln T_{\max}\ln\ln T_{\max}$ (when Hermite weights or certified simplicity hold in the ball with discarded genuine mass $o(R_E\ln R_E)$—as holds automatically for fixed $q$ by local zero-count bounds—the asymptotic leading constant rises to $\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\pi}$).

   Table 1 audits these square-root sufficient radii across all nine augmented test-spectrum benchmarks of [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] (reusing the exact same explicit smooth-bump peaks $(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}}, J_2^{\mathrm{peak}})$; $R_E^{\mathrm{sqrt}}$ and $R_E^{\mathrm{cosh}}$ outward-rounded upward, two-sided zero counts exact integers, collapse ratios rounded midpoints, and Jensen lower bounds outward-rounded downward; the three $q = 2$ rows show common lower bounds valid for both channels):

   | Hypothetical target | Square-root sufficient radius $R_E^{\mathrm{sqrt}}$ (UB) | $\cosh$ radius $R_E^{\mathrm{cosh}}$ (UB) | Two-sided zeros $2N_+$ (old $\to$ sqrt $\to \cosh$) | Zero-count collapse (old / sqrt) | Jensen $\log_{10}\lVert k_i\rVert_{L^1}$ at sqrt (LB) |
   | :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
   | $(1,\ 0.25)$ | $278.1084$ | $170.5032$ | $207{,}256 \to 248 \to 128$ | $835.71$ | $2.0519$ |
   | $(1,\ 0.05)$ | $274.0837$ | $168.0357$ | $200{,}604 \to 244 \to 124$ | $822.15$ | $1.4680$ |
   | $(1,\ 0.01)$ | $273.9203$ | $167.9355$ | $200{,}338 \to 244 \to 124$ | $821.06$ | $1.4680$ |
   | $(5,\ 0.1)$ | $822.9732$ | $504.5535$ | $2{,}282{,}632 \to 1{,}018 \to 546$ | $2{,}242.27$ | $184.3498$ |
   | $(14.134725,\ 0.1)$ | $2{,}076.7568$ | $1{,}273.2318$ | $17{,}077{,}164 \to 3{,}174 \to 1{,}748$ | $5{,}380.33$ | $928.4522$ |
   | $(14.134725,\ 0.01)$ | $2{,}076.7092$ | $1{,}273.2027$ | $17{,}076{,}320 \to 3{,}174 \to 1{,}748$ | $5{,}380.06$ | $929.4524$ |
   | $q=2,\ \delta=1$ | $209{,}223.7735$ | $128{,}272.3163$ | $301{,}869{,}823{,}250 \to 626{,}908 \to 364{,}372$ | $481{,}521.73$ | $392{,}796.6$ |
   | $q=2,\ \delta=0.1$ | $146{,}628.8987$ | $89{,}896.2300$ | $143{,}398{,}521{,}320 \to 422{,}760 \to 245{,}188$ | $339{,}196.05$ | $257{,}050.0$ |
   | $q=2,\ \delta=0.01$ | $140{,}385.7207$ | $86{,}068.6205$ | $130{,}901{,}301{,}708 \to 402{,}816 \to 233{,}558$ | $324{,}965.50$ | $243{,}987.7$ |

   Going one step further at low height $(1, 0.25)$, replacing the narrow $h_0$-bump by the full closed-unit-support smooth polynomial bump peak $b_{\mathrm{opt}}(x) := \psi_1(x)(c_0 + c_1 x^2) \in C_c^\infty(\mathbb{R})_{\mathrm{even}}$ ($\operatorname{supp} b_{\mathrm{opt}} = [-1, 1]$; the exact $2\times 2$ linear-system solution yields exact equal-target readout, rounded as $c_0 \approx 1.918541895,\; c_1 \approx -5.818638261$, with $320$-bit Arb readout residual $< 2.65\times 10^{-88}$ and $256$-bit Arb readout residual $< 1.95\times 10^{-71}$) certifies via rigorous interval quadrature $J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} \le 1.263123$ and $J_2^{\mathrm{peak}} \le 26.107576$ (sum $J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}} \le 27.370698$), pushing even the frozen-constant-$3$ square-root cutoff down to **$R_E^{\mathrm{sqrt}} \le 12.785703 < \gamma_1 \approx 14.13472514$**: the exceptional ball contains **literally $0$ genuine nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros** (whereas the linear hypothesis of `peak_tail_of_two_jet_budget` previously triggered $207{,}256$ zeros and $\lVert k_1\rVert_{L^1} \ge 10^{118{,}669}$); meanwhile, for the original peaks in the first three rows of Table 1 under the square-root cutoff, the critical bandwidths already drop to $A_{\mathrm{crit}} \approx 1.0192, 1.0140, 1.0140$ (whereas the optimized peak $b_{\mathrm{opt}}$ has an empty genuine window and hence $A_{\mathrm{crit}} = 0$).

2. **Zero-killing-free exponential-leverage spectral-tail packets and unconditional strip-dominance theorem (direct packets with zero peak-convolution factors, avoiding critical-line pointwise killing)**: The direct escape from the pointwise-killing bottleneck is that the **direct packets with zero peak-convolution factors** constructed below (distinct from `burnolBasis` indexing, where $N = 0$ still applies one peak convolution) certify negative Weil Gram margin $\operatorname{Re}(a^* G_{\mathrm{Weil}} a) \le -(4\min_j m_j - \varepsilon)\lVert a\rVert_2^2$ without forcing any critical-line zero to vanish exactly. Fix bandwidth $A > 0$, smooth bump half-width $w \in (0, A/2]$, and center $c := A - w \ge w$; from the normalized bump $\psi_w(x) = w^{-1}\psi_1(x/w)$ of [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] ($\lVert\psi_w\rVert_{L^1} = 1$, $\lVert\psi_w'\rVert_{L^1} = B_1/w$, $\lVert\psi_w''\rVert_{L^1} = B_2/w^2$, where $B_1 = \frac{2}{e Z_1} \approx 1.657137680$ and $B_2 \approx 7.193161010$), form the symmetric and antisymmetric shifted bump pairs $b_{c,w}(x) := \frac{1}{2}\bigl(\psi_w(x - c) + \psi_w(x + c)\bigr)$ and $\tilde{b}_{c,w}(x) := \frac{1}{2}\bigl(\psi_w(x - c) - \psi_w(x + c)\bigr)$. For $q \ge 1$ pairwise distinct first-quadrant target orbits $z_r = t_r + i\eta_r$ ($t_r > 0,\; \eta_r > 0$, $\eta_{\min} := \min_r \eta_r$), define the real even target representers $P_j(x) := \cos(t_j x)\cosh(\eta_j x)$ and $Q_j(x) := \sin(t_j x)\sinh(\eta_j x)$. For the single-channel analysis ($q = 1$) and the numerically verified invertible benchmarks in Table 2 below (note that if two distinct orbits share $t_1 = t_2 > 0$ with $\eta_1 \neq \eta_2$, the unweighted basis columns repeat and become singular, so the general multi-channel theorem uses the weighted Gram basis below), form the $2q$ real even smooth basis functions $f_{r,1}(x) := b_{c,w}(x)\cos(t_r x)$ and $f_{r,2}(x) := \tilde{b}_{c,w}(x)\sin(t_r x) \in C_c^\infty([-A, A]; \mathbb{R})_{\mathrm{even}}$ (whose Fourier–Laplace transforms equal $\frac{1}{2}[\cos(c(u - t_r))\widehat{\psi_w}(u - t_r) + \cos(c(u + t_r))\widehat{\psi_w}(u + t_r)]$ and $\frac{1}{2}[\sin(c(u + t_r))\widehat{\psi_w}(u + t_r) - \sin(c(u - t_r))\widehat{\psi_w}(u - t_r)]$), and set the purely imaginary even channel packets $g_i(x) := i h_i(x) = i\sum_{r=1}^q\bigl(\alpha_{i,r} f_{r,1}(x) + \beta_{i,r} f_{r,2}(x)\bigr)$ with real coefficients solving $\operatorname{Re}\widehat{h_i}(z_j) = 0$ and $\operatorname{Im}\widehat{h_i}(z_j) = -\delta_{ij}$. By realness and evenness of $h_i$, $\widehat{g_i}(z_j) = \delta_{ij}$ automatically implies the opposite-sign conjugate readout $\widehat{g_i}(\bar{z}_j) = -\delta_{ij}$; Leibniz's rule gives **basis** $L^1$ and two-jet constants independent of $A$ (at fixed $w, t_r$), which multiplied by the $A$-dependent solved coefficients $(\alpha_{i,r}, \beta_{i,r})$ yield the closed-form channel two-jet upper bounds

   $$
   \lVert g_i\rVert_{L^1} \;\le\; \sum_{r=1}^q\bigl(\lvert\alpha_{i,r}\rvert + \lvert\beta_{i,r}\rvert\bigr), \qquad M_i \;:=\; \lVert g_i\rVert_{L^1} + \lVert g_i''\rVert_{L^1} \;\le\; \sum_{r=1}^q\bigl(\lvert\alpha_{i,r}\rvert + \lvert\beta_{i,r}\rvert\bigr)\!\left(1 + t_r^2 + \frac{2 t_r B_1}{w} + \frac{B_2}{w^2}\right).
   $$

   For $q = 1$, set $\mu_0 := \widehat{\psi_w}(i\eta_1) \ge 1$ and $\mu_2 := \widehat{\psi_w}(2t_1 + i\eta_1)$; since $t_1 > 0$ implies $\lvert\cos(2t_1 x)\cosh(\eta_1 x) - i\sin(2t_1 x)\sinh(\eta_1 x)\rvert = \sqrt{\cosh^2(\eta_1 x) - \sin^2(2t_1 x)} < \cosh(\eta_1 x)$ almost everywhere, one has $\lvert\mu_2\rvert < \mu_0$. Expanding the real Jacobian determinant $\Delta_{12} := \operatorname{Im}\bigl(\overline{\widehat{f_{1,1}}(z_1)}\widehat{f_{1,2}}(z_1)\bigr)$ directly gives the exact identity $\Delta_{12} = -\frac{\mu_0^2 - \lvert\mu_2\rvert^2}{4}\cosh(c\eta_1)\sinh(c\eta_1) + \frac{\mu_0}{4}\sin(2ct_1)\operatorname{Im}\mu_2 = -\frac{1}{16} e^{2c\eta_1}(\mu_0^2 - \lvert\mu_2\rvert^2) + O(1)$: as $c\eta_1$ grows, the leading exponential oscillatory cross terms cancel identically, leaving only a bounded $O(1)$ oscillatory term, whence $\lvert\alpha_{1,1}\rvert + \lvert\beta_{1,1}\rvert = O_{t_1,\eta_1,w}(e^{-c\eta_1})$; moreover, at fixed $\eta_1 > 0$ and $w > 0$ as $t_1 \to \infty$, $\mu_0 \ge 1$ is independent of $t_1$ while two integrations by parts give $\lvert\mu_2\rvert \le \frac{\cosh(w\eta_1)B_2}{4w^2 t_1^2} \to 0$, so $\mu_0^2 - \lvert\mu_2\rvert^2$ stays uniformly bounded away from zero and $M_1 = O_{\eta_1,w}((1 + t_1^2)e^{-A\eta_1})$. For general multi-channel frames $q \ge 1$, use the cosh-normalized weighted even basis $\Phi_r^+(x) := b_{c,w}(x)\cos(t_r x)\frac{\cosh(\eta_r x)}{\cosh(\eta_r c)}$ and $\Phi_r^-(x) := b_{c,w}(x)\sin(t_r x)\frac{\sinh(\eta_r x)}{\cosh(\eta_r c)}$: because every real even $h$ obeys $\widehat{h}(z_j) = \langle P_j, h\rangle - i\langle Q_j, h\rangle$, arranging the $2q\times 2q$ target evaluation matrix in $(\operatorname{Re}, -\operatorname{Im})$ row order and scaling each $j$-th row pair by $\frac{1}{\cosh(\eta_j c)}$ produces the symmetric positive-definite Gram matrix $\widetilde{G}(c)$ in $L^2(\mathbb{R},\, b_{c,w}(x)\,dx)$ (with scaled right-hand side having positive entry $+1/\cosh(\eta_i c)$ in the $Q_i$ row). By evenness, the two half-weight shifted bumps combine into a single integral against $\psi_w(y)\,dy$ on $[-w, w]$ ($x = c + y$); applying the block-diagonal orthogonal carrier rotation $\mathcal{R}(c) := \operatorname{diag}(R(t_1 c), \dots, R(t_q c))$ ($R(t_j c) := \begin{pmatrix} \cos(t_j c) & \sin(t_j c) \\ -\sin(t_j c) & \cos(t_j c) \end{pmatrix}$), as $c \to \infty$, $\mathcal{R}(c)\widetilde{G}(c)\mathcal{R}(c)^\top$ converges to the $c$-independent limiting Gram matrix $G_\infty$ of the $2q$ functions $\{e^{\eta_j y}\cos(t_j y),\; e^{\eta_j y}\sin(t_j y)\}_{j=1}^q$ in $L^2([-w, w], \psi_w(y)\,dy)$. Because the $2q$ complex exponents $\eta_j \pm it_j$ are pairwise distinct, $G_\infty \succ 0$ is strictly positive definite, so for all sufficiently large $c$, $\lVert\widetilde{G}(c)^{-1}\rVert_2$ stays uniformly bounded while the right-hand side decays as $1/\cosh(\eta_i c) \sim 2e^{-\eta_i c}$; moreover, on the shifted bump supports $x = \pm(c + y)$ ($y \in [-w, w]$), the scaled hyperbolic factors $\frac{\cosh(\eta_r x)}{\cosh(\eta_r c)}$, $\frac{\sinh(\eta_r x)}{\cosh(\eta_r c)}$, and their first two derivatives are uniformly bounded independently of $c$, forcing $M_i = O_{z,w}(e^{-\eta_i A})$ for every channel of a fixed frame.

   **Unconditional strip-dominance theorem and two-jet certificate floor**: Write $S_4^{\mathrm{rem}} := \sum_{n \notin \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}} \frac{m_n}{(1 + (\operatorname{Re}\gamma_n)^2)^2} < \infty$ for the finite non-target fourth moment (whenever all surviving non-target zeros are genuine nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros, $S_4^{\mathrm{rem}} \le S_4^{\mathrm{gen}} \in [7.377245548\times 10^{-5} \pm 8.41\times 10^{-15}]$ is unconditionally certified in [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget]; any surviving artificial non-target nodes outside $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$ add their own fourth-moment terms and require a fixed finite upper budget $S_4^{\mathrm{rem}} \le \overline{S}_4 < \infty$). Whenever all non-target zeros stay inside a horizontal strip strictly narrower than the targets, $\sup_{n \notin \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}}\lvert\operatorname{Im}\gamma_n\rvert \le \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} < \eta_{\min}$ (covering critical-line zeros at $\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} = 0$ and any RH-independent strict strip separation $0 < \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} < \eta_{\min}$), each channel $g_i$ obeys $(1 + (\operatorname{Re}\gamma_n)^2)\lvert\widehat{g_i}(\gamma_n)\rvert \le \cosh(A\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}}) M_i$; applying Cauchy–Schwarz at each zero to $g = \sum_i a_i g_i$ yields (**no pointwise zero constraints, zero peak-convolution factors, and zero per-packet zero lookups once a valid upper budget $\overline{S}_4$ for the entire non-target spectrum is supplied—using the shared constant $S_4^{\mathrm{gen}}$ when that spectrum contains only genuine zeros**)

   $$
   \left\lvert\operatorname{Re}(a^* G_{\mathrm{Weil}} a) + 4\sum_{i=1}^q m_i\lvert a_i\rvert^2\right\rvert \;\le\; \cosh^2(A\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}})\!\left(\sum_{i=1}^q M_i^2\right) S_4^{\mathrm{rem}}\,\lVert a\rVert_2^2 \;=\; O_{z,w,\overline{S}_4}\!\left(e^{-2(\eta_{\min} - \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}})A}\right)\lVert a\rVert_2^2.
   $$

   Hence for any fixed target frame and tolerance $0 < \varepsilon < 1$, sufficient bandwidth $A = O_{z,w,\overline{S}_4}\!\left(\frac{1 + \ln(1/\varepsilon)}{\eta_{\min} - \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}}}\right)$ bounds the full remainder by $\le \varepsilon\lVert a\rVert_2^2$ (for all $a$, strictly for $a \neq 0$); for single-channel targets $q = 1$ (fixed $\eta_1 > \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} \ge 0$ and $w > 0$), the uniform large-height upper bound on $M_1$ above further yields sufficient bandwidth logarithmic in $t_1$, $A = \frac{1}{\eta_1 - \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}}}\bigl(2\ln(1 + t_1) + \frac{1}{2}\ln(1/\varepsilon) + O_{\eta_1,w,\overline{S}_4}(1)\bigr)$ (whereas across general multi-channel frames $q \ge 2$, uniform $T_{\max}$-scaling additionally requires bounding $\lambda_{\min}(G_\infty)$ and the finite-$c$ onset uniformly over a quantitatively separated orbit family). Conversely, for any single-channel packet $q = 1$ certified via the two-jet bound $M_1^2 S \le \varepsilon$ (with fixed fourth-moment scalar $S > 0$, e.g. $S = S_4^{\mathrm{gen}}$), the necessary bound $M_1 \ge \frac{1 + \lvert t_1^2 - \eta_1^2\rvert}{\min(1, A t_1)\sinh(A\eta_1)}$ of [@rem-ch34-signed-orbit-support-budget] forces $\min(1, A t_1)\sinh(A\eta_1) \ge (1 + \lvert t_1^2 - \eta_1^2\rvert)\sqrt{S/\varepsilon}$, and since $\min(1, A t_1) \le 1$, every $A > 0$ obeys the certificate-specific floor

   $$
   A \;\ge\; A_{\min}^{\mathrm{2\text{-}jet}}(\varepsilon) \;:=\; \frac{1}{\eta_1}\operatorname{arsinh}\!\left(\bigl(1 + \lvert t_1^2 - \eta_1^2\rvert\bigr)\sqrt{\frac{S}{\varepsilon}}\right)
   $$

   (when small $t_1$ makes $A t_1 < 1$, the preceding $\min(1, A t_1)$ inequality is stronger; because this floor applies specifically to two-jet certificates, directly summed spectral certificates can validly pass below $A_{\min}^{\mathrm{2\text{-}jet}}$, as shown in the $A_{\mathrm{spec}}$ column of Table 2). Likewise, $\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} < \eta_{\min}$ is a **sufficient condition** for this majorant: at $\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} \ge \eta_{\min}$ the majorant loses its exponential decay guarantee via $\cosh(A\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}}) \ge \cosh(A\eta_{\min})$ (without proving that actual evaluations or finer constructions cannot decay). Table 2 lists across all nine augmented benchmarks ($\varepsilon = 2^{-10}$, for the specified same-center unweighted $w = 1$ family; all nine readout residuals $< 1.22\times 10^{-57}$) the smallest integer radii passing each certificate with the coefficient-based two-jet bounds: $A_{\mathrm{2\text{-}jet}}$ (certified under the all-heights critical-line-background $\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} = 0$ model assumption via the two-jet certificate $(\sum_i M_i^2)S_4^{\mathrm{gen}} < \varepsilon$), $A_{\mathrm{spec}}$ (certified under the all-heights critical-line-background model assumption via $2\sum_{0 < \gamma_n \le 1500}\sum_{i=1}^q\lvert\widehat{g_i}(\gamma_n)\rvert^2 + \bigl(\sum_{i=1}^q M_i^2\bigr)\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(1500) < \varepsilon$—where the finite sum is the trace of the finite positive-semidefinite remainder matrix and bounds its quadratic form upon multiplication by $\lVert a\rVert_2^2$—with all $1{,}069$ zeros below $1500$ unconditionally isolated in FLINT plus tail $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(1500) \le 1.8274\times 10^{-10}$), and $A_{\mathrm{strip}}$ (half-strip majorant $\cosh^2(A\eta_{\min}/2)(\sum_i M_i^2)S_4^{\mathrm{gen}}$, rather than an independently sampled half-strip zero spectrum), alongside the $S = S_4^{\mathrm{gen}}$ single-channel certificate floor $A_{\min}^{\mathrm{2\text{-}jet}}$ (outward-rounded downward) and the certified-cap ratio $(N_{\mathrm{UB}} + 2)/A_{\mathrm{2\text{-}jet}}$ relative to the support cap $N_{\mathrm{UB}} + 2$ of [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] (rounded midpoints; $L^1$ and $M_i$ columns show outward-rounded upper bounds on the channel maxima):

3. **Exact even Paley–Wiener $L^2_{\mathrm{even}}([-A, A])$ Gram crossover and explicit cosine-product growing-bandwidth killer (for each fixed derivative order $\ell$, $R_{\mathrm{win}}$-uniform $O_{z_1,\ell}(1)$ jets at full-density polylogarithmic bandwidth)**: For a fixed target $z_1 = t_1 + i\eta_1$ with $t_1\eta_1 \neq 0$, when one instead insists on exact pointwise killing across the window $[-R_{\mathrm{win}}, R_{\mathrm{win}}]$ for $K$ selected distinct positive critical-line ordinate pairs $\pm\gamma_1, \dots, \pm\gamma_K$ (with $K = N_+(R_{\mathrm{win}})$ when the selection exhausts the window and all zeros therein are certified simple and on the critical line; for prescribed higher orders $m_n > 1$, one adds the derivative moment constraints $\widehat{k}^{(j)}(\gamma_n) = 0$ for $0 \le j < m_n$ and repeats each cosine factor $m_n$ times) via $\widehat{k}(\gamma_n) = 0$ together with signed readout $\widehat{k}(z_1) = 1,\; \widehat{k}(\bar{z}_1) = -1$, both the **exact $L^2$ extremal** and an **explicit smooth growing-bandwidth construction** can now be resolved:
   - *Exact even Paley–Wiener minimum $L^2$ norm and steep crossover across $A_{\mathrm{crit}}$*: Splitting $k = u + iv \in L^2_{\mathrm{even}}([-A, A])$ into real and imaginary parts, because the real-zero constraints and the target functions $P(x) = \cos(t_1 x)\cosh(\eta_1 x)$ and $Q(x) = \sin(t_1 x)\sinh(\eta_1 x)$ are all real-valued, the paired signed readouts $\widehat{k}(z_1) = 1$ and $\widehat{k}(\bar{z}_1) = -1$ force homogeneous constraints $\langle P, u\rangle = \langle Q, u\rangle = 0$ on the real part $u$, and the real-zero constraints are likewise homogeneous on it ($\langle\cos(\gamma_n\cdot), u\rangle = 0$), so setting $u = 0$ minimizes the $L^2$ norm and makes the extremal purely imaginary, $k_* = i v_*$, where the coefficient vector of $v_*$ solves the $(K + 2)\times(K + 2)$ real symmetric Gram system $G_{A,K} c = e_1$ imposing one value constraint per selected ordinate in basis order $(Q,\; P,\; \cos(\gamma_1 x), \dots, \cos(\gamma_K x))$ (for the Hermite extension with prescribed orders $m_n \ge 1$, this becomes a $(2 + \sum_{n=1}^K m_n)$-dimensional system using the real even representers $\left.\partial_\gamma^j\cos(\gamma x)\right|_{\gamma=\gamma_n}$ for $0 \le j < m_n$), with closed-form entries $\int_{-A}^A \cos(ux)\cos(vx)\,dx = \frac{\sin(A(u - v))}{u - v} + \frac{\sin(A(u + v))}{u + v}$ (at $u = \pm v \neq 0$, only the term with vanishing denominator tends to $A$ while the companion term stays $\frac{\sin(2Au)}{2u}$, and at $u = v = 0$ both terms combine to $2A$; evaluating at $\bar{z}_1 = t_1 - i\eta_1$ and taking real and imaginary parts yields $+\langle P, \cos(\gamma_n\cdot)\rangle$ and $+\langle Q, \cos(\gamma_n\cdot)\rangle$, respectively). Because the $2K + 4$ exponential frequencies $\pm z_1, \pm\bar{z}_1, \pm\gamma_1, \dots, \pm\gamma_K$ are pairwise distinct, for every $A > 0$, $G_{A,K} \succ 0$ is strictly positive definite (and likewise linear independence of exponential polynomials $x^j e^{\pm i\gamma_n x}$ with distinct frequencies makes the general $(2 + \sum_{n=1}^K m_n)$-dimensional Hermite Gram matrix strictly positive definite), so the exact minimum norm equals $\lVert k_*\rVert_{L^2} = \sqrt{(G_{A,K}^{-1})_{1,1}}$ and varies real-analytically in $A > 0$; approximating both $v_*$ and all $K + 2$ Gram representers (or all $2 + \sum_{n=1}^K m_n$ representers in the Hermite case) in $C_c^\infty((-A, A))_{\mathrm{even}}$ (so the smoothed representers form an invertible $(K + 2)\times(K + 2)$ or corresponding-dimension moment-correction matrix removing the finite-dimensional moment residuals exactly) shows that the same value is the exact infimum over $C_c^\infty((-A, A))_{\mathrm{even}}$. Table 3 uses adaptive Arb precision up to $4{,}864$ bits to verify at $z_1 = 1 + 0.25i$ across five windows the values of $\log_{10}\lVert k_*\rVert_{L^2}$ (rounded to five decimals; all Gram residuals $< 10^{-30}$ and all Arb ball radii for $\log_{10}\lVert k_*\rVert_{L^2}$ $< 5\times 10^{-10}$, compared to the zero-free Schur-complement floor at $A = 3$, $\log_{10}(1.390504) \approx 0.14317$):

     | Window radius $R_{\mathrm{win}}$ | Positive zeros $K$ | $A = 0.5$ | $A = 1$ | $A = A_{\mathrm{crit}}$ | $1.25\,A_{\mathrm{crit}}$ | $1.5\,A_{\mathrm{crit}}$ | $2\,A_{\mathrm{crit}}$ | $A = 3$ |
     | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
     | $50$ | $10$ | $2.91920$ | $0.99046$ | $6.84711$ | $4.24357$ | $2.01298$ | $1.41077$ | $0.14320$ |
     | $100$ | $29$ | $14.77175$ | $1.02930$ | $9.35868$ | $1.95265$ | $1.23530$ | $0.79903$ | $0.14320$ |
     | $200$ | $79$ | $59.60103$ | $2.48248$ | $14.44036$ | $1.18942$ | $0.72658$ | $0.37977$ | $0.14321$ |

     The table exhibits a steep real-analytic crossover past the Jensen obstruction threshold $A_{\mathrm{crit}}$: subcritically at $A = 1$, for $R_{\mathrm{win}} = 403$ and $R_{\mathrm{win}} = 500$ the true $\log_{10}\lVert k_*\rVert_{L^2}$ reaches $62.94033$ and $102.25050$, substantially exceeding the outward-downward-rounded Jensen $L^2$ lower bounds $30.17299$ and $58.19109$; yet at $R_{\mathrm{win}} = 500$, widening bandwidth $A$ from $A_{\mathrm{crit}} \approx 1.2836$ by just $25\%$ to $1.25\,A_{\mathrm{crit}} \approx 1.6045$ drops the minimum norm $\lVert k_*\rVert_{L^2}$ by over $29.149$ orders of magnitude ($\log_{10}\lVert k_*\rVert_{L^2}$ falls from $29.75573$ to $0.60638$), and at $A = 3$ all five finite-window values of $\log_{10}\lVert k_*\rVert_{L^2}$ round to $0.14320\text{–}0.14321$ at five decimal places (lying inside the outward enclosure $[0.1431969, 0.1432079]$), within $4\times 10^{-5}$ of the zero-free floor $0.14317$.
   - *Explicit cosine-product convolution growing-bandwidth killer (at full-density $\frac{1}{8}(\ln R_{\mathrm{win}})^2$ bandwidth, for each fixed derivative order $\ell$, $R_{\mathrm{win}}$-uniform $O_{z_1,\ell}(1)$ jets and $\eta := \eta_1 \to 0^+$ advantage)*: To build an explicit smooth growing-bandwidth killer directly, associate to the $K$ selected distinct critical-line zero pairs $\pm\gamma_1, \dots, \pm\gamma_K \in [-R_{\mathrm{win}}, R_{\mathrm{win}}]$ the half-steps $\tau_n := \frac{\pi}{2\gamma_n}$ and two-point even probability measures $\mu_n := \frac{1}{2}(\delta_{-\tau_n} + \delta_{\tau_n})$; their $K$-fold convolution $\mu_{1..K} := \mu_1 * \cdots * \mu_K$ is an even probability measure with total variation $\lVert\mu_{1..K}\rVert_{\mathrm{TV}} = 1$ supported in $[-A_{\mathrm{sum}}(R_{\mathrm{win}}),\; A_{\mathrm{sum}}(R_{\mathrm{win}})]$; whenever the selected distinct real ordinates obey the full-density count $K(u) = \frac{u}{2\pi}\ln u + O(u)$, partial summation gives

     $$
     A_{\mathrm{sum}}(R_{\mathrm{win}}) \;:=\; \sum_{n=1}^K \frac{\pi}{2\gamma_n} \;=\; \frac{1}{8}(\ln R_{\mathrm{win}})^2 + O(\ln R_{\mathrm{win}}), \qquad C_K(z) \;:=\; \widehat{\mu_{1..K}}(z) \;=\; \prod_{n=1}^K \cos\!\left(\frac{\pi z}{2\gamma_n}\right).
     $$

     (On every finite window below, the selection exhausts the window and Platt–Trudgian (2021) plus FLINT isolation directly certify that all zeros lie on the critical line and are simple, so $K = N_+(R_{\mathrm{win}})$; when cosine factors are repeated $m_n$ times for higher multiplicities, the sum and product above become the weighted sum $\sum_{n=1}^K m_n\frac{\pi}{2\gamma_n}$ and multiplicity product $\prod_{n=1}^K \cos^{m_n}\!\left(\frac{\pi z}{2\gamma_n}\right)$, with the full-density hypothesis applied to the weighted count $\sum_{\gamma_n \le u} m_n = \frac{u}{2\pi}\ln u + O(u)$, while the $R_{\mathrm{win}} \to \infty$ asymptotic requires the corresponding full-density critical-line input.) Since $\lvert\cos(u + iv)\rvert^2 = \cos^2 u + \sinh^2 v \ge \cos^2 u$ and $\cos u \ge 1 - \frac{u^2}{2} \ge e^{-u^2}$ on $\lvert u\rvert \le 1$, under genuine-spectrum multiplicity weights (weighting each genuine zero by its analytic multiplicity, certified simple below $1500$ as in the replay), combining $\sum_{n=1}^\infty \gamma_n^{-2} < \infty$ with the replay's Brent–Platt–Trudgian (2021) ordinate-cutoff tail (setting $T = R$ in the right-hand side of item 4's $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}^{(p)}$ formula to define $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT,ord}}^{(p)}(R)$, here taking $T = 1500$ for the inverse-square tail $\frac{1}{2}\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT,ord}}^{(2)}(1500)$, distinct from item 4's geometric cutoff $T = \sqrt{1500^2 - \frac{1}{4}}$, though all four infinite-product lower bounds and three uniform jet caps below pass under the geometric cutoff as well) keeps the genuine-spectrum infinite product bounded away from zero at every fixed target $z_1 = t_1 + i\eta_1$ ($t_1\eta_1 \neq 0$; Arb-certified lower bounds on $\lvert C_\infty(z_1)\rvert$ at $(1, 0.25)$, $(1, 0.01)$, $(5, 0.1)$, and $(14.134725, 0.1)$ are $\ge 0.972742635$, $0.971058508$, $0.474207115$, and $7.8954166\times 10^{-5}$); separately, for any fixed weighted selection obeying $\sum_{n=1}^\infty m_n\gamma_n^{-2} < \infty$ (automatically implied by the weighted full-density law above), the corresponding weighted infinite product likewise stays bounded away from zero. For any fixed target $z_1$ with $t_1\eta_1 \neq 0$, strict positivity of $\psi_1$ on $(-1, 1)$ together with linear independence of $P, Q$ on $(-1, 1)$ guarantees that taking the explicit real even basis functions $\psi_1 P,\; \psi_1 Q \in C_c^\infty([-1, 1]; \mathbb{R})_{\mathrm{even}}$ and solving their invertible $2\times 2$ weighted Gram system in $L^2([-1, 1], \psi_1(x)\,dx)$ explicitly produces a purely imaginary even seed $g_{0,K} \in C_c^\infty([-1, 1])_{\mathrm{even}}$ with $\widehat{g_{0,K}}(z_1) = 1/C_K(z_1)$ (which by pure-imaginary evenness automatically satisfies $\widehat{g_{0,K}}(\bar{z}_1) = -1/\overline{C_K(z_1)} = -1/C_K(\bar{z}_1)$); setting total bandwidth $A_{\mathrm{cos}} := 1 + A_{\mathrm{sum}}(R_{\mathrm{win}})$, the **explicit smooth growing-bandwidth killer** $k_{\mathrm{cos}} := \mu_{1..K} * g_{0,K} \in C_c^\infty([-A_{\mathrm{cos}}, A_{\mathrm{cos}}])_{\mathrm{even}}$ kills every $\pm\gamma_1, \dots, \pm\gamma_K$ identically, satisfies $\widehat{k_{\mathrm{cos}}}(z_1) = 1$ and $\widehat{k_{\mathrm{cos}}}(\bar{z}_1) = -1$, and by probability-measure contraction for distinct genuine-spectrum ordinates (or any fixed weighted selection obeying $\sum_{n=1}^\infty m_n\gamma_n^{-2} < \infty$, with the constant then also depending on that weighted selection) for each fixed derivative order $\ell \ge 0$ obeys $\lVert k_{\mathrm{cos}}^{(\ell)}\rVert_{L^1} \le \lVert g_{0,K}^{(\ell)}\rVert_{L^1} = O_{z_1,\ell}(1)$—**its $L^1$ jet upper bounds are completely uniform as $R_{\mathrm{win}} \to \infty$**. Writing $M(k) := \lVert k\rVert_{L^1} + \lVert k''\rVert_{L^1}$, using specifically the unweighted shifted-bump pair ($w = \frac{1}{2}, c = \frac{1}{2}$) from item 2 as the seed, and denoting by $M_{\mathrm{cert}}(R_{\mathrm{win}})$ the coefficient-based seed two-jet upper bound from item 2 (obeying $M(k_{\mathrm{cos}}) \le M_{\mathrm{cert}}(R_{\mathrm{win}})$), we independently certified seed invertibility and readout residuals across all $28$ pairs formed by the four targets $(1, 0.25)$, $(1, 0.01)$, $(5, 0.1)$, and $(14.134725, 0.1)$ with the seven windows $R_{\mathrm{win}} \in \{50, 100, 200, 403, 500, 1000, 1500\}$, and separately for the three $t_1 = 1$ targets $(1, 0.25)$, $(1, 0.05)$, and $(1, 0.01)$, under genuine-spectrum weights the replay checks $\frac{2}{\pi} t_1 > \eta\cosh(\frac{\pi\eta}{2\gamma_1})$ so every cosine factor obeys $\lvert\cos(\frac{\pi z_1}{2\gamma_n})\rvert \le 1$, turning the infinite-product bound into $\lvert C_K(z_1)\rvert \ge \lvert C_\infty(z_1)\rvert$ for every finite prefix and certifying via the fixed shifted-bump inverse uniform-over-all-genuine-windows two-jet caps $M(k_{\mathrm{cos}}) \le M_{\mathrm{cert}}(R_{\mathrm{win}}) \le 5{,}538.68$, $26{,}041.87$, and $128{,}540.03$. Furthermore, for any fixed $t_1 \in (0, \pi/2)$ as $\eta \to 0^+$, defining the half-interval weight $W := \int_0^1 \cos^2(t_1 x)b_{1/2,1/2}(x)\,dx > 0$ and positive probability measure $d\nu(x) := W^{-1}\cos^2(t_1 x)b_{1/2,1/2}(x)\,dx$, the $\eta$-derivative of this explicit seed's Jacobian determinant at $\eta = 0$ equals $-4 W^2 \operatorname{Cov}_\nu(\tan(t_1 x),\; x\tan(t_1 x)) < 0$ (strictly negative by Chebyshev's covariance inequality for two strictly increasing functions, directly proving invertibility of the explicit pair for $0 < t_1 < \frac{\pi}{2}$ and small $\eta > 0$), while $\frac{\pi t_1}{2\gamma_1} < \frac{\pi^2}{4\gamma_1} < 1$ and $\lvert\cos(\frac{\pi(t_1 + i\eta)}{2\gamma_n})\rvert \ge \cos(\frac{\pi t_1}{2\gamma_n}) \ge \exp(-(\frac{\pi t_1}{2\gamma_n})^2)$ yield $\lvert C_K(t_1 + i\eta)\rvert \ge \lvert C_\infty(t_1)\rvert > 0$ uniformly in $K$ and $\eta$, whence $M_{\mathrm{cert}}(R_{\mathrm{win}}) = O_{t_1}(1/\eta)$; under the all-heights critical-line-background model assumption, explicitly selecting every background ordinate in $[-R_{\mathrm{win}}, R_{\mathrm{win}}]$ (leaving no unkilled surviving zero inside the window), certifying directly with zero peak-convolution factors via $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_{\mathrm{win}})$ the coefficient-bound BPT criterion $M_{\mathrm{cert}}(R_{\mathrm{win}})^2\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_{\mathrm{win}}) < \varepsilon$ requires only $R_{\mathrm{win}} = O_{t_1}\bigl((\eta^{-2}\varepsilon^{-1}\ln(\eta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-1}))^{1/3}\bigr)$, giving total bandwidth **polylogarithmic in both $1/\eta$ and $1/\varepsilon$**: $A_{\mathrm{cos}} = O_{t_1}\bigl(\ln^2\frac{1}{\eta\varepsilon}\bigr)$. At $\varepsilon = 2^{-10}$ under this critical-line-background assumption, the first continuous pass of this coefficient-bound BPT certificate $M_{\mathrm{cert}}(R_{\mathrm{win}})^2\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}(R_{\mathrm{win}}) < \varepsilon$ in its domain (each attained at the right-continuous BPT count jump) and the first integer window are: at $(1, 0.25)$, continuous $R_{\mathrm{win}} \approx 2{,}624.0507$ ($K = 2{,}104$, $A_{\mathrm{cos}} \approx 5.5254$) and integer $R_{\mathrm{win}} = 2{,}625$ ($K = 2{,}105$, $A_{\mathrm{cos}} \le 5.5261$, $M_{\mathrm{cert}} \le 5{,}109.21$, cert. $\le 9.7449\times 10^{-4}$); at $(1, 0.05)$, continuous $R_{\mathrm{win}} \approx 8{,}110.6377$ ($K = 7{,}957$, $A_{\mathrm{cos}} \approx 7.3868$) and integer $8{,}111$ ($A_{\mathrm{cos}} \le 7.3869$, $M_{\mathrm{cert}} \le 25{,}595.91$, cert. $\le 9.7653\times 10^{-4}$); and at $(1, 0.01)$, continuous $R_{\mathrm{win}} \approx 24{,}845.6591$ ($K = 28{,}799$, $A_{\mathrm{cos}} \approx 9.5482$) and integer $24{,}846$ ($A_{\mathrm{cos}} \le 9.5482$, $M_{\mathrm{cert}} \le 128{,}003.87$, cert. $\le 0.000976562499 < 2^{-10}$). Comparing the two sufficient certificates of items 2 and 3: for single-channel high targets $t_1 \to \infty$ at fixed $\eta_1 > \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}}$, item 2 proves logarithmic sufficient bandwidth $O_{\eta_1,w,\overline{S}_4}\bigl(\frac{\ln(1 + t_1) + \ln(1/\varepsilon)}{\eta_1 - \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}}}\bigr)$ (with no matching large-$t_1$ lower bound proved here for item 3); conversely, at moderate height $t_1 \in (0, \pi/2)$ and fixed tolerance $\varepsilon \in (0, 1)$ under the critical-line-background ($\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} = 0$) assumption as $\eta \to 0^+$, pairing item 3's polylogarithmic upper bound $O_{t_1}\bigl(\ln^2\frac{1}{\eta\varepsilon}\bigr)$ against item 2's fixed full-spectrum fourth-moment two-jet certificate floor $A_{\min}^{\mathrm{2\text{-}jet}} = \Omega_{t_1,\varepsilon}(1/\eta)$ rigorously proves an asymptotic bandwidth advantage over the fixed full-spectrum fourth-moment certificate of item 2 (illustrated numerically at $(1, 0.01)$ by $A_{\mathrm{2\text{-}jet}} = 235$ versus $A_{\mathrm{cos}} \le 9.5482$).

4. **Higher-power BPT spectral-decay upper bound and exact two-sided Arb sandwich for the actual infinite remainder (certifying strict separation $N_{\mathrm{actual}} < N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}} < N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$)**: Turning to the second target of [@prob-ch34-signed-orbit-joint-budget], for `burnolBasis` $= b^{*(N+1)} * k_i$ and the synthesized packet $\mathrm{syn} := \sum_{i\in\iota} a_i(b^{*(N+1)} * k_i)$ in the actual full-Gram remainder $\operatorname{Re}(a^* G_{\mathrm{Weil}} a) + 4\sum_{i\in\iota} m_i\lvert a_i\rvert^2$, whenever the exceptional set contains all target indices $E \supseteq \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$ (both genuine and adjoined, matching the frozen `target_subset` condition), the target quadratic form cancels identically and the non-target head $E \setminus \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$ vanishes by `kills_exception`, leaving purely the exterior tail $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{actual}}(N) := \operatorname{Re}\sum_{n \notin E} m_n\,\widehat{\mathrm{syn}}(\gamma_n)\overline{\widehat{\mathrm{syn}}(\bar{\gamma}_n)}$ (on symmetric zero spectra, whenever the exterior $E^c$ is conjugation-invariant—or any unmatched reflected term vanishes by head killing—conjugation pairing makes this tail automatically real). Whereas [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] replaced $\lvert\widehat{b}(\gamma_n)\rvert$ uniformly by $\frac{1}{2}$ in the majorant (yielding only $4^{-1}$ decay per step), whenever $D > 0$, keeping the actual two-jet decay $\lvert\widehat{b}(\gamma_n)\rvert,\; \lvert\widehat{b}(\bar{\gamma}_n)\rvert \le \frac{D}{1 + (\operatorname{Re}\gamma_n)^2} < \frac{D}{(\operatorname{Re}\gamma_n)^2}$ inside the $2(N + 1)$-th power lets Brent–Platt–Trudgian (2021, Theorem 1) apply directly on $[T_0, \infty)$ ($2\pi \le T_0 \le T := \sqrt{R_E^2 - \frac{1}{4}}$) to every nonnegative, nonincreasing, convex $C^2$ power weight integrable against $\ln(u/(2\pi))$, namely $\phi_p(u) := u^{-p}$ ($p \ge 2$ satisfies $\phi_p > 0,\; \phi_p' < 0,\; \phi_p'' = p(p + 1)u^{-p-2} > 0$, using the right-hand limit of the source's half-weight convention to substitute the right-continuous genuine multiplicity count $N_+(T)$ for the strict tail):

   $$
   \Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}^{(p)}(R_E) \;:=\; 2\!\left[\frac{(p - 1)\ln(T/(2\pi)) + 1}{2\pi(p - 1)^2 T^{p - 1}} \;+\; \frac{L_{\mathrm{RvM}}(T) - N_+(T)}{T^p} \;+\; \frac{2p(2.067 + 0.059\ln T) + 0.059 + \frac{1}{150}}{T^{p + 1}}\right].
   $$

   Whenever the exceptional set $E$ contains the full genuine two-sided ball $\{n : \lvert\gamma_n\rvert \le R_E\}$ together with all target indices $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$ (both genuine and adjoined, i.e. $E \supseteq \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{targ}}$, and no artificial non-target nodes lie outside $E$; any such nodes would require separate tail accounting), letting $J_{\mathrm{tot}}$ bound the sum of the killers' strip-decay coefficients (specifically the frozen budget $J_{\mathrm{tot}} = \sum_{i\in\iota} 3(J_0(i) + J_2(i))$ in Table 4), subtracting the target quadratic allows dropping the coarse $4\sum_{i\in\iota} m_i$ term from the **tail majorant**, so unconditionally across the full half-strip ($D = D_{\mathrm{frozen}} := 3(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}})$ or $D = D_{\mathrm{cosh}} := \cosh(\frac{1}{2})(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}})$) and under the critical-line-background model assumption ($D = D_{\mathrm{real}} := J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}}$) the actual remainder obeys $\lvert\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{actual}}(N)\rvert \le D^{2(N+1)} J_{\mathrm{tot}}^2\,\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}^{(4(N+2))}(R_E)\lVert a\rVert_2^2$; meanwhile, for a normalized single-channel ($\lvert a_1\rvert = 1$) killer $k_* \in L^2_{\mathrm{even}}([-1, 1])$ on a critical-line background—bounded only in $L^1$ without $C^2$ smoothness—$\lvert\widehat{k_*}(\gamma)\rvert \le \lVert k_*\rVert_{L^1}$ with $p = 4(N + 1)$ gives $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{actual}}(N) \le D_{\mathrm{real}}^{2(N+1)}\lVert k_*\rVert_{L^1}^2\,\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}^{(4(N+1))}(R_E)$ (multiplying by $\lVert a\rVert_2^2$ with the corresponding $L^1$ channel budget in general). Thus the general geometric per-step factor from the peak envelope is $(D/T^2)^2$, which whenever $D/T^2 < \frac{1}{2}$ strictly sharpens the old $4^{-1}$ step factor (combined with the $p$-dependent bracket inside $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}^{(p)}$).

   Table 4 certifies across all nine benchmarks of [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] using the **exact same original packets, original cutoffs $R_E$, and original frozen $J_{\mathrm{tot}}$** the minimal higher-power BPT depth $N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}}$ in log space (all integer depths are exact and identical across $256$-bit and $320$-bit runs):

   | :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |

   | $q=2,\ \delta=1$ | $34{,}031{,}070{,}304{,}400$ | $1{,}017{,}501{,}874{,}350$ | **$936{,}221{,}619{,}922$** | $901{,}221{,}542{,}383$ | $897{,}104{,}654{,}235$ |

   Strikingly, on all three $q = 2$ rows—even while **retaining the exact same differential killer and frozen $J_{\mathrm{tot}}$**—the unconditional full-strip higher-power upper bound $N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}}$ already drops **strictly below** the jet-proxy lower bound $N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$ (by $6.4\%\text{–}8.0\%$, while cutting Cycle 8's $N_{\mathrm{UB}}$ by a factor of $17.2\text{–}36.3$ across all nine rows). To pin down under the all-heights critical-line-background augmented-spectrum model assumption the **exact least localization depth $N_{\mathrm{actual}}$ (at tolerance $2^{-10}$) for each specified peak–killer pair** and certify $N_{\mathrm{actual}} < N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}} < N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$, fix $z_1 = 1 + 0.25i$, $R_{\mathrm{win}} \in \{403, 500\}$, and the original smooth peak $b = P_b(-\partial_x^2)\psi_{h_0}$, paired with three unit-support killers: (a) item 3's exact $L^2$-minimal killer $k_* \in L^2_{\mathrm{even}}([-1, 1])$ (which itself lies outside the frozen `burnolBasis` smooth-killer input requirement, so this row evaluates the underlying convolution formula $b^{*(N+1)} * k_*$, which remains smooth and compactly supported after convolving with smooth $b$, taking $k_{\mathrm{core}} = k_*$ and $a = 1$ in the core budget below); (b) the closed-support smooth convolution killer $k_{\mathrm{sm}} := \psi_\delta * k_{*, 1-\delta} \in C_c^\infty([-1, 1])_{\mathrm{even}}$ ($\delta = \frac{1}{64}$, core half-width $a = \frac{63}{64}$, setting $w_\delta := 1/\widehat{\psi_\delta}(z_1)$ and taking $k_{\mathrm{core}} = k_{*, 1-\delta}$ to be the unique even $L^2([-a, a])$ minimizer with the prescribed window zeros and adjusted signed readouts $w_\delta$ and $-\overline{w_\delta}$ to preserve exact zero killing and $\pm 1$ readouts); and (c) the strictly interior-support smooth variant $k_{\mathrm{int}} := \psi_{1/64} * k_{\mathrm{core}} \in C_c^\infty((-1, 1))_{\mathrm{even}}$ (core half-width $a = \frac{4031}{4096}$, $\delta = \frac{1}{64}$, total support radius $a + \delta = 1 - 2^{-12}$, where $k_{\mathrm{core}}$ is the unique even $L^2([-a, a])$ minimizer with the same prescribed window zeros and adjusted signed readouts $w_\delta$ and $-\overline{w_\delta}$). Denoting the core $L^1$ upper bound by $L_1 := \sqrt{2a}\lVert k_{\mathrm{core}}\rVert_{L^2}$ (in case (a), Cauchy–Schwarz directly yields $\lVert k_*\rVert_{L^1} \le L_1$), for the two smooth variants (b) and (c) Young's convolution inequality ($\lVert\psi_\delta\rVert_{L^1} = 1$, $\lVert\psi_\delta''\rVert_{L^1} = B_2/\delta^2$) applied directly to the actual norms gives $\lVert k\rVert_{L^1} \le L_1$ and $\lVert k''\rVert_{L^1} \le (B_2/\delta^2) L_1$, yielding the chosen smooth-variant two-jet budget $J_0(k) + J_2(k) \le L_1(1 + B_2/\delta^2)$. At each depth $N$, the replay verifies zero by zero across the isolated surviving head $R_{\mathrm{win}} < \gamma_n \le 1500$ that $\lvert\widehat{b}(\gamma_n)\rvert^2 < 1$ (making the finite head sum strictly decreasing in depth); under the critical-line-background full-spectrum positivity model assumption, the exact surviving-head sum $2\sum_{R_{\mathrm{win}} < \gamma_n \le 1500}\lvert\widehat{b}(\gamma_n)\rvert^{2(N+1)}\lvert\widehat{k}(\gamma_n)\rvert^2$ gives a certified lower bound, and adding the higher-power BPT tail above $1500$, $L_1^2 D_{\mathrm{real}}^{2(N+1)}\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}^{(4(N+1))}(1500)$, gives a certified upper bound (the archived replay certifies that at **every** integer $0 \le N < N_{\mathrm{actual}}$, the head lower bound strictly exceeds $2^{-10} \approx 9.7656\times 10^{-4}$, with Table 5 displaying the tightest failure at $N_{\mathrm{actual}} - 1$ outward-rounded downward and the passing upper bound at $N_{\mathrm{actual}}$ outward-rounded upward strictly below $2^{-10}$; smooth rows also list the $p = 4(N + 2)$ jet bound $N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow,sm}}$):

   | Window $R_{\mathrm{win}}$ | Unit-support killer | Exact actual depth $N_{\mathrm{actual}}$ | Higher-power UB $N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}}$ ($p = 4(N+1)$) | Smooth-jet UB $N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow,sm}}$ ($p = 4(N+2)$) | Jet-proxy LB $N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$ | Certified LB at $N_{\mathrm{actual}} - 1$ (LB) | Certified UB at $N_{\mathrm{actual}}$ (UB) |
   | :---: | :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
   | $403$ | Exact $L^2$-minimal $k_*$ | **$41$** | $59$ | — | $93$ | $5.6984\times 10^{-3}$ | $5.4499\times 10^{-6}$ |
   | $403$ | Smooth conv. $k_{\mathrm{sm}}$ (radius $1$) | **$42$** | $62$ | $61$ | $93$ | $7.5388\times 10^{-3}$ | $6.1254\times 10^{-6}$ |
   | $403$ | Strictly interior $k_{\mathrm{int}}$ (radius $1 - 2^{-12}$) | **$42$** | $62$ | $61$ | $93$ | $9.5054\times 10^{-3}$ | $7.6831\times 10^{-6}$ |
   | $500$ | Exact $L^2$-minimal $k_*$ | **$55$** | $81$ | — | $186$ | $6.7879\times 10^{-2}$ | $1.5285\times 10^{-5}$ |
   | $500$ | Smooth conv. $k_{\mathrm{sm}}$ (radius $1$) | **$56$** | $84$ | $83$ | $186$ | $5.1119\times 10^{-1}$ | $9.4305\times 10^{-5}$ |
   | $500$ | Strictly interior $k_{\mathrm{int}}$ (radius $1 - 2^{-12}$) | **$56$** | $84$ | $83$ | $186$ | $6.7559\times 10^{-1}$ | $1.2458\times 10^{-4}$ |

   Every row certifies under the all-heights critical-line-background augmented-spectrum model assumption via two-sided Arb enclosures for each fixed peak–killer pair the exact strict separation chain **$N_{\mathrm{actual}} < N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}} < N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$** (at $R_{\mathrm{win}} = 500$, true depth $55\text{–}56$ is less than one third of the jet-proxy lower bound $186$). Furthermore, the per-depth trace (archived in `summary_tables.md`) uncovers a sharp spectral-overshoot phenomenon: at $R_{\mathrm{win}} = 403$ ($k_*$), the first surviving reflected pair of ordinates $\pm\gamma_{205}$ ($\gamma_{205} \approx 404.2364$) at $N = 0$ contributes only $\approx 4.2683\times 10^{39}$ and by $N = 15$ already drops below $2^{-10}$ ($\approx 8.1787\times 10^{-4}$), whereas the finite surviving head $403 < \gamma_n \le 1500$ at $N = 0$ reaches $\approx 4.0542\times 10^{122}$ and stays above until $N = 41$ before falling below $2^{-10}$ (by which point the BPT tail above $1500$ is already $< 1.27\times 10^{-61}$)—because $\lvert\widehat{k_*}(\gamma)\rvert$ surges immediately outside the killed window so substantial surviving spectral energy vastly exceeding the $\pm\gamma_{205}$ pair alone lies on subsequent surviving zeros, making the multi-zero finite head plus higher-power BPT tail far sharper than the first surviving reflected pair $\pm\gamma_{205}$ lower bound alone.
:::

::: {.problem #prob-ch34-signed-orbit-joint-budget title="Supercritical Killers with Logarithmic Bandwidth, Wide-Strip Non-Target Zeros, and Direct Formalization (Open Problem)"}
Working strictly in both the conditional augmented test-spectrum regime and the unconditional general `ZeroData` regime without invoking the externally verified classical Riemann Hypothesis ([@conj-riemann-hypothesis], `riemannZeta_eq_zero_re : RiemannZetaZerosOnLine`, under which any genuine off-line orbit frame is empty, $q = 0$), [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] and [@rem-ch34-spectral-tail-bypass-and-pw-transition] now **resolve the explicit conditional joint certification, unconditional Jensen bottleneck, square-root cutoff tightening, zero-killing-free exponential-leverage spectral-tail bypass, polylogarithmic growing-bandwidth explicit killer, and exact two-sided actual-remainder depth sandwich**: first, using the same jet envelope as the frozen lemma `peak_tail_of_two_jet_budget`, an unconditional written square-root sufficient radius achieves for the fixed-$q$ Newton divided-difference derivative-budget peaks $R_E^{\mathrm{sqrt}} = \sqrt{\max(0, 6(J_0^{\mathrm{peak}} + J_2^{\mathrm{peak}}) - \frac{3}{4})} = \Theta_q(1 + T_{\max})$ (collapsing ball zero counts across all nine benchmarks by roughly $821\text{–}4.8\times 10^5$ and at $(1, 0.25)$ reducing the sufficient radius below the first genuine zero via the closed-support smooth peak $b_{\mathrm{opt}}$, so the ball contains no genuine nontrivial $\zeta$-zeros); second, zero-killing-free shifted-bump exponential-leverage direct packets with zero peak-convolution factors achieve under the sufficient strip-separation condition $\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} < \eta_{\min}$, finite non-target fourth-moment budget $S_4^{\mathrm{rem}} \le \overline{S}_4 < \infty$ (with $S_4^{\mathrm{rem}} \le S_4^{\mathrm{gen}}$ on a genuine background), and tolerance $\varepsilon \in (0, 1)$ remainder bounded by $\le \varepsilon\lVert a\rVert_2^2$ (for all $a$, strictly for $a \neq 0$) at fixed-frame bandwidth $A = O_{z,w,\overline{S}_4}\bigl(\frac{1 + \ln(1/\varepsilon)}{\eta_{\min} - \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}}}\bigr)$ (and logarithmic single-channel high-target scaling $O_{\eta_1,w,\overline{S}_4}\bigl(\frac{\ln(1 + t_1) + \ln(1/\varepsilon)}{\eta_1 - \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}}}\bigr)$), achieving under the $\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} = 0$ critical-line-background benchmarks certified-support-cap ratios of roughly $4.5\times 10^4\text{–}3.8\times 10^{11}$ (the six single-channel measured radii running from roughly $1.20\text{–}5.88$ times the certificate floor $A_{\min}^{\mathrm{2\text{-}jet}}$), while the complementary cosine-product convolution killer $k_{\mathrm{cos}} = \mu_{1..K} * g_{0,K}$ whenever the selected critical-line ordinates obey the full-density count of item 3 (whether under genuine-spectrum multiplicities or any fixed weighted selection obeying $\sum_{n=1}^\infty m_n\gamma_n^{-2} < \infty$) achieves at polylogarithmic bandwidth $A_{\mathrm{cos}} = 1 + A_{\mathrm{sum}}(R_{\mathrm{win}}) = \frac{1}{8}(\ln R_{\mathrm{win}})^2 + O(\ln R_{\mathrm{win}})$ an $R_{\mathrm{win}}$-uniform $O_{z_1,\ell}(1)$ jet cap (with the constant also depending on the fixed weighted selection) and, under the all-heights critical-line-background model assumption for fixed $0 < t_1 < \pi/2$ and $0 < \varepsilon < 1$ as $\eta \to 0^+$, polylogarithmic full-spectrum bandwidth $O_{t_1}(\ln^2\frac{1}{\eta\varepsilon})$, alongside adaptive high-precision Arb verification of the exact even Paley–Wiener $L^2_{\mathrm{even}}([-A, A])$ steep crossover across $A_{\mathrm{crit}}$; third, the higher-power BPT tail $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}^{(p)}(R_E)$ already pushes the unconditional upper bound $N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}}$ strictly below $N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$ on all three two-channel benchmarks, and on $R_{\mathrm{win}} \in \{403, 500\}$ under the all-heights critical-line-background augmented-spectrum model assumption two-sided head-plus-tail Arb sandwiches pin down each pair's true depth $N_{\mathrm{actual}}$, certifying $N_{\mathrm{actual}} < N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}} < N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$. The remaining Fourier–Weil spectral-side open problem therefore narrows to three sharper targets: first, in the fixed-center, full-density setting where exact pointwise killing of non-target zeros in $[-R_{\mathrm{win}}, R_{\mathrm{win}}]$ (excluding prescribed nonzero-readout targets) is required, narrow the bandwidth of explicit smooth small-norm killers from polylogarithmic $\frac{1}{8}(\ln R_{\mathrm{win}})^2$ down toward the supercritical logarithmic target $(\frac{1}{2} + \varepsilon)\ln R_{\mathrm{win}}$ (e.g., via Beurling–Malliavin or canonical-product multipliers), establish for quantitatively separated multi-channel frames $q \ge 2$ (where the full signed-orbit set $\mathcal{O} := \bigcup_{i=1}^q \{\pm z_i, \pm\bar{z}_i\}$ satisfies $\min_{u,v \in \mathcal{O},\; u \neq v}\lvert u - v\rvert \ge \delta_0 > 0$, simultaneously bounding inter-orbit spacing and keeping $t_i$ away from $0$, with constants depending on $q, w, \delta_0, \eta_{\min} - \sigma_{\mathrm{rem}}, \overline{S}_4$) uniform logarithmic-in-$T_{\max}$ bandwidth bounds, and construct a unified spectral-tail certificate handling wide-strip off-line non-target zeros ($\lvert\operatorname{Im}\gamma_n\rvert \ge \eta_{\min}$) without assuming $\sigma_{\mathrm{rem}} < \eta_{\min}$ or critical-line containment; second, extend the exact two-sided actual-remainder sandwich $N_{\mathrm{actual}} < N_{\mathrm{UB}}^{\mathrm{pow}} < N_{\mathrm{LB}}^{\mathrm{jet}}$ to large cutoffs $R_E$, multi-channel frames $q \ge 2$, and higher-order $2m$-jet / Gevrey peak-decay families; third, formalize and freeze the square-root sufficient radius $R_E^{\mathrm{sqrt}}$, the zero-killing-free shifted-bump strip-dominance certificate, the cosine-product convolution killer, the higher-power BPT tail $\Theta_{\mathrm{BPT}}^{(p)}(R_E)$, the explicit Trudgian fourth-moment tail bound $\Theta_{\mathrm{Tr}}$, and the smooth-bump derivative bounds and Jensen bottleneck of [@rem-ch34-unit-support-burnol-joint-budget] directly inside `trureturing`. This problem audits Fourier–Weil spectral-side synthesis, mixed majorants, and bandwidth phase transitions for compactly supported even packets, distinct from the next open problem on the dense spatial-side $L^2(0, \infty)$ fractional-part Gram matrix $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_{ab}$.
:::

::: {.problem #prob-ch34-arithmetic-schur-subcubic-question title="Analytic Upper Bounds for Spatial-Truncation Preconditioning, Polylogarithmic Conditioning, and Independent Convergence Certificates for Genuine $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N$ (Open Problem)"}
The divisibility-graph matrix $G = L_D + \frac{1}{4}I$ studied here is only a pedagogical model: **it is not equal to the NBB Gram matrix**, and because $G$ is symmetric strictly diagonally dominant, subcubic solvers for it are already known and must never be misrepresented as progress toward the Riemann Hypothesis. [@rem-ch34-nbb-gram-spectral-and-pruning], [@rem-ch34-nbb-subcubic-spectral-solver], and [@rem-ch34-nbb-preconditioning-and-hmatrix-obstruction] now **resolve for genuine $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N$ positive-definite subcubic and quasilinear solving, common-absolute-threshold high-frequency tail-rank attribution, and separated-block $\mathcal{H}$-matrix compression obstruction**: on the spatial side, on the standard strongly $\mathcal{H}$-admissible separated block $I \times J = \{1, \dots, \lfloor N/4\rfloor\} \times \{\lfloor 3N/4\rfloor + 1, \dots, N\}$, Weyl tail-rank attribution and the multiplication-by-four weighted-difference operator $X = \widetilde{D}_1 B_{\mathrm{mult}} \widetilde{D}_1^\top$ (via the elementary sawtooth cusp lower bound $d_k \ge \frac{1}{2}\ln k - (\ln 2 + \frac{413}{72})$ and exact Plancherel-isometry upper bound $\lVert X\rVert_2 \ll_\delta N^{2\mu + \delta}$) prove at solver-scale tolerances $\tau \le \frac{\ln N}{8N}$ (for $A_N$) and $\tau_G \le \frac{\ln N}{4N^2}$ (for $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N$) that $\operatorname{rank}_\tau(A_N|_{I \times J}),\; \operatorname{rank}_{\tau_G}(G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N|_{I \times J}) \gg_\delta N^{1 - 2\mu - \delta}$ ($\widetilde{\Omega}(N^{1/2})$ from convexity, $\widetilde{\Omega}(N^{2/3})$ from explicit Weyl, $\Omega(N^{55/82}\ln N)$ from explicit sub-Weyl, $\Omega_\delta(N^{29/42 - \delta})$ from Bourgain, and near-linear $N^{1 - o(1)}$ under external RH/Lindelöf), completely ruling out uniform polylogarithmic-rank approximation of the standard strongly $\mathcal{H}$-admissible separated block (for $A_N$ or $G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N$) at solver-scale absolute operator-norm tolerance (blocking any $\mathcal{H}$-matrix / multilevel Schur scheme compressing such blocks to polylogarithmic rank); conversely, pairing the $O(N)$ explicit tridiagonal Laplace preconditioner $K_N = K^{\mathrm{Lap}}(\frac{1}{2})$ with end-to-end finite-precision Chebyshev semi-iteration on the strictly positive-definite Mellin–Plancherel surrogate $S_{\mathbb{R}} \succ 0$ unconditionally breaks the cubic wall via the Euler product and Hardy $H^2$ monotonicity under batched Odlyzko–Schönhage precomputation already at elementary convexity $\mu = \frac{1}{4}$ with total time $O_\delta(N^{11/4+\delta}\tau^{-2-\delta})$ ($O_\delta(N^{13/6+\delta}\tau^{-3/2-\delta})$ via explicit Weyl, $O_\delta(N^{19443/9020+\delta}\tau^{-82/55-\delta})$ via explicit sub-Weyl, and $O_\delta(N^{5123/2436+\delta}\tau^{-42/29-\delta})$ via Bourgain (assuming supplied computable outward-rounded spectral endpoints realizing its condition-number rate); at fixed tolerance under per-point Riemann–Siegel, $\widetilde{O}(N^{9/4})$, $\widetilde{O}(N^{123/55})$, and $O_\delta(N^{63/29+\delta})$ respectively, multiplying by $\tau^{-3\nu/2}$ at variable tolerance), while invoking the externally proved [@conj-riemann-hypothesis] (`riemannZeta_eq_zero_re : RiemannZetaZerosOnLine`) via Borel–Carathéodory and Lindelöf collapses the effective condition number to $\kappa_2(K_N^{-1/2} A_N K_N^{-1/2}) = \exp(O(\ln N/\sqrt{\ln\ln(N + 16)})) = N^{o(1)}$ and, assuming supplied computable outward-rounded spectral endpoints realizing this $N^{o(1)}$ rate, lowers total arithmetic time to **quasilinear $O_\delta((N/\tau)^{1+\delta})$** ($O_\delta(N^{1+\delta})$ at fixed tolerance, with near-linear storage $O_\delta((N/\tau)^{1+\delta})$); in addition, the complementary exact $O(N\ln N)$ spatial-truncation preconditioners $M_N$ and $M_N^+$ unconditionally lock $\lambda_{\min} > 1$ (immune to the $K_N$ critical-line zero dip of order $O((\ln N)^{-2})$) and at $N = 512$ compress $\kappa_2(G^{\mathrm{NB}}_{512})$ $77{,}602$-fold down to $13.20$ ($32$ PCG steps). The remaining open algorithmic and analytic problem therefore narrows to two sharper targets: first, without routing through the RH-depth Mertens comparison chain $(\widetilde{K}_N, M_N)$, establish directly for the $O(N\ln N)$ spatial-truncation preconditioner an analytic upper bound $\lambda_{\max}(M_N^{-1/2} G^{\mathrm{NB}}_N M_N^{-1/2}) = N^{o(1)}$ (or polylogarithmic in $N$)—or construct an explicit fast preconditioner tightening the effective condition number further from $N^{o(1)}$ to $O((\ln N)^C)$ while pushing unconditional total time below quasi-quadratic—and formalize the genuine NBB spectral and preconditioning bounds directly inside `trureturing`; second, even though combining the externally verified [@conj-riemann-hypothesis] (`riemannZeta_eq_zero_re : RiemannZetaZerosOnLine`) with L. Báez-Duarte's integer-dilation closure criterion (*A strengthening of the Nyman-Beurling criterion for the Riemann hypothesis*, *Rend. Mat. Acc. Lincei*, ser. 9, **14**(1), 5–11, 2003, Theorem 1, p. 5; preprint [arXiv:math/0202141](https://arxiv.org/abs/math/0202141), Theorem 1.1; see [@rem-ch34-arithmetic-pivot-literature]) now establishes $d_N \to 0$, **independently deducing from finite-$N$ computable certificates that $d_N \to 0$ without circularly invoking that external proof** remains a separate open step.
:::

The full interactive laboratory below brings together natural-order fill-in, leaf-first elimination, thresholded Schur-complement pruning, self-looped random-walk repeated squaring on the divisibility network, and the 3D necessary budget lower-bound surface for resolving signed orbits with compactly supported even functions on a single stage:

::: {.viz src="arithmetic-schur-lab" height="640"}
**3D interactive laboratory for divisibility-network Schur elimination, repeated squaring, and signed-orbit support budgets (`arithmetic-schur-lab`).** Across the divisibility-network views, the 3D stage renders cyan original divisibility edges and magenta fill-in or multi-step coupling edges along purple axes; in the signed-orbit budget view (`budget`), it renders the purple necessary budget lower-bound surface $B_{\min} = (1 - \varepsilon) / \bigl(\min(1, R|t|)\sinh(R|\eta|)\bigr)$, the magenta excluded region beneath it, and an amber dot at the current parameter point, while in network views the amber marker highlights the active pivot or squaring depth. Controls adjust $N$, elimination order, pruning threshold, and squaring depth to inspect fill-in counts and back-substitution residuals, or adjust $t$ and relative error $\varepsilon$ in `budget` view to read out the necessary budget lower bound $B_{\min}$; entering the magenta region violates the necessary bound, whereas lying outside it is necessary rather than sufficient for an interpolating function to exist.
:::
